`Apps/Hardening.xcconfig`:

```xcconfig
// Release hardening for apps that embed KaiselKit.
// Referenced from project.yml (configFiles: Release). Copy into your own app.

// Strip all symbols from the shipped binary (keeps the dSYM for crash symbolication).
DEPLOYMENT_POSTPROCESSING = YES
STRIP_INSTALLED_PRODUCT = YES
STRIP_STYLE = all
COPY_PHASE_STRIP = YES
DEBUG_INFORMATION_FORMAT = dwarf-with-dsym
DEAD_CODE_STRIPPING = YES
STRIP_SWIFT_SYMBOLS = YES

// Hide C symbols by default, so rasp_* entry points are not in the export table.
GCC_SYMBOLS_PRIVATE_EXTERN = YES
GCC_INLINES_ARE_PRIVATE_EXTERN = YES

// Optimise: inlines the raw-syscall helpers and removes assertions.
GCC_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL = s
SWIFT_OPTIMIZATION_LEVEL = -O
SWIFT_COMPILATION_MODE = wholemodule
LLVM_LTO = YES

// Optional, not enabled here (see README "Build hardening"):
//   SWIFT_REFLECTION_METADATA_LEVEL = none   hides Swift type/case names, but
//       breaks String(describing:) of enums and some SwiftUI/Mirror use.
//   CODE_SIGN_INJECT_BASE_ENTITLEMENTS = NO  drops get-task-allow, but a
//       development-exported IPA can then no longer be debugged.

ENABLE_TESTABILITY = NO
VALIDATE_PRODUCT = YES

```

`Apps/KaiselIntegrityCheck/ContentView.swift`:

```swift
import SwiftUI
import KaiselKit

struct ContentView: View {
    @StateObject private var model = IntegrityModel()

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            List {
                if let r = model.report {
                    Section("Device") {
                        verdictRow("Device integrity", r.device)
                        infoRow("Jailbreak", r.jailbreakEvidence.isEmpty
                                ? "not detected" : "jailbroken (\(r.jailbreakEvidence.count) conclusive)")
                        verdictRow("This app's runtime", r.runtime)
                        if let a = r.app { verdictRow("App build", a) }
                        else { infoRow("App build", "not enforced (informational)") }
                        infoRow("Last verdict (protected)", model.kit.lastVerdict.read()?.rawValue ?? "-")
                        infoRow("Inline guard", IntegrityGuard.isClean ? "clean" : "flags \(IntegrityGuard.flags.rawValue)")
                        infoRow("Rules", r.rulesVersion.map { "v\($0)" } ?? "built-in only")
                    }
                    Section("Phone") {
                        infoRow("Model", r.posture.model)
                        infoRow("iOS", r.posture.osVersion)
                        infoRow("Developer Mode", r.posture.developerMode.rawValue)
                        infoRow("Developer Disk Image", r.posture.developerDiskImageMounted ? "mounted" : "not mounted")
                        infoRow("Passcode set", r.posture.passcodeSet.rawValue)
                        infoRow("Biometry", r.posture.biometry)
                        infoRow("Secure Enclave", r.posture.secureEnclaveAvailable ? "available" : "unavailable")
                        infoRow("App Attest (device)", r.posture.appAttestSupported ? "supported" : "unsupported")
                        infoRow("App Attest (this build)", appAttestLabel)
                    }
                    Section("This app") {
                        infoRow("Signed as", r.build.detectedDistribution)
                        infoRow("Expected", r.build.expectedDistribution ?? "not set")
                        infoRow("FairPlay cryptid", "\(r.build.cryptID)")
                    }
                }
                Section {
                    if model.running { ProgressView() }
                    Button("Evaluate now") { Task { await model.run() } }
                    Button(model.monitorOn ? "Stop monitor" : "Start monitor (20 s ± 5 s)") { model.toggleMonitor() }
                    Button("Seal report (App Attest / Secure Enclave)") { Task { await model.seal() } }
                        .disabled(model.report == nil)
                    if let s = model.sealedSummary { Text(s).font(.caption.monospaced()) }
                    Button("Copy result JSON (lab log)") { model.copyReportJSON() }
                        .disabled(model.report == nil)
                    if JailbreakHistory.firstSighting != nil {
                        Button("Reset jailbreak history") {
                            JailbreakHistory.reset()
                            Task { await model.run() }
                        }
                        .foregroundColor(.red)
                    }
                }
                Section("Reference server") {
                    TextField("http://<mac-ip>:8080", text: $model.serverURL)
                        .keyboardType(.URL)
                        .autocapitalization(.none)
                        .disableAutocorrection(true)
                    Button("1. Enrol App Attest") { Task { await model.enrol() } }
                        .disabled(!IntegrityModel.appAttestBuild)
                    Button("2. Verify with server") { Task { await model.verifyWithServer() } }
                    Button("Update signed rules") { Task { await model.updateRules() } }
                    if let s = model.serverResult { Text(s).font(.caption.monospaced()) }
                }
                if let r = model.report {
                    Section("Scored signals") {
                        if r.scoredSignals.isEmpty { Text("None").foregroundColor(.secondary) }
                        ForEach(Array(r.scoredSignals.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, s in signalRow(s) }
                    }
                    Section("Informational") {
                        if r.informationalSignals.isEmpty { Text("None").foregroundColor(.secondary) }
                        ForEach(Array(r.informationalSignals.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, s in signalRow(s) }
                    }
                    Section("Observability on this OS") {
                        ForEach(r.observabilityNotes, id: \.self) { Text($0).font(.caption) }
                    }
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Kaisel Integrity Check")
        }
        .task { await model.run() }
    }

    private var appAttestLabel: String {
        guard IntegrityModel.appAttestBuild else { return "not in this build" }
        return model.kit.appAttestStatus.rawValue
    }

    private func verdictRow(_ label: String, _ a: DomainAssessment) -> some View {
        HStack {
            Text(label)
            Spacer()
            Text(a.verdict.rawValue.capitalized).bold().foregroundColor(colour(a.verdict))
            Text("\(a.score)").monospacedDigit().foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
    }

    private func infoRow(_ label: String, _ value: String) -> some View {
        HStack { Text(label); Spacer(); Text(value).foregroundColor(.secondary) }
    }

    private func signalRow(_ s: Signal) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
            HStack {
                if s.hard && !s.informational { Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.octagon.fill").foregroundColor(.red) }
                Text(s.title).font(.subheadline.bold())
                Spacer()
                Text("\(s.domain.rawValue) · \(s.code.rawValue)").font(.caption).foregroundColor(.secondary)
            }
            Text(s.detail).font(.caption.monospaced()).foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
    }

    private func colour(_ v: Verdict) -> Color {
        switch v {
        case .trusted: return .green
        case .low: return .mint
        case .elevated: return .orange
        case .high, .compromised: return .red
        }
    }
}

```

`Apps/KaiselIntegrityCheck/KaiselIntegrityCheckApp.swift`:

```swift
import SwiftUI
import KaiselKit

@main
struct KaiselIntegrityCheckApp: App {
    var body: some Scene {
        WindowGroup { ContentView() }
    }
}

@MainActor
final class IntegrityModel: ObservableObject {
    @Published var report: IntegrityReport?
    @Published var running = false
    @Published var monitorOn = false
    @Published var sealedSummary: String?
    @Published var serverURL: String = "http://192.168.1.10:8080"
    @Published var serverResult: String?

    /// True only in the KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest scheme, which carries the App Attest
    /// entitlement (paid developer team). The KaiselIntegrityCheck scheme builds with a
    /// free Personal Team and seals with the Secure Enclave key only.
    nonisolated static let appAttestBuild: Bool = {
        #if APP_ATTEST
        return true
        #else
        return false
        #endif
    }()

    /// Paste the contents of Server/keys/rules_public.pem here to enable rules updates.
    nonisolated static let rulesPublicKeyPEM: String = ""

    // No expectedDistribution: build/signing signals are informational, so an
    // Xcode or ad hoc install does not affect the device verdict. In a
    // production app set expectedDistribution: .appStore and your team ID.
    let kit: IntegrityKit = IntegrityModel.makeKit()

    nonisolated private static func makeKit() -> IntegrityKit {
        #if DEBUG
        let cfg = IntegrityConfiguration(allowedExceptionPorts: 1, debuggerExpected: true)
        #else
        let cfg = IntegrityConfiguration(allowedExceptionPorts: 1)
        #endif
        // Previously downloaded, verified rules (nil if none or invalid).
        var rules: DetectionRules? = nil
        if !rulesPublicKeyPEM.isEmpty, let store = try? RulesStore(publicKeyPEM: rulesPublicKeyPEM) {
            rules = store.current
        }
        return IntegrityKit(configuration: cfg, rules: rules)
    }
    private var monitor: IntegrityMonitor?

    func run() async {
        running = true
        report = await kit.evaluate()
        running = false
    }

    func toggleMonitor() {
        if let m = monitor { m.stop(); monitor = nil; monitorOn = false; return }
        let m = IntegrityMonitor(kit: kit, interval: 20, jitter: 5) { [weak self] r in
            Task { @MainActor in self?.report = r }
        }
        m.start()
        monitor = m
        monitorOn = true
    }

    func seal() async {
        guard let r = report else { return }
        // In production the nonce comes from your server.
        var nonce = Data(count: 32)
        _ = nonce.withUnsafeMutableBytes { SecRandomCopyBytes(kSecRandomDefault, 32, $0.baseAddress!) }
        do {
            let sealed = try await kit.seal(r, serverNonce: nonce)
            let hashHex: String = sealed.clientDataHash.prefix(8).map { String(format: "%02x", $0) }.joined()
            let attest: String = sealed.appAttestAssertion == nil ? "none" : "assertion"
            let se: String = sealed.secureEnclaveSignature == nil ? "none" : "signed"
            let err: String = sealed.attestationError ?? ""
            var lines: [String] = []
            lines.append("payload \(sealed.payload.count) B, hash \(hashHex)...")
            lines.append("App Attest: \(attest)")
            lines.append("Secure Enclave: \(se)")
            lines.append(err)
            sealedSummary = lines.joined(separator: "\n")
        } catch {
            sealedSummary = "seal failed: \(error)"
        }
    }
}

```

`Apps/KaiselIntegrityCheck/ServerClient.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit
import KaiselKit

/// Talks to Server/server.py. Demo-quality: no auth, plain HTTP on the LAN.
@MainActor
extension IntegrityModel {
    private func post(_ path: String, _ body: [String: Any]) async throws -> [String: Any] {
        guard let url = URL(string: serverURL + path) else { throw URLError(.badURL) }
        var req = URLRequest(url: url)
        req.httpMethod = "POST"
        req.setValue("application/json", forHTTPHeaderField: "Content-Type")
        req.httpBody = try JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: body)
        let (data, _) = try await URLSession.shared.data(for: req)
        let obj = try JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: data)
        return obj as? [String: Any] ?? [:]
    }

    private func challenge() async throws -> (id: String, bytes: Data) {
        let r = try await post("/v1/challenge", [:])
        guard let id = r["challengeId"] as? String, let b64 = r["challenge"] as? String,
              let bytes = Data(base64Encoded: b64) else { throw URLError(.cannotParseResponse) }
        return (id, bytes)
    }

    private static func pretty(_ obj: [String: Any]) -> String {
        guard let d = try? JSONSerialization.data(withJSONObject: obj, options: [.prettyPrinted, .sortedKeys]) else {
            return "\(obj)"
        }
        return String(decoding: d, as: UTF8.self)
    }

    /// One-time App Attest enrolment with the server.
    func enrol() async {
        guard IntegrityModel.appAttestBuild else {
            serverResult = "App Attest: not enrolled (this build has no App Attest entitlement).\n"
                + "Build the KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest scheme with a paid developer team to enable it.\n"
                + "Verify still works: the report is sealed with the Secure Enclave key only."
            return
        }
        do {
            let ch = try await challenge()
            let e = try await kit.attestKey(serverChallenge: ch.bytes)
            let body: [String: Any] = ["challengeId": ch.id, "keyId": e.keyId,
                                       "attestationObject": e.attestationObject.base64EncodedString()]
            serverResult = Self.pretty(try await post("/v1/attest", body))
        } catch IntegrityError.appAttestUnsupported {
            serverResult = "App Attest: unsupported on this device (simulator, Mac or virtual device)"
        } catch IntegrityError.appAttestUnavailable(let why) {
            serverResult = "App Attest: not enrolled (\(why))"
        } catch {
            serverResult = "enrol failed: \(error)"
        }
    }

    /// Evaluate, seal with the server's challenge, and ask the server to decide.
    func verifyWithServer() async {
        do {
            let ch = try await challenge()
            let r = await kit.evaluate()
            report = r
            let sealed = try await kit.seal(r, serverNonce: ch.bytes)
            let sealedData = try JSONEncoder().encode(sealed)
            let sealedObj = try JSONSerialization.jsonObject(with: sealedData)
            let body: [String: Any] = ["challengeId": ch.id, "sealed": sealedObj]
            serverResult = Self.pretty(try await post("/v1/verify", body))
        } catch {
            serverResult = "verify failed: \(error)"
        }
    }

    /// Downloads signed rules. Requires `rulesPublicKeyPEM` to be set.
    func updateRules() async {
        guard !IntegrityModel.rulesPublicKeyPEM.isEmpty else {
            serverResult = "Set IntegrityModel.rulesPublicKeyPEM to your rules_public.pem first."
            return
        }
        do {
            let store = try RulesStore(publicKeyPEM: IntegrityModel.rulesPublicKeyPEM)
            guard let rURL = URL(string: serverURL + "/v1/rules.json"),
                  let sURL = URL(string: serverURL + "/v1/rules.sig") else { throw URLError(.badURL) }
            let rules = try await store.update(rulesURL: rURL, signatureURL: sURL)
            serverResult = "rules v\(rules.version) installed (applies on next launch)"
        } catch {
            serverResult = "rules update failed: \(error)"
        }
    }

    func copyReportJSON() {
        guard let r = report, let d = try? r.canonicalJSON() else { return }
        UIPasteboard.general.string = String(decoding: d, as: UTF8.self)
        serverResult = "Report JSON copied (\(d.count) bytes)"
    }
}

`Apps/KaiselJBCheck/KaiselJBCheckApp.swift`:

```swift
import SwiftUI
import UIKit
import KaiselKit

/// Jailbreak-only checker. Says nothing about how this app was built or signed.
@main
struct KaiselJBCheckApp: App {
    var body: some Scene { WindowGroup { JailbreakView() } }
}

@MainActor
final class JailbreakModel: ObservableObject {
    @Published var result: JailbreakResult?
    @Published var running = false
    @Published var copied = false
    private let detector = JailbreakDetector()

    func copyJSON() {
        guard let r = result else { return }
        let enc = JSONEncoder()
        enc.outputFormatting = [.prettyPrinted, .sortedKeys]
        enc.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        guard let d = try? enc.encode(r) else { return }
        UIPasteboard.general.string = String(decoding: d, as: UTF8.self)
        copied = true
    }

    func run() async {
        running = true
        result = await detector.check()
        running = false
    }

    func resetHistory() async {
        JailbreakHistory.reset()
        await run()
    }
}

struct JailbreakView: View {
    @StateObject private var model = JailbreakModel()

    var body: some View {
        NavigationView {
            List {
                Section {
                    VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 8) {
                        Image(systemName: icon).font(.system(size: 44)).foregroundColor(colour)
                        Text(title).font(.largeTitle.bold()).foregroundColor(colour)
                        if let r = model.result {
                            Text("\(r.model) · iOS \(r.osVersion) · \(String(format: "%.0f", r.durationMs)) ms")
                                .font(.footnote).foregroundColor(.secondary)
                        }
                    }
                    .padding(.vertical, 8)
                    Button(model.running ? "Checking…" : "Check again") { Task { await model.run() } }
                        .disabled(model.running)
                    Button(model.copied ? "Copied" : "Copy result JSON (lab log)") { model.copyJSON() }
                        .disabled(model.result == nil)
                    if JailbreakHistory.firstSighting != nil {
                        Button("Reset jailbreak history") { Task { await model.resetHistory() } }
                            .foregroundColor(.red)
                    }
                }

                if let r = model.result {
                    Section("Conclusive evidence") {
                        if r.conclusive.isEmpty { Text("None").foregroundColor(.secondary) }
                        ForEach(Array(r.conclusive.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, s in row(s) }
                    }
                    Section("Indicators") {
                        if r.indicators.isEmpty { Text("None").foregroundColor(.secondary) }
                        ForEach(Array(r.indicators.enumerated()), id: \.offset) { _, s in row(s) }
                    }
                    Section("Checks run") {
                        Text(r.checksRun.joined(separator: ", ")).font(.caption.monospaced())
                    }
                }
            }
            .navigationTitle("Kaisel JB Check")
        }
        .task { await model.run() }
    }

    private func row(_ s: Signal) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: 2) {
            Text(s.title).font(.subheadline.bold())
            Text(s.detail).font(.caption.monospaced()).foregroundColor(.secondary)
        }
    }

    private var title: String {
        switch model.result?.status {
        case .jailbroken?: return "Jailbroken"
        case .suspicious?: return "Suspicious"
        case .notJailbroken?: return "Not jailbroken"
        case nil: return "Checking…"
        }
    }
    private var icon: String {
        switch model.result?.status {
        case .jailbroken?: return "xmark.shield.fill"
        case .suspicious?: return "exclamationmark.shield.fill"
        case .notJailbroken?: return "checkmark.shield.fill"
        case nil: return "shield"
        }
    }
    private var colour: Color {
        switch model.result?.status {
        case .jailbroken?: return .red
        case .suspicious?: return .orange
        case .notJailbroken?: return .green
        case nil: return .secondary
        }
    }
}

```

`Apps/project.yml`:

```yml
# XcodeGen spec for the Kaisel apps.
#   brew install xcodegen && cd Apps && xcodegen generate && open Kaisel.xcodeproj
#
# Schemes
#   KaiselJBCheck               Kaisel JB Check: jailbreak-only        (free Apple ID works)
#   KaiselIntegrityCheck        Kaisel Integrity Check: full kit        (free Apple ID works)
#   KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest  Integrity Check + App Attest            (paid developer team only)
#
# Bundle IDs derive from KAISEL_BUNDLE_PREFIX. Change it (or pass
# KAISEL_BUNDLE_PREFIX=... to xcodebuild) if you fork the project, because
# bundle IDs are unique per Apple developer account.
name: Kaisel
options:
  bundleIdPrefix: com.dhanesh.kaisel
  deploymentTarget:
    iOS: "15.0"
  createIntermediateGroups: true
settings:
  base:
    KAISEL_BUNDLE_PREFIX: com.dhanesh.kaisel
    MARKETING_VERSION: "1.4.1"
    CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION: "2"
    SWIFT_VERSION: "5.9"
    TARGETED_DEVICE_FAMILY: "1,2"
    CODE_SIGN_STYLE: Automatic
    ASSETCATALOG_COMPILER_APPICON_NAME: AppIcon
packages:
  KaiselKit:
    path: ..

targetTemplates:
  KaiselApp:
    type: application
    platform: iOS
    configFiles:
      Release: Hardening.xcconfig
    dependencies:
      - package: KaiselKit
    scheme: {}

  IntegrityInfo:
    info:
      properties:
        CFBundleDevelopmentRegion: en-GB
        LSRequiresIPhoneOS: true
        CFBundleShortVersionString: $(MARKETING_VERSION)
        CFBundleVersion: $(CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION)
        UILaunchScreen: {}
        NSLocalNetworkUsageDescription: Connects to the Kaisel reference server on your computer.
        NSAppTransportSecurity:
          NSAllowsLocalNetworking: true
          NSAllowsArbitraryLoads: true      # lab use: plain HTTP to a LAN IP. Remove in production apps.
        LSApplicationQueriesSchemes: [cydia, sileo, zbra, filza, undecimus, activator]

targets:
  KaiselJBCheck:
    templates: [KaiselApp]
    sources: [KaiselJBCheck]
    info:
      path: KaiselJBCheck/Info.plist
      properties:
        CFBundleDisplayName: Kaisel JB Check
        CFBundleDevelopmentRegion: en-GB
        LSRequiresIPhoneOS: true
        CFBundleShortVersionString: $(MARKETING_VERSION)
        CFBundleVersion: $(CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION)
        UILaunchScreen: {}
        LSApplicationQueriesSchemes: [cydia, sileo, zbra, filza, undecimus, activator]
    settings:
      base:
        PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER: $(KAISEL_BUNDLE_PREFIX).jbcheck

  KaiselIntegrityCheck:
    templates: [KaiselApp, IntegrityInfo]
    sources: [KaiselIntegrityCheck]
    info:
      path: KaiselIntegrityCheck/Info.plist
      properties:
        CFBundleDisplayName: Kaisel Integrity Check
    # No entitlements, so a free Apple ID (Personal Team) can sign it.
    settings:
      base:
        PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER: $(KAISEL_BUNDLE_PREFIX).integritycheck

  KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest:
    templates: [KaiselApp, IntegrityInfo]
    sources:
      - path: KaiselIntegrityCheck
        excludes: [Info.plist]
    info:
      path: KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest/Info.plist
      properties:
        CFBundleDisplayName: Kaisel Integrity Attest
    # Same sources plus the App Attest entitlement. Personal Teams cannot provision it.
    entitlements:
      path: KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest/KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest.entitlements
      properties:
        com.apple.developer.devicecheck.appattest-environment: development
    settings:
      base:
        PRODUCT_BUNDLE_IDENTIFIER: $(KAISEL_BUNDLE_PREFIX).integritycheck.attest
        SWIFT_ACTIVE_COMPILATION_CONDITIONS: "$(inherited) APP_ATTEST"

```

`CHANGELOG.md`:

```md
# Changelog

All notable changes are documented here. The format follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/)
and the project uses [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/).

## [1.4.1] - 2026-11-21

### Fixed
- Release packaging: the app version (`MARKETING_VERSION` in `Apps/project.yml`) is now 1.4.1. The v1.4.0
  release IPAs were built with the stale 1.3.1 version in their file names and `CFBundleShortVersionString`.
  No code changes; the SDK version reported to the server stays 1.4.0.

## [1.4.0] - 2026-11-21

Reduces image false positives, makes the reference server fail closed, and adds reproducible lab evidence
tooling. Not yet run on a device; do not tag until the open release gates pass.

### Fixed
- **Broad image markers could prove a "jailbreak".** Up to 1.3.1 the C core matched substrings such as
  `crane`, `liberty` and `choicy` against the whole image path, and Swift and the server treated every such
  match that did not mention Frida as a conclusive tweak injector. A framework named `CraneBank`,
  `LibertyFinance` or `ChoicyAnalytics` could therefore make a stock phone "Jailbroken". Found by code
  review; never observed on a device.
- **Bundle prefix without a directory boundary.** `/var/App.app2/...` counted as inside `/var/App.app`.
- **Late-loaded images were conclusive on confidence alone** (`imageAddedAtRuntime` at 95 without "frida").
- **Signed remote-rules image markers could create a conclusive verdict.** They are now indicators only.
- **Server accepted reports without checking completion.** A report with no signals and incomplete checks
  could be allowed.
- **Server history trusted unbound Secure Enclave keys.** A self-generated key could create or clear
  device history.

### Added
- Explicit image classes in the C core (`rasp_classify.c`, `rasp_image_class`): jailbreak injector,
  instrumentation, trusted, marker, unknown external, unknown. Swift wrapper `ImageEvidence` /
  `ImageEvidenceClass`.
- Finding codes `instrumentationImage` (205, runtime, hard, weight 0.90) and `imageMarkerMatch`
  (206, runtime, indicator, weight 0.50).
- `Server/classification.json`: one manifest for the conclusive, indicator and corroborating code sets and
  the image lists. `Scripts/gen_strings.py` compiles the lists into the C core; the server loads it directly.
- `Server/classification.py`, the server's copy of the classifier.
- Server strict and lab modes (`--mode`), `policy.validate_report()`, server-side SDK profiles with the
  expected check set, and `validation` / `mode` in every `/v1/verify` response.
- `IntegrityReport.reportSchemaVersion` (2) and `IntegrityReport.currentSchemaVersion`.
- `JailbreakResult.checksIncomplete` and `JailbreakResult.sdkVersion` for lab records.
- `JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(code:detail:)`.
- Tests: shared fixtures `Tests/Fixtures/image_classification.json` (34 cases) run by the C core
  (`Tests/CClassifier`), the server (`test_classification.py`) and Swift (`ImageClassificationTests`);
  `test_validation.py` (32 strict-mode and sealed-flow tests); `test_parity.py` (Swift/manifest/server
  parity, weights, string table); `Lab/tests` (11 tests).
- `Scripts/gen_strings.py --verify` (seed-independent check of the committed table) and reproducible
  output with `RASP_SEED`.
- Lab: run metadata (`.meta.json`) and its validation, PARTIAL / INVALID / NOT TESTED outcomes and
  `--coverage` in `compare_results.py`; `Lab/templates/`; `Lab/SCENARIOS.md` (P2 measurement plans);
  `Lab/COVERAGE.md`; scenarios `stock_ios26`, `dopamine_roothide_hidden_fresh`,
  `dopamine_roothide_hidden_seen`, `frida_spawn`, `frida_renamed`, `frida_late_attach`,
  `shadow_class_hiding`.
- CI: C classifier job, Lab tests, string-table verification and seeded reproducibility.
- `docs/V1.4.0-VALIDATION.md`, `IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT.md`.

### Changed (classification, with reasons)
- `suspiciousImage` (200) now means *known jailbreak injector* only: an exact injector basename outside the
  app bundle, or an image under `/var/jb/`, `MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/`, `/usr/lib/TweakInject/` or a
  `.jbroot-*` component. It is in the conclusive set and is re-validated from its path wherever it is read.
  Title changed to "Known jailbreak injection library loaded".
- FridaGadget, frida-agent and Cycript images are `instrumentationImage` (205): runtime risk, never a
  jailbreak (before: 200, not conclusive because of the Frida exception).
- An injector basename **inside** the app bundle (for example Substrate injected into a re-signed IPA) is 205,
  not a jailbreak: such apps run on stock phones.
- Broad markers apply only to the file name of images outside the system and the bundle, and produce 206.
- `/usr/lib/libsubstrate.dylib` (rootful) is now conclusive by exact identity. 1.3.1 missed it because no
  marker matched; the same file is already a conclusive jailbreak path (100).
- Late-loaded images are reported with their class (200/205/206/201) plus `imageAddedAtRuntime` (203),
  which is an indicator only.
- `JailbreakEvidence.isTweakInjector(_:)` now means "classifies as a known jailbreak injector".
- Keychain markers and server history entries are re-validated with the new rule; 1.3.x entries from a
  broad-marker match are ignored.
- Server: strict mode is the default and always requires App Attest; `--no-require-app-attest` now
  selects lab mode. Tampered payloads, wrong challenges and malformed payloads return a structured `deny`
  instead of raising. History is keyed by the App Attest key (`aa:`); legacy `se:` entries are read only
  when the Secure Enclave key is bound to the verified App Attest instance.
- `IntegrityKit.sdkVersion` is "1.4.0".
- Docs: README, DETECTION, SERVER, LIMITATIONS, LAB, LAB_CHECKLIST and expected.json distinguish measured
  results from expectations, state that "Not jailbroken" is not proof, and list untested combinations.

### Compatibility
- Server: 1.3.x reports (no `reportSchemaVersion`) are rejected in strict mode and accepted as `step_up`
  in lab mode. Deploy the 1.4.0 SDK before switching a production server to strict mode.
- Integrators who matched on `suspiciousImage` for Frida must also handle `instrumentationImage` (205).
- `IntegrityReport` gained a stored property; code that builds reports by hand must pass it.

## Documentation and lab updates after 1.3.1 (2026-11-21, folded into 1.4.0)

### Added
- `docs/Pentesting iOS RASP.md`: adversary-centred assessment methodology in five layers (hide, hook/patch,
  tamper, kernel, server), tiered test plan, results and reporting templates, MASVS/MASTG/ATT&CK/CBUAE/PCI DSS
  mapping, and a gap analysis of Kaisel with proposed improvements.

### Changed
- README: version 1.3.1, lab results so far, roadmap from the gap analysis, and clearer limitations
  (per-app hiding, dyld indicator). Lab results, limitations and roadmap now state the hidden RootHide case
  as a miss, matching the screenshots.

### Lab
- Dopamine + RootHide with the app hidden (iPhone9,3, iOS 15.8.2): an earlier 1.3.1 run read **Suspicious**,
  but the latest runs read clean in both apps (Not jailbroken, Trusted 0). The hidden case is a miss.
  Result JSON not yet exported.
- Screenshots of the RootHide hidden and unhidden runs and the stock iPhone18,1 (iOS 27.2.1) run added
  under `docs/images/lab/` and embedded in the README.
- TrollStore installed with TrollInstallerX: detected by both apps.
- Stock iOS 26 and iOS 27.2.1: clean on 1.3.1.

## [1.3.1] - 2026-11-21

### Fixed
- **False "Jailbroken" on a stock iPhone18,1 / iOS 27.2.1.** The 1.3.0 dyld check compared the dyld running
  from the shared cache (iOS 16+) with `/usr/lib/dyld` and found 162,671 differing words. The check now skips
  in-cache dyld, ignores large layout differences, and `dyldModified` is an indicator, not conclusive.
- Keychain jailbreak markers and server history entries created by that false positive are discarded.
- Keychain markers are written only for conclusive evidence with confidence 90 or more.

### Added
- `JailbreakHistory.reset()` and a **Reset jailbreak history** button in both apps; `--clear-history` for the server.
- Stock iOS 27 regression tests (Swift and server).

## [1.3.0] - 2026-11-21

First jailbroken-device results: Dopamine + RootHide on iPhone9,3, iOS 15.8.2.

### Added
- `JailbreakEvidence`: one rule for "conclusive" evidence, shared by JB Check, the full kit and the server.
- `dyldModified` (110): dyld `__text` compared with `/usr/lib/dyld` on disk, and dyld's load path checked (made an indicator in 1.3.1).
- `userspaceReboot` (111): `kern.userspacereboottime` indicator (Dopamine restarts userspace).
- `previouslyJailbroken` (908): Keychain memory of an earlier conclusive result.
- Reference server: per-key jailbreak history and `device.jailbreakEvidence`.
- `IntegrityReport.jailbreakEvidence` and a "Jailbreak" row in Kaisel Integrity Check.
- Lab scenarios `dopamine_roothide` and `dopamine_roothide_hidden`, with the first recorded run.

### Changed
- The device verdict is `compromised` whenever conclusive jailbreak evidence exists in any domain
  (a RootHide-injected device showed "Elevated 56" before).
- `syscallStubAltered` follows a same-image branch before firing, records the entry bytes, and uses confidence 60.

### Known gap
- With the app in RootHide's Blacklist, 1.2 reported a clean device. See docs/LIMITATIONS.md.

## [1.2.0] - 2026-11-21

### Added
- Rebranded as **Kaisel**: Swift package `KaiselKit`, apps **Kaisel JB Check** and **Kaisel Integrity Check**, new app icons.
- `KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest` scheme: App Attest is now opt-in, so both main apps build with a free Apple ID.
- `IntegrityKit.appAttestStatus` and `IntegrityError.appAttestUnavailable`; App Attest failures no longer surface as raw errors.
- Unsigned IPA builds (`Scripts/build_unsigned_ipa.sh`) and a release workflow for Impactor and Sideloadly users.
- Runtime self-integrity (`__text` hash, prologue snapshots), inline guard, tamper-evident verdict.
- Signed remote rules with anti-rollback and bounded tuning.
- Python reference server with App Attest verification and policy re-scoring.
- Lab harness: checklist, expected results and `compare_results.py`.
- GitHub CI, issue and pull request templates, contributing and security guides.

### Changed
- Results are split into device, runtime and app domains; build and posture signals are informational by default.

### Fixed
- A stock iOS 27 device was reported as compromised (build-context signals, the `apple-magnifier` URL scheme and objc trampoline memory).
- Swift type-checker timeouts in `IntegrityKit.swift` and related files.

## [1.0.0]

### Added
- First version: C core with raw-syscall checks and the Swift scoring layer.

[Unreleased]: https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel/compare/v1.4.1...HEAD
[1.4.1]: https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel/compare/v1.4.0...v1.4.1
[1.4.0]: https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel/compare/v1.3.1...v1.4.0

`CONTRIBUTING.md`:

```md
# Contributing to Kaisel

Thank you for helping. The most valuable contributions are:

1. **Lab results** from real jailbroken or instrumented devices (see [docs/LAB.md](docs/LAB.md)).
2. **False-positive reports** from stock devices, with the result JSON.
3. **New indicators** for current jailbreaks, hiding tweaks and instrumentation tools.
4. Documentation fixes.

All contributions must come from authorised testing on devices you own or are permitted to test.

## Development setup

```bash
brew install xcodegen
git clone https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel.git && cd Kaisel
cd Apps && xcodegen generate && open Kaisel.xcodeproj
```

Reference server and tools:

```bash
cd Server && python3 -m venv .venv && . .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
python3 -m unittest discover -s tests
```

## Ground rules for detection changes

- **No false positives on stock devices.** A signal that a stock, sealed iOS can produce must not be
  `conclusive` and must not be a hard indicator. Explain in the pull request why the new evidence is
  impossible on a stock device, or mark it as an indicator.
- **No plaintext indicators.** Add paths, library markers and URL schemes to `Scripts/gen_strings.py`
  and regenerate with `python3 Scripts/gen_strings.py`. Commit the generated files.
- **URL schemes** must be audited against every stock Apple app on iOS 15 to 27 before being added.
- **Weights** live in `Sources/KaiselKit/Finding.swift`. After changing them, run
  `python3 Server/tools/export_weights.py` so the server stays in sync.
- Add or update the expectation in `Lab/expected.json` for any new finding code.

## Pull requests

1. Fork, then branch from `main` (`feature/…`, `fix/…`, `detect/…`).
2. Keep pull requests focused; one detection technique per pull request is ideal.
3. Make sure CI passes: the package and both apps build, and the server tests pass.
4. Update `CHANGELOG.md` under **Unreleased**.
5. Fill in the pull request template, including how you tested and on which iOS versions.

## Style

- Swift: follow the existing style; avoid long chained expressions that slow the type checker.
- C: C11, `-Wall -Wextra` clean, no heap allocation in the hot path, raw syscalls through `rasp_internal.h`.
- Python: standard library first, PEP 8.
- Docs: British English, concise and technical.

## Commit messages

Use the imperative mood, for example `Detect ElleKit loader on iOS 17`. Reference issues with `Fixes #123`.

## Licence

By contributing you agree that your contributions are licensed under the [MIT licence](LICENSE).

```

`IMPLEMENTATION_REPORT.md`:

```md
# Implementation report: Kaisel 1.4.0

Scope: the "Kaisel v1.4.0 Implementation Specification" (P0, P1, P2), applied to the repository at
commit `1cbb965`. Date: 2026-11-21. 1.4.0 is **not released or tagged**; device-dependent release gates
are open (see [docs/V1.4.0-VALIDATION.md](docs/V1.4.0-VALIDATION.md)).

## Defects addressed

Each spec claim was checked against the code before it was changed.

| # | Claim | Verified? | Where | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Broad image markers (`crane`, `liberty`, `choicy`, ...) are matched against the whole path | **Confirmed** | `rasp_images.c` `rasp_classify_image` (1.3.1) | Markers apply only to the file name of external images and yield 206 (indicator) |
| 2 | Swift and Python infer an injector from any suspicious image not mentioning Frida | **Confirmed** | `JailbreakEvidence.isTweakInjector`, `policy._is_tweak_injector` | Explicit conclusive allow-list; 200 re-validated by the classifier |
| 3 | Bundle prefix check has no directory boundary | **Confirmed** | `in_bundle` (1.3.1): `/var/App.app2` matched `/var/App.app` | `rasp_path_within` with boundary and `/private` normalisation |
| 4 | Remote-rules markers can create a conclusive verdict | **Confirmed** | `rasp_rules_marker_match` returned 200 | Now 206 only |
| 5 | Late-loaded images conclusive on confidence | **Confirmed** (not in the spec) | `imageAddedAtRuntime` at 95 | Late images reported with their class plus 203 (indicator) |
| 6 | Server does not enforce completion bitmaps | **Confirmed** | `server.verify_sealed` (1.3.1) | `policy.validate_report`, server-side SDK profiles, strict mode |
| 7 | Arbitrary self-reported key used as device identity for history | **Confirmed** | `hist_key = "se:" + ...` for unbound signatures | History keyed by App Attest-verified key only |
| 8 | Tampered payload raised an exception rather than a structured decision | Confirmed (minor) | `verify_sealed` | Structured `deny` with reasons |

Replaying the shared fixtures through the 1.3.1 rule shows the false positives concretely: 1.3.1 would
have reported **conclusive** evidence for the `CraneBank` app executable, `CraneBank.framework`,
`LibertyFinance.framework`, `ChoicyAnalytics.framework`, an in-app framework re-validated without a
bundle path, and two external images whose names contain broad markers. One case moves the other way:
`/usr/lib/libsubstrate.dylib` (rootful) is now conclusive by exact identity; 1.3.1 missed it because no
marker matched it, and the same file was already conclusive as a jailbreak path (100).

## Where I disagree with or narrowed the spec

- **"Don't trust a framework solely because it is in the bundle."** Implemented only by identity: an
  in-bundle image with a known Frida, Cycript or injector name is flagged (205). Telling the app's own
  frameworks from a renamed library added by a re-signer needs a build manifest, which is new detection
  outside this release; it stays on the roadmap and is recorded as a limitation.
- **Incomplete reports in strict mode** give `step_up`, not `deny`. The spec allows either; `step_up` is
  never `allow`, and a hard `checkSuppressed` signal usually pushes the runtime verdict to `deny` anyway.
- **Swift/Python parity** is enforced by parsing the Swift sets and comparing them with the manifest and
  by running one fixture file through C, Python and Swift. The Swift scorer is not executed side by side
  with the Python scorer on the same reports.
- **Real-device evidence (P1.1)** could not be produced: no device runs of 1.4.0 have been made and no
  JSON was exported from earlier runs. Those scenarios are NOT TESTED; existing screenshots are cited as
  screenshots. No result JSON was constructed by hand.
- **P2** is delivered as measurement plans and templates (`Lab/SCENARIOS.md`), as the spec asks. It
  deliberately contains no instructions for building, renaming or concealing instrumentation or
  jailbreak tooling.

## Files changed

C core: `include/rasp_core.h` (codes 205, 206, image-class API), `rasp_classify.c` (new), `rasp_images.c`,
`rasp_frida.c`, `rasp_internal.h`, `rasp_strings_table.h` (regenerated).
Swift: `Finding.swift`, `JailbreakDetector.swift` (`ImageEvidence`, new `JailbreakEvidence` rule,
`JailbreakResult.checksIncomplete`/`sdkVersion`), `IntegrityKit.swift`, `Report.swift`
(`reportSchemaVersion`), `Checks/CoreBridge.swift`, `DetectionRules.swift`, `ObfuscatedLists.swift`
(regenerated).
Server: `classification.json` (new), `classification.py` (new), `policy.py`, `server.py`, `weights.json`
(regenerated), tests `test_server.py` (updated), `test_validation.py`, `test_classification.py`,
`test_parity.py` (new).
Scripts and lab: `Scripts/gen_strings.py` (manifest input, `--verify`), `Lab/compare_results.py`,
`Lab/expected.json`, `Lab/LAB_CHECKLIST.md`, `Lab/SCENARIOS.md`, `Lab/COVERAGE.md`, `Lab/templates/*`,
`Lab/tests/test_compare_results.py`.
Tests: `Tests/Fixtures/image_classification.json`, `Tests/CClassifier/*`,
`Tests/KaiselKitTests/ImageClassificationTests.swift`.
CI: `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (C classifier job, lab tests, string-table verification).
Docs: `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/DETECTION.md`, `docs/SERVER.md`, `docs/LIMITATIONS.md`,
`docs/LAB.md`, `docs/V1.4.0-VALIDATION.md`, this report.

## Tests executed

| Suite | Where | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Server unit tests (`Server/tests`: 14 existing, 32 validation, 9 classification, 9 parity) | Linux, Python 3.13 | **64/64 passed** |
| Lab tooling tests (`Lab/tests`) | Linux, Python 3.13 | **11/11 passed** |
| C image classifier vs fixtures and server (`Tests/CClassifier/run_tests.py`) | Linux, clang and gcc, `-Wall -Wextra -Werror` | **34/34 agree** with both compilers |
| `gen_strings.py --verify` and seeded reproducibility | Linux | **Passed** |
| `py_compile` of lab and server tools | Linux | **Passed** |
| Swift package tests (`xcodebuild test -scheme KaiselKit`) | macOS, Xcode 27.2 (27B52), Swift 6.4.2, iPhone 18 Pro simulator | **27/27 passed** (ImageClassificationTests 9, HardeningTests 3, JailbreakEvidenceTests 5, RiskScorerTests 10) |
| App builds (KaiselJBCheck, KaiselIntegrityCheck, KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest) | macOS, Xcode 27.2, Release, generic iOS, unsigned | **All three succeeded**, 0 errors (1 standard AppIntents metadata warning each) |
| Full C core compile (Apple headers) | macOS, Xcode 27.2 | **Passed** (compiled as part of the three app builds and the package tests) |
| Server, lab, C classifier and `gen_strings.py --verify` re-run on the Mac | macOS, Python venv from `Server/requirements.txt` | **Passed** (64, 11, 34/34, 205 strings) |

## Build status

Built and tested on macOS (Xcode 27.2, Swift 6.4.2) on 2026-11-21: the three apps build unsigned in
Release for generic iOS, and the KaiselKit package tests pass on the iPhone 18 Pro simulator. The server,
lab, C classifier and string-table checks were also re-run on the Mac and passed. No source changes were
needed. This is a build and simulator result only: 1.4.0 has not been run on a device.

## Real-device evidence

None for 1.4.0. Existing evidence (screenshots and owner reports for 1.2 to 1.3.x) is listed in
[Lab/COVERAGE.md](Lab/COVERAGE.md).

## Unsupported or untested scenarios

Everything in `Lab/expected.json` except the runs listed in COVERAGE.md: palera1n, Dopamine without
RootHide, Shadow-class hiding, all Frida scenarios, iOS 16 to 25, iPad, the App Attest variant on a
device, and the reference server with a real App Attest enrolment.

## Remaining risks

- RootHide per-app hiding remains a measured miss.
- In-bundle code is classified by identity only.
- Completion bits are self-reported; App Attest does not prove in-process measurements are true.
- 1.4.0 has not been run on a stock device, so a new false positive cannot yet be ruled out.
- Strict mode rejects 1.3.x reports; deploy the SDK before switching a production server to strict.

## Regressions identified

None in the suites that ran. Existing server tests were updated in three places to reflect deliberate
behaviour changes: strict mode requires App Attest even with `require_app_attest=False` (lab mode now
gives `step_up`), the end-to-end test now sends a complete 1.4.0 report, and a tampered payload returns
`deny` instead of raising `ValueError`.

`Lab/COVERAGE.md`:

```md
# Coverage

What Kaisel detects, how each claim is backed, and what has never been tested. Updated for 1.4.0
(unreleased) on 2026-11-21. **Measured** means observed on a real device; everything else is a design
intent or a unit test.

Evidence levels used below:

- **Implemented**: code exists.
- **Unit-tested**: an automated test exercises the logic on synthetic input (C, Swift or server).
- **Device-validated**: observed on a real device, with the evidence named. "Screenshots" means the app
  JSON was not exported, so individual finding codes and confidences beyond what is on screen are unknown.

## Devices and OS versions actually tested

| Device | iOS | Kaisel | Scenario | Evidence | Result |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| iPhone9,3 (iPhone 7) | 15.8.2 | 1.2.0 | Dopamine + RootHide, not hidden | Screenshots (transcribed in [the lab record](results/2026-11-21_iphone9-3_ios15.8.2_dopamine-roothide.md)) | JB Check PASS; full app inconsistent (fixed in 1.3.0) |
| iPhone9,3 | 15.8.2 | 1.2.0 | Dopamine + RootHide, hidden | Screenshots | FAIL: both apps clean |
| iPhone9,3 | 15.8.2 | 1.3.x | Dopamine + RootHide, not hidden | [Screenshots](../docs/images/lab/) | PASS: Jailbroken; device Compromised 85, runtime Compromised 97 |
| iPhone9,3 | 15.8.2 | 1.3.x | Dopamine + RootHide, hidden | [Screenshots](../docs/images/lab/) | FAIL: Not jailbroken, Trusted 0 (known gap) |
| iPhone9,3 | 15.8.2 | 1.3.1 | Dopamine + RootHide, hidden | Owner's report only | Suspicious; not exported and not reproduced, so unconfirmed |
| iPhone18,1 | 27.2.1 | 1.0 / 1.3.0 | Stock | Screenshots | False positives, fixed in 1.1 and 1.3.1 |
| iPhone18,1 | 27.2.1 | 1.3.x | Stock | [Screenshots](../docs/images/lab/) | PASS: Not jailbroken, Trusted 0 |
| Not recorded | 26 | 1.3.1 | Stock | Owner's report only | PASS: clean |
| Not recorded | Not recorded | 1.3.1 | TrollStore (TrollInstallerX) | Owner's report only | PASS: detected by both apps |

**Kaisel 1.4.0 has not been run on any device.** Its classification changes are unit-tested only.
No result JSON has been exported for any run, so `compare_results.py` has nothing to check yet.

## Assessment categories

| Category | Main codes | Implemented | Unit-tested | Device-validated |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Rootless jailbreak artefacts (`/var/jb`) | 105, 100, 101 | Yes | Scoring only | No (RootHide removes `/var/jb`) |
| Rootful artefacts, sealed volume | 102, 103, 104 | Yes | Scoring only | No |
| Sandbox integrity | 120, 121, 122 | Yes | Scoring only | No |
| Hiding hooks (libc vs kernel) | 108 | Yes | Scoring only | No |
| Known jailbreak injector image | 200 | Yes (1.4.0 identity rules) | Yes: C, Swift, server, 34 shared fixtures | Yes: `roothideinit.dylib`, iPhone9,3 / 15.8.2 (1.2 and 1.3.x, screenshots) |
| Instrumentation image (Frida, Cycript, in-bundle injector) | 205 | Yes (new in 1.4.0) | Yes | No |
| Broad marker / unknown external image | 206, 201 | Yes | Yes | No |
| Image-list filtering | 202 | Yes | Scoring only | No |
| Late-loaded images | 203 | Yes | Classification only | No |
| Inline hooks, rebinding, swizzling | 300-306 | Yes | Scoring only | Partly: 304 seen with RootHide unhidden (1.2) |
| Debugger | 400-403 | Yes | Scoring only | Xcode debug run on iPhone18,1 (1.0) |
| Frida runtime (threads, D-Bus, memory) | 500-504 | Yes | Scoring only | No |
| Code signing, entitlements (TrollStore) | 600-610 | Yes | Scoring only | TrollStore detected (owner's report, 1.3.1) |
| Package-manager URL schemes | 905 | Yes | Scoring only | Yes: `sileo://` with RootHide unhidden |
| Residual signals for hidden jailbreaks | 110, 111, 908 | Yes | Yes | **Did not fire** in the 1.3.x hidden run |
| Server per-key history | server | Yes (App Attest key only since 1.4.0) | Yes | No |
| Server strict report validation | server | Yes (new in 1.4.0) | Yes (32 tests) | No |

## Detected and missed conditions (measured)

| Condition | Outcome |
|---|---|
| RootHide injector in a non-hidden app | Detected (conclusive) |
| RootHide per-app hiding (Blacklist) | **Missed**, both apps, two builds |
| Package-manager URL scheme with RootHide unhidden | Detected (indicator) |
| Stock iPhone18,1 / iOS 27.2.1 | No false positive since 1.3.1 |
| Stock iOS 26 device | No false positive (owner's report) |

## False-positive regressions

| Release | Device | Cause | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1.0 | iPhone18,1 / 27.2.1 | Build-context signals, Developer Mode, `apple-magnifier`, anonymous r-x region | Fixed in 1.1 (scored as informational) |
| 1.3.0 | iPhone18,1 / 27.2.1 | `dyldModified` compared shared-cache dyld with `/usr/lib/dyld` | Fixed in 1.3.1 (indicator only, skipped in cache) |
| up to 1.3.1 | None observed | Broad image markers (`crane`, `liberty`, `choicy`, ...) matched anywhere in a path and were conclusive | Fixed in 1.4.0 by code review; covered by fixtures, never observed on a device |

## Unknown coverage

Not tested on any device: palera1n (rootless and rootful), Dopamine without RootHide, Dopamine on A12+,
any Shadow-class hiding tweak, frida-server attach or spawn, FridaGadget repackaging, renamed
instrumentation, late attach, iOS 16, 17, 18, 19 to 25, any iPad, the App Attest variant on a device,
and the reference server against a real App Attest enrolment.

## Open research gaps

- Per-app hiding (RootHide Blacklist): no in-process signal survived in measured runs. Candidates
  (mount-table enumeration, DeviceCheck bits) are listed in the README roadmap and are unproven.
- In-bundle code is classified by identity only. A re-signed app with a renamed injected library inside
  its bundle is not detected by image classification; a build manifest of expected bundle contents is
  needed (roadmap).
- Completion bits are self-reported. App Attest proves which app instance signed a report, not that the
  measurements inside a compromised process are true.

`Lab/LAB_CHECKLIST.md`:

```md
# Lab validation checklist

Run only on test devices you own or are authorised to test. The goal is to
measure, on real jailbroken and instrumented devices, which signals fire, and
to catch false positives on stock devices. One jailbroken device has been tested so far:
[Dopamine + RootHide on iOS 15.8.2](results/2026-11-21_iphone9-3_ios15.8.2_dopamine-roothide.md).
What has been measured is in [COVERAGE.md](COVERAGE.md); everything else in `expected.json` is a
hypothesis. Measurement scenarios for Frida and hiding are in [SCENARIOS.md](SCENARIOS.md).

## Devices to cover

| Device | iOS | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Any stock iPhone, latest iOS (e.g. iPhone18,1 / iOS 27) | 27.x | False-positive baseline. Already run once with v1.0. |
| A12+ device on iOS 15.0-16.6.1 | 15/16 | Dopamine (rootless), with and without a hiding tweak |
| A8-A11 device (iPhone 8/X) | 15/16 | palera1n rootless and rootful |
| Any device on 15.0-16.6.1 or 17.0 | | TrollStore install |
| Jailbroken device + frida-server | any | Frida attach and spawn |
| Stock device | any | FridaGadget-repackaged IPA |

## Per scenario

1. Install **Kaisel JB Check** and **Kaisel Integrity Check** (see [docs/BUILDING.md](../docs/BUILDING.md)).
2. Set up the scenario exactly as described in `expected.json`.
3. Open each app, wait for the result, tap **Copy result JSON**, and paste it into
   `Lab/results/<scenario>__<app>__<device-ios>.json`, for example
   `dopamine__jbcheck__iphonexs-16.5.json`.
4. Copy [`templates/run.meta.json`](templates/run.meta.json) next to it as
   `<same name>.meta.json` and fill in every field: Kaisel version and commit, device model, exact iOS,
   tool and version, install method, scenario, whether the app was hidden, reproduction steps and
   limitations. Set `sanitised` to true only after removing personal identifiers, internal app names
   and local paths. Write the narrative with [`templates/RESULT_RECORD.md`](templates/RESULT_RECORD.md)
   and add a line to `Lab/results/NOTES.md`. Never edit or hand-write the app JSON.
5. Optional, but valuable: repeat with the app opened a second time, after
   attaching Frida, and after enabling or disabling the hiding tweak.

Scenario keys: `stock`, `stock_ios26`, `xcode_debugger`, `dopamine`, `dopamine_hidden`,
`dopamine_roothide`, `dopamine_roothide_hidden`, `dopamine_roothide_hidden_fresh`,
`dopamine_roothide_hidden_seen`, `palera1n_rootless`, `palera1n_rootful`, `trollstore`, `frida_server`,
`frida_spawn`, `frida_gadget`, `frida_renamed`, `frida_late_attach`, `shadow_class_hiding`.

## RootHide (Dopamine) scenarios

1. **`dopamine_roothide`**: install both apps, make sure neither is in RootHide's **Blacklist**, run, export.
2. **`dopamine_roothide_hidden`**: in the RootHide app, enable both Kaisel apps in **Blacklist**, force-quit
   them, open again, run, export.
3. Run the hidden case twice: once on a fresh install (no history), and once after an unhidden run
   (tests `previouslyJailbroken` and the server history).
4. Note whether `kern.userspacereboottime` fired (111) and whether `dyldModified` (110) fired.
   If 110 fires on any **stock** device, report it immediately as a false positive.

## Check the results

```bash
python3 Lab/compare_results.py --coverage --require-metadata Lab/results/*.json
```

Each row prints PASS, PARTIAL, FAIL, INVALID or NOT TESTED, the codes that fired and whether every
check completed. A scenario you could not run stays NOT TESTED; never mark it otherwise. Send back the table,
`NOTES.md` and the JSON files. The results contain no personal data: they hold
device model, iOS version, finding codes and file paths of jailbreak artefacts.

## Frida scenario commands (jailbroken device)

```bash
frida-ps -U | grep -i integrity
frida -U -n KaiselIntegrityCheck -e 'Interceptor.attach(Module.getExportByName(null, "stat"), {onEnter(){}})'
# then tap "Evaluate now" in Kaisel Integrity Check and copy the result
```

## What to look for

- **Stock devices**: any `scored` signal is a false positive. Report it with the
  `detail` text. This matters most.
- **Jailbroken devices**: which codes fire, and whether the hiding tweak
  removes them. The codes that survive hiding are your strongest signals.
- `checkSuppressed`, `prologueChanged` or `textModified` appearing means a
  bypass tool is actively patching the kit. Note which tool.

```

`Lab/SCENARIOS.md`:

```md
# Assessment scenarios (P2)

Repeatable scenarios for measuring which existing Kaisel detectors respond to instrumentation, jailbreaks
and hiding. They are measurement plans, not new detection: a heuristic is added only after it has been
shown not to fire on stock devices (see [COVERAGE.md](COVERAGE.md)).

Run them only on devices you own or are authorised to test, with tools obtained from their official
sources. This document says what to set up and what to record. It does not describe how to build, modify
or conceal instrumentation or jailbreak tooling.

Each scenario key matches [`expected.json`](expected.json). Record every run with
[`templates/RESULT_RECORD.md`](templates/RESULT_RECORD.md) and
[`templates/run.meta.json`](templates/run.meta.json), and export JSON from both apps.

Keep the two questions apart throughout: **is the device jailbroken?** (Kaisel JB Check, device domain)
and **is this process instrumented?** (runtime domain). Frida on its own never makes a device "Jailbroken".

## Runtime instrumentation (Frida)

| Key | Set-up | Record | Expected (hypothesis) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `frida_server` | Jailbroken lab device, frida-server running, Frida attached to the already running Kaisel Integrity Check with a simple hook | Codes 500, 501, 502, 503, 504, 205, 300, 301, 306, 610, 1000 | Runtime high or compromised. Device verdict comes from the jailbreak, not from Frida |
| `frida_spawn` | Same device, the app spawned under Frida | As above | Runtime high or compromised, detected at first evaluation |
| `frida_gadget` | Stock device, an authorised lab build of Kaisel Integrity Check repackaged with FridaGadget and re-signed | 205, 500, 503, 504, 603, 900, 901, 601, server App Attest result | Runtime high or compromised; JB Check *Not jailbroken* or *Suspicious*, never *Jailbroken* |
| `frida_renamed` | Authorised test environment where instrumentation file and thread names are not Frida's defaults | 201, 206, 503, 504, 300, 301, 306, 610, 901, 1000 | Name-based detectors (205, 500) miss. Record which behaviour-based detectors still respond |
| `frida_late_attach` | Evaluate once with nothing attached, then attach, then **Evaluate now** again with IntegrityMonitor running | 203, 205, 206, 500, 504, 306, 610, 1003 | The second evaluation reports the late image (203 plus its class) or runtime hooks |

## Jailbreaks and hiding

| Key | Set-up | Record | Expected (hypothesis) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `palera1n_rootless` | palera1n rootless on a checkm8 device | 105, 100, 200, 905, 202 | Jailbroken |
| `palera1n_rootful` | palera1n rootful (fakefs), where the device supports it | 104, 103, 102, 100 | Jailbroken |
| `dopamine` | Dopamine, no hiding | 105, 100, 200, 701, 905 | Jailbroken |
| `dopamine_hidden` | Dopamine with a hiding tweak enabled for the app | 108, 202, 300, 301, 304, 305 | Suspicious or Jailbroken |
| `dopamine_roothide` | Dopamine + RootHide, app **not** on the Blacklist | 200 (`roothideinit.dylib`), 905 | Jailbroken. **Measured: PASS** (see COVERAGE.md) |
| `dopamine_roothide_hidden` | Dopamine + RootHide, app on the Blacklist | 110, 111, 908, every code that survives | Target Suspicious. **Measured: missed** (known gap) |
| `dopamine_roothide_hidden_fresh` | Blacklist on a fresh install that never ran unhidden | 110, 111 | Expected miss today; measures what survives with no memory |
| `dopamine_roothide_hidden_seen` | Blacklist after a verified unhidden run of the same install; reference server in strict mode | 908 on the device; server decision and reason | Device Suspicious (908); server `deny` with "previously seen" |
| `shadow_class_hiding` | Jailbroken device with a Shadow-class hiding tweak enabled for the app | 108, 202, 300, 301, 303, 304, 305, 201, 206 | Suspicious or Jailbroken (hooks on file and dyld APIs are visible) |

For every hiding scenario, write down which checks still produced evidence and which stopped. Those that
survive hiding are the strongest signals; those that stop are documented as limitations.

## False-positive guard

Before any detection change is scored, run the `stock` scenario on every stock device available
(currently iPhone18,1 on iOS 27.2.1 and an iOS 26 device) and confirm no conclusive finding and no scored
signal. A new heuristic that fires on a stock device is not shipped.

```

`Lab/compare_results.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Checks exported lab results against expected.json (Kaisel 1.4.0).

Each real-device run is two files in Lab/results/:

  <scenario>__<app>__<device>.json       the app's own export ("Copy result JSON")
  <scenario>__<app>__<device>.meta.json  the run record (see METADATA_FIELDS below)

for example dopamine_roothide__jbcheck__iphone9-3-15.8.2.json and its .meta.json.
<app> is jbcheck or integritycheck. Paste the export exactly as copied: do not
build or edit result JSON by hand. Redact personal identifiers, internal app
names and local paths in the metadata before publishing.

  python3 Lab/compare_results.py Lab/results/*.json              # PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL per file
  python3 Lab/compare_results.py --coverage Lab/results/*.json   # also lists NOT TESTED scenarios
  python3 Lab/compare_results.py --require-metadata ...          # fail on a missing or invalid .meta.json

Outcome per file:
  PASS     the verdict expectation and the code expectation are both met
  PARTIAL  one of the two is met
  FAIL     neither is met (or the only expectation is missed)
  INVALID  the file is not a recognisable app export, or its metadata is invalid
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
with open(os.path.join(HERE, "expected.json")) as _f:
    EXPECTED = {k: v for k, v in json.load(_f).items() if not k.startswith("_")}

APPS = ("jbcheck", "integritycheck")
OUTCOMES = ("PASS", "PARTIAL", "FAIL", "NOT TESTED")
# Required run-record fields (spec P1.1). Types: str unless noted.
METADATA_FIELDS = {
    "kaiselVersion": str,          # e.g. "1.4.0"
    "sourceCommit": str,           # git commit the apps were built from
    "deviceModel": str,            # e.g. "iPhone9,3"
    "iosVersion": str,             # exact, e.g. "15.8.2"
    "tool": str,                   # jailbreak or instrumentation tool and version, or "none"
    "installMethod": str,          # e.g. "Xcode", "Sideloadly (free Apple ID)", "TrollStore"
    "scenario": str,               # key in expected.json
    "hiddenFromInjection": bool,   # app on a hiding list (RootHide Blacklist, Choicy, Shadow)
    "date": str,                   # YYYY-MM-DD
    "reproductionSteps": list,     # ordered steps, as text
    "limitations": list,           # anything that weakens the result
    "sanitised": bool,             # must be true before publishing
}


def load_json(path: str):
    with open(path) as f:
        return json.load(f)


def codes_of(result: dict) -> set:
    sigs = result.get("signals") or (result.get("conclusive", []) + result.get("indicators", []))
    return {int(s["code"]) for s in sigs if isinstance(s, dict) and "code" in s}


def validate_export(app: str, r) -> list[str]:
    """Structural check that this is an app export, not a hand-made summary."""
    if not isinstance(r, dict):
        return ["result is not a JSON object"]
    need = {"jbcheck": ("status", "conclusive", "indicators", "checksRun", "model", "osVersion"),
            "integritycheck": ("sdkVersion", "signals", "checksCompleted", "checksExpected", "device", "runtime",
                               "posture")}[app]
    missing = [k for k in need if k not in r]
    return [f"not an app export: missing {', '.join(missing)}"] if missing else []


def validate_metadata(meta, scenario: str) -> list[str]:
    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
        return ["metadata is not a JSON object"]
    problems = []
    for k, t in METADATA_FIELDS.items():
        if k not in meta:
            problems.append(f"metadata missing {k}")
        elif not isinstance(meta[k], t) or (t is str and not meta[k].strip()):
            problems.append(f"metadata {k} must be a non-empty {t.__name__}")
    if meta.get("scenario") not in (None, scenario):
        problems.append(f"metadata scenario {meta.get('scenario')!r} differs from file name {scenario!r}")
    if meta.get("sanitised") is False:
        problems.append("metadata sanitised is false: redact before publishing")
    return problems


def completion_note(app: str, r: dict) -> str:
    if app == "integritycheck":
        done, exp = r.get("checksCompleted"), r.get("checksExpected")
        if isinstance(done, int) and isinstance(exp, int):
            return "checks complete" if done & exp == exp else f"checks incomplete (mask {exp & ~done:#x})"
    if app == "jbcheck":
        inc = r.get("checksIncomplete")
        if inc:
            return f"checks incomplete: {', '.join(inc)}"
        return "checks complete" if inc == [] else "completion not exported (pre-1.4.0 build)"
    return ""


def evaluate(app: str, exp: dict, r: dict) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    verdict_ok, verdict_checked, problems = True, False, []
    if app == "jbcheck" and "status" in exp:
        verdict_checked = True
        if r.get("status") not in exp["status"]:
            verdict_ok = False
            problems.append(f"status {r.get('status')} not in {exp['status']}")
    if app == "integritycheck":
        for dom in ("device", "runtime"):
            if dom in exp:
                verdict_checked = True
                v = (r.get(dom) or {}).get("verdict")
                if v not in exp[dom]:
                    verdict_ok = False
                    problems.append(f"{dom} {v} not in {exp[dom]}")
    codes_checked = "any_codes" in exp
    codes_ok = not codes_checked or bool(codes_of(r) & set(exp["any_codes"]))
    if not codes_ok:
        problems.append(f"none of codes {exp['any_codes']} seen")
    if verdict_ok and codes_ok:
        return "PASS", problems
    if verdict_checked and codes_checked and (verdict_ok or codes_ok):
        return "PARTIAL", problems
    return "FAIL", problems


def check(path: str, require_metadata: bool = False) -> dict:
    name = os.path.basename(path)[:-len(".json")]
    parts = name.split("__")
    row = {"file": os.path.basename(path), "scenario": parts[0], "app": parts[1].lower() if len(parts) > 1 else "?"}
    if len(parts) < 3:
        return {**row, "outcome": "INVALID", "why": "file name must be <scenario>__<app>__<device>.json"}
    scenario, app = row["scenario"], row["app"]
    if app not in APPS:
        return {**row, "outcome": "INVALID", "why": f"app must be one of {APPS}"}
    exp = EXPECTED.get(scenario, {}).get(app)
    if exp is None:
        return {**row, "outcome": "INVALID", "why": "no expectation for this scenario/app in expected.json"}
    try:
        r = load_json(path)
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
        return {**row, "outcome": "INVALID", "why": f"unreadable JSON: {e}"}
    problems = validate_export(app, r)
    meta_path = path[:-len(".json")] + ".meta.json"
    if os.path.exists(meta_path):
        try:
            problems += validate_metadata(load_json(meta_path), scenario)
        except ValueError as e:
            problems.append(f"unreadable metadata: {e}")
    elif require_metadata:
        problems.append(f"missing {os.path.basename(meta_path)}")
    if problems:
        return {**row, "outcome": "INVALID", "why": "; ".join(problems)}
    outcome, why = evaluate(app, exp, r)
    gap = " [known gap]" if EXPECTED[scenario].get("knownGap") and outcome != "PASS" else ""
    detail = "; ".join(why) or "expectations met"
    return {**row, "outcome": outcome,
            "why": f"{detail}{gap}; codes {sorted(codes_of(r))}; {completion_note(app, r)}"}


def coverage(rows: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    seen = {(r["scenario"], r["app"]) for r in rows if r["outcome"] in OUTCOMES}
    out = []
    for scenario, exp in EXPECTED.items():
        for app in APPS:
            if app in exp and (scenario, app) not in seen:
                out.append({"file": "-", "scenario": scenario, "app": app, "outcome": "NOT TESTED",
                            "why": "no exported result"})
    return out


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("paths", nargs="*")
    ap.add_argument("--coverage", action="store_true", help="also list scenarios with no result as NOT TESTED")
    ap.add_argument("--require-metadata", action="store_true", help="treat a missing .meta.json as INVALID")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    paths = [p for p in a.paths if not p.endswith(".meta.json")]
    rows = [check(p, a.require_metadata) for p in paths]
    if a.coverage:
        rows += coverage(rows)
    if not rows:
        print("no results given")
        return 0
    w = max(len(r["scenario"]) for r in rows)
    for r in rows:
        print(f"{r['outcome']:<10}  {r['scenario']:<{w}}  {r['app']:<15}  {r['why']}")
    return 1 if any(r["outcome"] in ("FAIL", "INVALID") for r in rows) else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`Lab/results/2026-11-21_iphone9-3_ios15.8.2_dopamine-roothide.md`:

```md
# Lab run: Dopamine + RootHide on iPhone9,3 (iOS 15.8.2)

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Date | 2026-11-21 |
| Device / iOS | iPhone9,3 (iPhone 7, A10), iOS 15.8.2 |
| Jailbreak | Dopamine rootless, RootHide variant (randomised `.jbroot-*` under `/var/containers/Bundle/Application/`) |
| Installed | Sileo, Zebra |
| Kit version | 1.2.0 (apps named "Jailbreak Check" and "Integrity Demo" at the time) |
| Source | Screenshots of both apps; result JSON not exported. Values below are transcribed. |

## Run 1: app not hidden (scenario `dopamine_roothide`)

| App | Result |
|---|---|
| JB Check | **Jailbroken**. Conclusive: known injection library loaded, `.jbroot-…/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib` (200). Indicators: syscall stub does not trap, `csops: no svc #0x80 in first 8 insns` (304); package-manager URL scheme `sileo://` (905). |
| Integrity Check | Device **Elevated 56**; this app's runtime **Compromised 97**; protected verdict compromised. |

**Verdict: PASS for JB Check. Inconsistent for the full app**: the conclusive injector is scored in the
runtime domain, so the device domain only saw the URL scheme. Fixed in 1.3.0: both apps now use one rule
(`JailbreakEvidence`), and conclusive evidence in any domain makes the device **Compromised**.

**RootHide note:** RootHide hides the jailbreak by randomising the jailbreak root and removing `/var/jb`,
so every path-based check missed it. It was defeated here only because RootHide still injects
`roothideinit.dylib` into apps that are not on its Blacklist, and the dyld image list exposed it.

## Run 2: app hidden with RootHide's Blacklist (scenario `dopamine_roothide_hidden`)

| App | Result |
|---|---|
| JB Check | **Not jailbroken**. Conclusive: none. Indicators: none. All checks ran (filesystem, sandbox, images, hooks, environment, codesign, canary, urlSchemes), 36 ms. |
| Integrity Check | Device **Trusted 0**, runtime **Trusted 0**, inline guard clean, protected verdict trusted. |

**Verdict: FAIL (detection gap).** Blacklisting stops injection into the app and hides the package managers'
URL schemes, so the app sees a near-stock environment. Every signal from run 1 disappeared, including the
syscall-stub indicator, which shows that indicator came from the injected code and was a true positive.

## What 1.3.0 adds for this case (to be re-tested)

| Code | Check | Why it may survive per-app hiding |
|---|---|---|
| 110 `dyldModified` | Compares dyld's `__text` in memory with `/usr/lib/dyld` on disk (same LC_UUID) and checks the path dyld was loaded from. Indicator since 1.3.1 | A jailbreak that patches dyld does so for every spawned process. On iOS 15 dyld runs from the file, so the comparison is meaningful there |
| 111 `userspaceReboot` | Reads `kern.userspacereboottime` | Dopamine restarts userspace after the exploit; this is kernel state, not in the app |
| 908 `previouslyJailbroken` | Keychain marker written when conclusive evidence was seen | The marker from run 1 survives hiding; reported as an indicator |
| server history | Reference server remembers the Secure Enclave / App Attest key that once sent conclusive evidence | A hidden re-run signed by the same key is raised to `high` and denied |

## Run 3: Kaisel 1.3.x, not hidden and hidden (screenshots)

Source: screenshots in [`docs/images/lab/`](../../docs/images/lab/); result JSON not exported. Dopamine stayed off the Blacklist; only the two Kaisel apps were toggled.

| Scenario | App | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Not hidden | JB Check | **Jailbroken**, 44 ms. Conclusive: known injection library, `.jbroot-…/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib`. Indicator: `sileo://` URL scheme. **Reset jailbreak history** shown |
| Not hidden | Integrity Check | Device **Compromised 85**, jailbroken (1 conclusive); runtime **Compromised 97**; protected verdict compromised; inline guard flags 2 |
| Hidden | JB Check | **Not jailbroken**, 50 ms. Conclusive: none. Indicators: none. No Reset button, so the Keychain marker was not seen |
| Hidden | Integrity Check | Device **Trusted 0**, jailbreak not detected, runtime **Trusted 0**, protected verdict trusted, inline guard clean |

**Verdict:** unhidden PASS, and the 1.3.0 consistency fix holds (device and runtime both Compromised).
Hidden **FAIL**: none of `dyldModified` (110), `userspaceReboot` (111) or `previouslyJailbroken` (908) fired, so
the *Suspicious* result reported earlier for 1.3.1 is not confirmed. Export the hidden-run JSON to see whether
those checks ran and what they read.

## To complete this record

1. Install 1.3.0, run both apps unhidden once, then hidden, and use **Copy result JSON** each time.
2. Save the files as `Lab/results/dopamine_roothide__jbcheck__iphone9-3-15.8.2.json`, and so on (they are
   git-ignored; redact before sharing).
3. Run `python3 Lab/compare_results.py Lab/results/*.json` and update this file.

`Lab/tests/test_compare_results.py`:

```py
"""Tests for Lab/compare_results.py and Lab/expected.json (Kaisel 1.4.0, spec P1.2).

All inputs are synthetic and built in a temporary directory: they exercise the
comparison logic only and are not lab results.

  python3 -m unittest discover -s Lab/tests
"""
import json
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

LAB = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
sys.path.insert(0, LAB)
import compare_results as cr  # noqa: E402

META = {"kaiselVersion": "1.4.0", "sourceCommit": "0000000", "deviceModel": "iPhone0,0", "iosVersion": "0.0",
        "tool": "none", "installMethod": "Xcode", "scenario": "stock", "hiddenFromInjection": False,
        "date": "2026-01-01", "reproductionSteps": ["synthetic"], "limitations": ["synthetic"], "sanitised": True}
JB_CLEAN = {"status": "notJailbroken", "conclusive": [], "indicators": [], "checksRun": ["filesystem"],
            "checksIncomplete": [], "model": "iPhone0,0", "osVersion": "0.0", "sdkVersion": "1.4.0"}
IC_CLEAN = {"sdkVersion": "1.4.0", "signals": [], "checksCompleted": 2047, "checksExpected": 2047,
            "device": {"verdict": "trusted"}, "runtime": {"verdict": "trusted"}, "posture": {}}


class CompareResults(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def write(self, name, obj, meta=None):
        p = os.path.join(self.tmp.name, name)
        with open(p, "w") as f:
            json.dump(obj, f)
        if meta is not None:
            with open(p[:-5] + ".meta.json", "w") as f:
                json.dump(meta, f)
        return p

    def test_expected_json_is_well_formed(self):
        for scenario, exp in cr.EXPECTED.items():
            self.assertIn("description", exp, scenario)
            for app in cr.APPS:
                if app in exp:
                    e = exp[app]
                    self.assertTrue({"status", "device", "runtime", "any_codes"} & set(e), (scenario, app))
                    for c in e.get("any_codes", []):
                        self.assertIsInstance(c, int)

    def test_expected_codes_exist(self):
        weights = json.load(open(os.path.join(LAB, "..", "Server", "weights.json")))
        for scenario, exp in cr.EXPECTED.items():
            codes = set(exp.get("measure", []))
            for app in cr.APPS:
                codes |= set(exp.get(app, {}).get("any_codes", []))
            for c in codes:
                self.assertIn(str(c), weights, f"{scenario}: unknown code {c}")

    def test_pass(self):
        r = cr.check(self.write("stock__jbcheck__synthetic.json", JB_CLEAN, META))
        self.assertEqual(r["outcome"], "PASS", r)

    def test_fail_and_known_gap(self):
        meta = dict(META, scenario="dopamine_roothide_hidden", hiddenFromInjection=True)
        r = cr.check(self.write("dopamine_roothide_hidden__jbcheck__synthetic.json", JB_CLEAN, meta))
        self.assertEqual(r["outcome"], "FAIL")
        self.assertIn("known gap", r["why"])

    def test_partial(self):
        res = dict(JB_CLEAN, status="jailbroken", conclusive=[{"code": 105, "confidence": 99}])
        meta = dict(META, scenario="dopamine_roothide")
        r = cr.check(self.write("dopamine_roothide__jbcheck__synthetic.json", res, meta))
        self.assertEqual(r["outcome"], "PARTIAL", r)     # verdict met, expected code 200 absent

    def test_hand_made_summary_is_invalid(self):
        r = cr.check(self.write("stock__jbcheck__synthetic.json", {"status": "notJailbroken"}, META))
        self.assertEqual(r["outcome"], "INVALID")

    def test_metadata_required_and_validated(self):
        p = self.write("stock__integritycheck__synthetic.json", IC_CLEAN)
        self.assertEqual(cr.check(p)["outcome"], "PASS")
        self.assertEqual(cr.check(p, require_metadata=True)["outcome"], "INVALID")
        bad = dict(META)
        del bad["sourceCommit"]
        bad["sanitised"] = False
        r = cr.check(self.write("stock__integritycheck__synthetic.json", IC_CLEAN, bad))
        self.assertEqual(r["outcome"], "INVALID")
        self.assertIn("sourceCommit", r["why"])
        self.assertIn("sanitised", r["why"])

    def test_scenario_mismatch_in_metadata(self):
        r = cr.check(self.write("stock__jbcheck__synthetic.json", JB_CLEAN, dict(META, scenario="trollstore")))
        self.assertEqual(r["outcome"], "INVALID")

    def test_incomplete_checks_reported(self):
        res = dict(IC_CLEAN, checksCompleted=2047 & ~4)
        r = cr.check(self.write("stock__integritycheck__synthetic.json", res, META))
        self.assertIn("checks incomplete", r["why"])

    def test_coverage_lists_not_tested(self):
        rows = [cr.check(self.write("stock__jbcheck__synthetic.json", JB_CLEAN, META))]
        cov = cr.coverage(rows)
        self.assertNotIn(("stock", "jbcheck"), {(c["scenario"], c["app"]) for c in cov})
        self.assertIn(("palera1n_rootless", "jbcheck"), {(c["scenario"], c["app"]) for c in cov})
        self.assertTrue(all(c["outcome"] == "NOT TESTED" for c in cov))

    def test_bad_file_name(self):
        self.assertEqual(cr.check(self.write("stock.json", JB_CLEAN))["outcome"], "INVALID")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

<h1 align="center">Kaisel</h1>

<p align="center">
  Layered jailbreak, instrumentation and device-integrity detection for iOS 15 to 27.<br>
