## Apps

| | App | What it answers |
|---|---|---|
| <img src="Branding/KaiselJBCheck-1024.png" width="56"> | **Kaisel JB Check** | *Is this phone jailbroken?* **Jailbroken**, **Suspicious** or **Not jailbroken**, with the evidence. Nothing about how the app itself was built or signed. |
| <img src="Branding/KaiselIntegrityCheck-1024.png" width="56"> | **Kaisel Integrity Check** | Separate **device**, **runtime** and **app-build** verdicts with risk scores, phone posture (Developer Mode, passcode, biometry, Secure Enclave, App Attest), and a report sealed for server-side verification. |

Both apps install with a **free Apple ID** through Xcode, [Impactor](https://github.com/khcrysalis/impactor) or Sideloadly. Both show a **Reset jailbreak history** button while a "seen jailbroken" marker exists on the device.

## What Kaisel is, and is not

Kaisel is a reference implementation of the techniques commercial RASP products use for jailbreak and instrumentation detection. It cross-checks independent views of the same fact rather than grepping for paths, scores evidence per domain, and hands the final decision to a server.

It is **not** a certified product, it has no commercial-grade code obfuscation, and its indicator set is maintained by hand. It is suited to lab work, security assessments and as a baseline to compare RASP products against. It should not be the only control protecting a production app.

## Features

- **Jailbreak detection that is not a path grep.** Raw `svc #0x80` syscalls compared with libc to catch hiding tweaks, an `errno` existence oracle, sealed-system-volume and mount checks, sandbox-escape probes, and rootless (`/var/jb`) and rootful artefacts.
- **Injection and hooking.** Two independent loaded-image lists (dyld info vs `_dyld` API), explicit image classes (known jailbreak injector, instrumentation, broad marker, unknown external; since 1.4.0 only exact injector identities or jailbreak-root paths are conclusive), inline trampoline decoding, GOT / fishhook rebinding, `__interpose`, Objective-C swizzling and syscall-stub integrity.
- **Frida and debuggers.** Frida threads, D-Bus handshake probe, anonymous executable memory, `P_TRACED`, `CS_DEBUGGED`, exception ports and the parent process.
- **App integrity.** Entitlement blob (TrollStore and ldid fingerprints), FairPlay `cryptid`, provisioning profile, team and bundle ID, `__TEXT` hash.
- **Residual signals for hidden jailbreaks** (indicators, never conclusive on their own): dyld integrity where dyld runs from disk, userspace-reboot state, and a Keychain marker of an earlier conclusive result. None of them fired against RootHide's Blacklist in the latest lab runs (see below).
- **One jailbreak rule everywhere.** `JailbreakEvidence` decides what is conclusive for JB Check, the full kit, stored history and the server. Since 1.4.0 the rule and the image lists live in one manifest, `Server/classification.json`, and parity tests fail if the Swift, C and Python copies drift.
- **Self-protection.** Runtime self-integrity snapshots, scattered inline guards, check-completion bookkeeping, randomised check order, a tamper-evident verdict and XOR-obfuscated indicator strings.
- **Weighted scoring per domain.** Every signal is evidence with a weight; a damped noisy-OR produces device, runtime and app verdicts. Build and posture facts are informational unless you opt in.
- **Hardware-backed sealing.** App Attest assertions and a Secure Enclave P-256 signature bind a report to a server nonce.
- **Signed remote rules.** Add indicators and tune weights without an app release (ECDSA P-256, anti-rollback, expiry, additive only).
- **Reference server** in Python that verifies App Attest and the seal, validates the report against its own SDK profile (strict mode by default since 1.4.0: an incomplete, malformed, unsupported or unauthenticated report is never `allow`), re-scores with its own policy, remembers App Attest keys that were once seen jailbroken, and answers `allow`, `step_up` or `deny`.

## Lab results so far

Real-device results recorded for 1.3.x on 2026-11-21. Screenshots are below; details and earlier runs are in [docs/LAB.md](docs/LAB.md).

| Device / iOS | Scenario | Result |
|---|---|---|
| iPhone9,3 (iPhone 7) / iOS 15.8.2 | Dopamine + RootHide, app **not hidden** | **Detected**. JB Check: Jailbroken (conclusive: RootHide injector `roothideinit.dylib` loaded from the `.jbroot-*` path; indicator: `sileo://` URL scheme). Integrity Check: device Compromised 85, runtime Compromised 97, inline guard flags 2 |
| iPhone9,3 / iOS 15.8.2 | Dopamine + RootHide, app **hidden** (Blacklist) | **Missed**. JB Check: Not jailbroken, no evidence, no indicators. Integrity Check: device Trusted 0, runtime Trusted 0, inline guard clean. An earlier 1.3.1 run read Suspicious; that did not reproduce in these runs |
| Lab device (model and iOS not yet recorded) | TrollStore installed with TrollInstallerX | **Detected** by both apps |
| Stock device / iOS 26 | Not jailbroken | **Clean**, no false positive |
| Stock iPhone18,1 / iOS 27.2.1 | Not jailbroken | **Clean** in 1.3.1 (1.3.0 had a dyld false positive, fixed). JB Check: Not jailbroken; Integrity Check: Trusted 0 |

Not yet tested: palera1n (rootful and rootless), Dopamine on A12+, frida-server and FridaGadget, Shadow-class hiding tweaks, iOS 16 to 25. Result JSON for the runs above has not yet been exported into `Lab/results/`, so the hidden-case miss is recorded from screenshots only. The 1.4.0 changes (unreleased) have not been run on any device yet. The full matrix of implemented, unit-tested and device-validated detection is in [Lab/COVERAGE.md](Lab/COVERAGE.md).

"Not jailbroken" means no conclusive or indirect evidence was observed by the app. It is not proof that the device is stock.

### Evidence: Dopamine + RootHide, iPhone9,3, iOS 15.8.2

The same two apps were run with RootHide's Blacklist toggles off (not hidden) and on (hidden). Dopamine itself stayed off the Blacklist.

| Blacklist off (not hidden) | Blacklist on (hidden) |
|---|---|
| <img src="docs/images/lab/roothide-blacklist-off.png" width="240" alt="RootHide Blacklist with Kaisel Integrity Check and Kaisel JB Check toggled off"> | <img src="docs/images/lab/roothide-blacklist-on.png" width="240" alt="RootHide Blacklist with Kaisel Integrity Check and Kaisel JB Check toggled on"> |

| | Not hidden: **detected** | Hidden: **missed** |
|---|---|---|
| **JB Check** | <img src="docs/images/lab/jb-check_iphone9-3_ios15.8.2_roothide-unhidden.png" width="240" alt="JB Check reporting Jailbroken, roothideinit.dylib loaded, sileo URL scheme"> | <img src="docs/images/lab/jb-check_iphone9-3_ios15.8.2_roothide-hidden.png" width="240" alt="JB Check reporting Not jailbroken with no evidence or indicators"> |
| **Integrity Check** | <img src="docs/images/lab/integrity-check_iphone9-3_ios15.8.2_roothide-unhidden.png" width="240" alt="Integrity Check reporting device Compromised 85 and runtime Compromised 97"> | <img src="docs/images/lab/integrity-check_iphone9-3_ios15.8.2_roothide-hidden.png" width="240" alt="Integrity Check reporting device Trusted 0 and runtime Trusted 0"> |

Unhidden, RootHide still injects `roothideinit.dylib` into the app, and that single image is enough for a conclusive verdict. Hidden, nothing is injected, the jailbreak root sits at a random path, the package-manager URL schemes are filtered, and none of the 1.3 residual signals (`dyldModified`, `userspaceReboot`, `previouslyJailbroken`) fired. JB Check also showed no **Reset jailbreak history** button in the hidden run, so the Keychain marker was not seen there.

### Evidence: stock iPhone18,1, iOS 27.2.1 (not jailbroken)

| JB Check | Integrity Check |
|---|---|
| <img src="docs/images/lab/jb-check_iphone18-1_ios27.2.1_stock.png" width="240" alt="JB Check on a stock iPhone18,1 reporting Not jailbroken"> | <img src="docs/images/lab/integrity-check_iphone18-1_ios27.2.1_stock.png" width="240" alt="Integrity Check on a stock iPhone18,1 reporting Trusted 0"> |

No false positive: no evidence or indicators, and device and runtime both Trusted 0 with Developer Mode on.

## Quick start

```bash
brew install xcodegen
git clone https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel.git
cd Kaisel/Apps && xcodegen generate && open Kaisel.xcodeproj
```

Pick the `KaiselJBCheck` or `KaiselIntegrityCheck` scheme, choose your team under **Signing & Capabilities**, and run on a real device.

**No Mac?** Download an unsigned IPA from [Releases](https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel/releases) and install it with Impactor or Sideloadly: see [Sideloading](docs/SIDELOADING.md). Use 1.3.1 or later; the 1.3.0 release has a false positive on stock iOS 16 and later.

## Requirements

| | |
|---|---|
| Devices | iPhone or iPad on iOS 15.0 to 27 (the Simulator is detected and flagged) |
| Build | macOS 14+, Xcode 15+ (Swift 5.9), XcodeGen |
| Signing | Free Apple ID for both apps; paid team only for the App Attest variant |
| Server (optional) | Python 3.10+ |

## Installation options

| Method | Needs a Mac | Account | Guide |
|---|---|---|---|
| Xcode, run on device | Yes | Free or paid | [Building › Option A](docs/BUILDING.md#option-a-xcode-run-directly-on-your-phone) |
| Signed IPA (`Scripts/build_ipa.sh`) | Yes | Free or paid | [Building › Option B](docs/BUILDING.md#option-b-signed-ipa-from-the-command-line) |
| Unsigned IPA + Impactor / Sideloadly | No (with Releases) | Free | [Sideloading](docs/SIDELOADING.md) |

A free Apple ID signs apps for **7 days**; reinstall to renew. See [free account limits](docs/SIDELOADING.md#free-apple-id-limits).

## Using the package

```swift
// Package.swift
.package(url: "https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel.git", from: "1.3.1")
.product(name: "KaiselKit", package: "Kaisel")
```

```swift
import KaiselKit

// Jailbreak only
let result = await JailbreakDetector().check()
print(result.status)                 // .jailbroken / .suspicious / .notJailbroken

// Full assessment, sealed for your backend
let kit = IntegrityKit(configuration: .init(expectedDistribution: .appStore,
                                            expectedTeamIdentifier: "ABCDE12345"))
let report = await kit.evaluate()
let sealed = try await kit.seal(report, serverNonce: nonceFromServer)
// send `sealed` to your server; the server decides

// Clear the "seen jailbroken" Keychain marker (for example after a device is restored)
JailbreakHistory.reset()
```

Full guide: [Integration](docs/INTEGRATION.md).

## Honest limitations

Any check running on a device the attacker controls can be bypassed. Kaisel makes bypassing more expensive and more visible, and gives your server verifiable evidence. It is not proof.

- **Per-app hiding is currently a miss.** With the app in RootHide's Blacklist both apps read clean (Not jailbroken, Trusted 0; see the screenshots above): the app runs with nothing injected, the jailbreak root is at a random path and the URL schemes are filtered. An earlier run read *Suspicious*, but it did not reproduce. Treat "Not jailbroken" as *no evidence*, not as proof of a stock device, and keep the decision on the server: its per-key history targets the "run once, then hide" pattern but has not yet been lab-verified.
- **dyld integrity is an indicator, not proof.** From iOS 16 dyld runs from its copy in the shared cache, which differs from `/usr/lib/dyld` on any stock phone. Since 1.3.1 in-cache dyld is skipped, large differences are ignored, and `dyldModified` can never make a device "Jailbroken" on its own. In practice it only has a chance on iOS 15.
- **Kernel-level hiding** defeats filesystem and code-signing checks, because the kernel itself answers. In-process signals (images, hooks, threads, memory, entitlements) still apply.
- **Apple has no "this device is not jailbroken" attestation.** App Attest proves a genuine app on real hardware, not a stock OS.
- **The server cannot prove the checks ran.** Check-completion bits are reported by the app. The reference server requires every expected check before `allow`, but App Attest only proves which app instance signed the report, not that measurements inside a compromised process are true.
- **In-bundle code is classified by name only.** A renamed library injected into a re-signed app's bundle is not caught by image classification; App Attest, team ID and `__TEXT` hash checks are the backstops.
- **Coverage is limited to the runs listed above.** Every other expectation in [`Lab/expected.json`](Lab/expected.json) is a hypothesis until tested. Please contribute lab results.

Read [Limitations](docs/LIMITATIONS.md) before relying on Kaisel for any security decision. **Never gate anything security-critical on the client-side verdict alone.**

## Roadmap

Candidate improvements from the gap analysis in [Pentesting iOS RASP § 11](docs/Pentesting%20iOS%20RASP.md#11-proposed-kaisel-tweaks). None is implemented yet; each will be validated on stock iOS 15 to 27 before it is scored. Per-app hiding is the priority, because the RootHide Blacklist case is a miss today.

| Area | Item |
|---|---|
| Quick wins | Hardware breakpoint / watchpoint detection from debug registers · full code-signing flag profile · kernel task-port probe for older jailbreaks · Apple-Silicon-Mac and virtual-device signals · report to the server by default rather than blocking locally |
| Hidden and renamed jailbreaks (priority) | Re-test the 1.3 residual signals hidden, with result JSON exported, to see why none fired · mount-table enumeration · build manifest of load commands and bundled frameworks (catches a renamed Frida Gadget inside the app bundle) · DeviceCheck "seen jailbroken" bits in the reference server |
| Instrumentation | Mach-O header scan of anonymous executable memory · Kaisel self-`__TEXT` hash · new-thread inventory |
| Server | `AppTransaction` (StoreKit 2) verification · transaction-bound nonces · App Attest receipt risk metric · report consistency checks |
| Research (lab only first) | Sandbox-policy probes · boot-session timeline per key · sysctl surface fingerprint |

## Responsible use

Kaisel is for defensive security, research and testing that you are **authorised** to perform: protecting your own apps, assessing devices you own or manage, and engagements with written permission. Do not use it to evade other apps' protections or against devices or users without consent. See [SECURITY.md](SECURITY.md) to report vulnerabilities.

## Contributing

Contributions are welcome, especially lab results from jailbroken devices and new indicators. See [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md).

Contact: [0xdhanesh](https://github.com/0xdhanesh) · dhanesh.professional@gmail.com

`SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Security policy

## Supported versions

| Version | Supported |
|---|---|
| 1.3.x | Yes |
| < 1.3 | No |

## Reporting a vulnerability

Please **do not** open a public issue for a vulnerability.

- Preferred: [report privately through GitHub](https://github.com/0xdhanesh/Kaisel/security/advisories/new).
- Or email **dhanesh.professional@gmail.com** with the subject `Kaisel security`.

Include the affected version, the iOS version and device, steps to reproduce and the impact.
You will get an acknowledgement within 5 working days. Fixes are coordinated with the reporter, and
reporters are credited in the release notes unless they prefer otherwise.

## What counts

In scope:

- Flaws that let a forged or replayed report pass the reference server's verification.
- Signature, anti-rollback or bounds bypasses in signed remote rules.
- Memory-safety bugs in the C core.
- False positives that mark a **stock** device as jailbroken (please include the result JSON).

Generally **not** treated as vulnerabilities, but welcome as issues or pull requests:

- A new jailbreak, hiding tweak or Frida technique that is not detected. On-device detection is
  bypassable by design; see [Limitations](docs/LIMITATIONS.md). Use the *Detection gap* issue template.
- Issues in the reference server's deliberate simplifications (no TLS, no authentication, JSON storage).

## Handling sensitive data

Result JSON can contain device model, iOS version and file paths. Redact anything that identifies a
person or organisation before attaching it to an issue.

```

`Scripts/build_ipa.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build a signed .ipa with your own Apple developer team.
#
#   Scripts/build_ipa.sh --team ABCDE12345 [--scheme KaiselJBCheck]
#                        [--method development|ad-hoc] [--bundle-prefix com.you.kaisel]
#
# Schemes: KaiselJBCheck (default), KaiselIntegrityCheck, KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest.
# A free Apple ID (Personal Team) can build KaiselJBCheck and KaiselIntegrityCheck.
# KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest needs a paid team (App Attest entitlement).
# Output: build/ipa/<Scheme>.ipa
set -euo pipefail

TEAM="" SCHEME="KaiselJBCheck" METHOD="development" PREFIX=""
usage() { sed -n '2,10p' "$0" | sed 's/^# \{0,1\}//'; exit "${1:-0}"; }
while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
  case "$1" in
    --team) TEAM="$2"; shift 2 ;;
    --scheme) SCHEME="$2"; shift 2 ;;
    --method) METHOD="$2"; shift 2 ;;
    --bundle-prefix) PREFIX="$2"; shift 2 ;;
    -h|--help) usage 0 ;;
    *) echo "Unknown option: $1" >&2; usage 1 ;;
  esac
done
[[ -n "$TEAM" ]] || { echo "error: --team is required (Xcode > Settings > Accounts shows your Team ID)" >&2; exit 1; }
case "$SCHEME" in
  KaiselJBCheck|KaiselIntegrityCheck) ;;
  KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest) echo "note: $SCHEME needs a paid developer team; Personal Teams cannot sign App Attest." ;;
  *) echo "error: unknown scheme $SCHEME" >&2; exit 1 ;;
esac
case "$METHOD" in development|ad-hoc) ;; *) echo "error: --method must be development or ad-hoc" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
OUT="$ROOT/build"
mkdir -p "$OUT/ipa"
command -v xcodegen >/dev/null || { echo "error: install XcodeGen first: brew install xcodegen" >&2; exit 1; }
(cd "$ROOT/Apps" && xcodegen generate --quiet)

EXTRA=()
[[ -n "$PREFIX" ]] && EXTRA+=("KAISEL_BUNDLE_PREFIX=$PREFIX")
sed "s/YOUR_TEAM_ID/$TEAM/" "$ROOT/Apps/ExportOptions/$METHOD.plist" > "$OUT/export-$METHOD.plist"

xcodebuild -project "$ROOT/Apps/Kaisel.xcodeproj" -scheme "$SCHEME" -configuration Release \
  -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' -archivePath "$OUT/$SCHEME.xcarchive" \
  DEVELOPMENT_TEAM="$TEAM" CODE_SIGN_STYLE=Automatic ${EXTRA[@]+"${EXTRA[@]}"} \
  -allowProvisioningUpdates archive
xcodebuild -exportArchive -archivePath "$OUT/$SCHEME.xcarchive" \
  -exportOptionsPlist "$OUT/export-$METHOD.plist" -exportPath "$OUT/ipa" -allowProvisioningUpdates

echo "IPA: $OUT/ipa/$SCHEME.ipa"

```

`Scripts/build_unsigned_ipa.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build unsigned .ipa files for sideloading tools (Impactor, Sideloadly, AltStore),
# which re-sign the app with your Apple ID on install. No developer account needed
# to build; Xcode and XcodeGen are required.
#
#   Scripts/build_unsigned_ipa.sh                 # both free-account apps
#   Scripts/build_unsigned_ipa.sh KaiselJBCheck   # one app
#
# Output: dist/<Scheme>-<version>-unsigned.ipa
# KaiselIntegrityCheckAttest is not offered: App Attest needs a paid team's
# provisioning profile, which a sideloading tool cannot add.
set -euo pipefail

ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
SCHEMES=("$@")
[[ ${#SCHEMES[@]} -gt 0 ]] || SCHEMES=(KaiselJBCheck KaiselIntegrityCheck)
VERSION="$(sed -n 's/^ *MARKETING_VERSION: *"\{0,1\}\([^"]*\)"\{0,1\}$/\1/p' "$ROOT/Apps/project.yml" | head -1)"
BUILD="$ROOT/build/unsigned"
DIST="$ROOT/dist"
mkdir -p "$BUILD" "$DIST"

command -v xcodegen >/dev/null || { echo "error: install XcodeGen first: brew install xcodegen" >&2; exit 1; }
(cd "$ROOT/Apps" && xcodegen generate --quiet)

for SCHEME in "${SCHEMES[@]}"; do
  case "$SCHEME" in
    KaiselJBCheck|KaiselIntegrityCheck) ;;
    *) echo "error: $SCHEME cannot be built unsigned (use KaiselJBCheck or KaiselIntegrityCheck)" >&2; exit 1 ;;
  esac
  ARCHIVE="$BUILD/$SCHEME.xcarchive"
  rm -rf "$ARCHIVE" "$BUILD/Payload"
  xcodebuild -project "$ROOT/Apps/Kaisel.xcodeproj" -scheme "$SCHEME" -configuration Release \
    -destination 'generic/platform=iOS' -archivePath "$ARCHIVE" \
    CODE_SIGNING_ALLOWED=NO CODE_SIGNING_REQUIRED=NO CODE_SIGN_IDENTITY="" VALIDATE_PRODUCT=NO \
    archive | if command -v xcpretty >/dev/null; then xcpretty; else cat; fi
  APP="$(find "$ARCHIVE/Products/Applications" -maxdepth 1 -name '*.app' | head -1)"
  [[ -d "$APP" ]] || { echo "error: no .app in $ARCHIVE" >&2; exit 1; }
  mkdir -p "$BUILD/Payload"
  cp -R "$APP" "$BUILD/Payload/"
  IPA="$DIST/$SCHEME-$VERSION-unsigned.ipa"
  rm -f "$IPA"
  (cd "$BUILD" && zip -qry "$IPA" Payload)
  rm -rf "$BUILD/Payload"
  echo "IPA: $IPA"
done
(cd "$DIST" && shasum -a 256 ./*-unsigned.ipa > SHA256SUMS.txt)
echo "Checksums: $DIST/SHA256SUMS.txt"

```

`Scripts/gen_strings.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Generates Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_strings_table.h

Every indicator string (paths, image names, thread names, env vars, URL schemes,
entitlement keys) is stored XOR-encoded with a per-string rolling key so the
compiled binary carries no plaintext indicator list for `strings`/`grep` or a
bypass tweak to harvest. Strings are decoded onto the stack only for the duration
of a single check and wiped afterwards.

This is obfuscation, not encryption: it raises the cost of static analysis and of
generic "hide these paths" tweaks, it does not stop a determined reverser.

Re-run after editing the lists:  python3 Scripts/gen_strings.py
Reproducible output (tests, CI):  RASP_SEED=1 python3 Scripts/gen_strings.py
Check the committed table:        python3 Scripts/gen_strings.py --verify
"""
import json
import os
import random
import sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, ".."))
MANIFEST = os.path.join(ROOT, "Server", "classification.json")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Indicator lists. Keep generic and public: these are all well-known artefacts.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
LISTS = {
    # Files and directories created by jailbreaks, package managers and tools.
    # Covers rootful (unc0ver, checkra1n, palera1n rootful, Taurine) and
    # rootless (Dopamine, palera1n rootless, XinaA15) layouts.
    "JB_PATHS": [
        "/Applications/Cydia.app",
        "/Applications/Sileo.app",
        "/Applications/Zebra.app",
        "/Applications/Filza.app",
        "/Library/MobileSubstrate/MobileSubstrate.dylib",
        "/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries",
        "/usr/lib/libsubstrate.dylib",
        "/usr/lib/substrate",
        "/usr/lib/TweakInject",
        "/usr/lib/libhooker.dylib",
        "/usr/lib/libsubstitute.dylib",
        "/usr/lib/ellekit",
        "/usr/libexec/cydia",
        "/usr/libexec/sftp-server",
        "/usr/sbin/sshd",
        "/usr/bin/ssh",
        "/usr/bin/apt",
        "/bin/bash",
        "/bin/sh",
        "/etc/apt",
        "/etc/ssh/sshd_config",
        "/var/lib/apt",
        "/var/lib/cydia",
        "/var/cache/apt",
        "/var/log/apt",
        "/private/var/lib/dpkg",
        "/.bootstrapped",
        "/.installed_unc0ver",
        "/.installed_dopamine",
        "/.procursus_strapped",
        "/.mount_rw",
        "/var/checkra1n.dmg",
        "/cores/binpack",
        "/cores/jbloader",
        "/var/binpack",
        "/var/jb",
        "/var/jb/basebin",
        "/var/jb/.installed_dopamine",
        "/var/jb/.procursus_strapped",
        "/var/jb/usr/bin/su",
        "/var/jb/usr/lib/TweakInject",
        "/var/jb/usr/lib/ellekit",
        "/var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate",
        "/var/jb/Applications/Sileo.app",
        "/var/jb/Applications/Zebra.app",
        "/var/containers/Bundle/tweaksupport",
        "/private/preboot/jb",
        "/var/mobile/Library/palera1n",
        "/usr/share/jailbreak/injectme.plist",
    ],
    # Top-level system directories that rootful jailbreaks historically
    # relocated and replaced with symlinks (stashing).
    "SYMLINK_DIRS": [
        "/Applications",
        "/Library/Ringtones",
        "/Library/Wallpaper",
        "/usr/arm-apple-darwin9",
        "/usr/include",
        "/usr/libexec",
        "/usr/share",
    ],
    # Loaded-image classification lists come from Server/classification.json
    # (see load_image_lists below), so the C core, the server and the parity
    # tests share one definition.
    # Thread names used by Frida's runtime (gum / GLib main loop / D-Bus).
    "FRIDA_THREADS": [
        "gum-js-loop",
        "gmain",
        "gdbus",
        "pool-frida",
        "frida",
        "pool-spawner",
        "linjector",
    ],
    # Byte markers searched in anonymous executable/readable regions.
    "FRIDA_MEM_MARKERS": [
        "frida_agent_main",
        "LIBFRIDA",
        "GumScript",
        "frida:rpc",
    ],
    # Environment variables.
    "ENV_INJECTION": [
        "DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES",
        "DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH",
        "DYLD_FRAMEWORK_PATH",
        "DYLD_FALLBACK_LIBRARY_PATH",
        "DYLD_IMAGE_SUFFIX",
    ],
    "ENV_JB": [
        "_MSSafeMode",
        "_SafeMode",
        "_SubstituteSafeMode",
        "JB_ROOT_PATH",
        "JB_SANDBOX_EXTENSIONS",
        "JB_BOOTSTRAP_PATH",
        "JBROOT",
    ],
    # Entitlements an App Store / enterprise app should never carry.
    # Their presence typically means sideloading via TrollStore or a re-sign
    # on a jailbroken device.
    "SUSPICIOUS_ENTITLEMENTS": [
        "com.apple.private.security.no-sandbox",
        "com.apple.private.security.container-required",
        "platform-application",
        "com.apple.private.skip-library-validation",
        "task_for_pid-allow",
        "com.apple.private.persona-mgmt",
        "com.apple.private.security.storage.AppDataContainers",
        "run-unsigned-code",
        "dynamic-codesigning",
        "com.apple.private.kernel.jetsam",
    ],
    # URL schemes registered by jailbreak package managers and tools.
    # The host app must list these under LSApplicationQueriesSchemes.
    # Audit rule: never list a scheme any stock Apple app can register.
    # "apple-magnifier" (used by TrollStore) was removed because the stock
    # Magnifier app owns it on current iOS, which caused a false positive.
    "URL_SCHEMES": [
        "cydia",
        "sileo",
        "zbra",
        "filza",
        "undecimus",
        "activator",
    ],
    # Developer disk image mount points (iOS <=16 and iOS 17+ personalised DDI).
    "DDI_PATHS": [
        "/Developer/usr/bin/debugserver",
        "/System/Developer/usr/bin/debugserver",
    ],
    # Misc single strings used by the C core.
    "MISC": [
        "security.mac.amfi.developer_mode_status",  # 0
        "com.apple.os.update-",                      # 1  sealed root snapshot prefix
        "/private/var/containers/Bundle/Application/",  # 2
        "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/",       # 3
        "libsystem_kernel.dylib",                    # 4
        "libsystem_c.dylib",                         # 5
        "libdyld.dylib",                             # 6
        "/usr/lib/system/",                          # 7
        "/System/",                                  # 8
        "/usr/lib/",                                 # 9
        "/private/preboot/Cryptexes/",               # 10
        "/Developer/",                               # 11
        "/System/Developer/",                        # 12
        "\0AUTH\r\n",                                # 13 D-Bus auth probe (length-prefixed)
        "REJECTED",                                  # 14 D-Bus reply
        "/private/",                                 # 15 sandbox write probe root
        "Foundation",                                # 16
        "UIKitCore",                                 # 17
        "libobjc.A.dylib",                           # 18
        "fork",                                      # 19
        "ptrace",                                    # 20
        "libdyld",                                   # 21 (dyld4 lives in /usr/lib/dyld)
        "/usr/lib/dyld",                             # 22
        "/private/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/",  # 23 sandbox read probe dir
        "/var/jb",                                   # 24 rootless jailbreak root symlink
        "kern.userspacereboottime",                  # 25 set by a userspace reboot (Dopamine does one)
    ],
    # Exported libc / libsystem functions whose prologue and binding we verify.
    # Prefixed "S:" means the function is a raw syscall stub that must contain
    # `svc #0x80` within its first instructions.
    "HOOK_TARGETS": [
        "S:stat",
        "S:lstat",
        "S:access",
        "S:csops",
        "S:getppid",
        "S:__sysctl",
        "S:readlink",
        "open",
        "fopen",
        "opendir",
        "getenv",
        "sysctl",
        "sysctlbyname",
        "ptrace",
        "fork",
        "dlopen",
        "dlsym",
        "dladdr",
        "_dyld_image_count",
        "_dyld_get_image_name",
        "_dyld_get_image_header",
        "task_info",
        "task_threads",
        "objc_msgSend",
        "NSClassFromString",
        "SecItemCopyMatching",
    ],
}



def load_image_lists() -> dict:
    """Image-classification lists from the shared manifest (1.4.0).

    IMAGE_INJECTOR_BASENAMES      exact basenames: conclusive outside the app bundle
    IMAGE_JB_ROOT_PREFIXES        structured jailbreak-root prefixes: conclusive
    IMAGE_JB_ROOT_COMPONENTS      path-component prefixes (RootHide .jbroot-*): conclusive
    IMAGE_INSTR_BASENAMES         exact basenames: runtime instrumentation, never a jailbreak on its own
    IMAGE_INSTR_PREFIXES          basename prefixes for instrumentation
    IMAGE_BROAD_MARKERS           broad substrings: indicator only, never conclusive
    IMAGE_TRUSTED_PREFIXES        system locations
    """
    with open(MANIFEST) as f:
        img = json.load(f)["images"]
    return {
        "IMAGE_INJECTOR_BASENAMES": img["jailbreakInjectorBasenames"],
        "IMAGE_JB_ROOT_PREFIXES": img["jailbreakRootPrefixes"],
        "IMAGE_JB_ROOT_COMPONENTS": img["jailbreakRootComponentPrefixes"],
        "IMAGE_INSTR_BASENAMES": img["instrumentationBasenames"],
        "IMAGE_INSTR_PREFIXES": img["instrumentationBasenamePrefixes"],
        "IMAGE_BROAD_MARKERS": img["broadMarkers"],
        "IMAGE_TRUSTED_PREFIXES": img["trustedPrefixes"],
    }


LISTS.update(load_image_lists())

HEADER = """// GENERATED by Scripts/gen_strings.py - do not edit by hand.
#ifndef RASP_STRINGS_TABLE_H
#define RASP_STRINGS_TABLE_H
#include <stdint.h>

typedef struct { uint32_t offset; uint16_t length; uint8_t key; uint8_t step; } rasp_obf_entry_t;
"""


def encode(s: bytes, key: int, step: int) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    k = key
    for b in s:
        out.append(b ^ k)
        k = (k + step) & 0xFF
    return bytes(out)


def decode_header(path: str) -> dict:
    """Decodes a generated header back into {list name: [strings]}.
    Independent of the random seed, so CI can prove the committed table matches LISTS."""
    import re
    text = open(path).read()
    ranges = {m.group(1): (int(m.group(2)), int(m.group(3))) for m in re.finditer(
        r"#define RASP_LIST_(\w+)_START (\d+)\n#define RASP_LIST_\1_COUNT (\d+)", text)}
    blob_txt = text[text.index("rasp_obf_blob[] = {"):text.index("};", text.index("rasp_obf_blob[] = {"))]
    blob = bytes(int(x, 16) for x in re.findall(r"0x([0-9a-f]{2})", blob_txt))
    tab_txt = text[text.index("rasp_obf_table[] = {"):]
    entries = [(int(a), int(b), int(c, 16), int(d, 16)) for a, b, c, d in re.findall(
        r"\{(\d+)u, (\d+)u, 0x([0-9a-f]{2})u, 0x([0-9a-f]{2})u\}", tab_txt)]
    out = {}
    for name, (start, count) in ranges.items():
        items = []
        for off, ln, key, step in entries[start:start + count]:
            items.append(encode(blob[off:off + ln], key, step).decode("utf-8"))   # XOR is symmetric
        out[name] = items
    return out


def verify() -> int:
    """Exit 1 if the committed header does not decode to exactly LISTS."""
    path = os.path.join(ROOT, "Sources", "CIntegrityCore", "rasp_strings_table.h")
    got = decode_header(path)
    bad = [n for n in LISTS if got.get(n) != LISTS[n]] + [n for n in got if n not in LISTS]
    if bad:
        print("rasp_strings_table.h is stale or edited by hand; re-run Scripts/gen_strings.py. Lists differing:",
              ", ".join(sorted(set(bad))))
        return 1
    print(f"rasp_strings_table.h matches the source lists ({sum(len(v) for v in LISTS.values())} strings)")
    return 0


def main():
    if "--verify" in sys.argv:
        sys.exit(verify())
    seed = os.environ.get("RASP_SEED")
    rng = random.Random(int(seed) if seed else None)
    blob = bytearray()
    entries = []
    list_ranges = {}
    for name, items in LISTS.items():
        start = len(entries)
        for item in items:
            raw = item.encode("utf-8")
            key = rng.randrange(1, 256)
            step = rng.randrange(1, 256) | 1
            enc = encode(raw, key, step)
            entries.append((len(blob), len(raw), key, step))
            blob += enc
            # random padding so string boundaries are not obvious
            blob += bytes(rng.randrange(0, 256) for _ in range(rng.randrange(1, 5)))
        list_ranges[name] = (start, len(items))

    lines = [HEADER]
    for name, (start, count) in list_ranges.items():
        lines.append(f"#define RASP_LIST_{name}_START {start}")
        lines.append(f"#define RASP_LIST_{name}_COUNT {count}")
    lines.append(f"#define RASP_OBF_ENTRY_COUNT {len(entries)}")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("static const uint8_t rasp_obf_blob[] = {")
    for i in range(0, len(blob), 16):
        lines.append("    " + ", ".join(f"0x{b:02x}" for b in blob[i:i + 16]) + ",")
    lines.append("};")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("static const rasp_obf_entry_t rasp_obf_table[] = {")
    for off, ln, key, step in entries:
        lines.append(f"    {{{off}u, {ln}u, 0x{key:02x}u, 0x{step:02x}u}},")
    lines.append("};")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("#endif")

    here = HERE
    out = os.path.join(here, "..", "Sources", "CIntegrityCore", "rasp_strings_table.h")
    with open(out, "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

    # Swift mirror of list ranges, so the Swift layer can decode URL schemes.
    swift_out = os.path.join(here, "..", "Sources", "KaiselKit", "ObfuscatedLists.swift")
    with open(swift_out, "w") as f:
        f.write("// GENERATED by Scripts/gen_strings.py - do not edit by hand.\n")
        f.write("enum ObfuscatedList {\n")
        for name, (start, count) in list_ranges.items():
            ident = "".join(p.capitalize() for p in name.lower().split("_"))
            ident = ident[0].lower() + ident[1:]
            f.write(f"    static let {ident}: Range<UInt16> = {start}..<{start + count}\n")
        f.write("}\n")
    print(f"wrote {len(entries)} strings, {len(blob)} bytes")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`Scripts/make_kaisel_icons.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Kaisel app icons: original stylised shadow-wyvern guardian artwork.

  pip install Pillow && python3 Scripts/make_kaisel_icons.py

Writes:
  Apps/KaiselJBCheck/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/AppIcon-1024.png
  Apps/KaiselIntegrityCheck/Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/AppIcon-1024.png
  Branding/KaiselJBCheck-1024.png, Branding/KaiselIntegrityCheck-1024.png
  Branding/KaiselIconPreview.png

A front-facing horned wyvern with glowing eyes looms over a shield, its wings
spread behind it. JB Check carries a padlock in violet; Integrity Check carries
a checkmark in ice blue. All shapes are drawn procedurally here (no third-party
artwork). Masters are 1024x1024, opaque RGB, square corners.
"""
import os
import sys
from PIL import Image, ImageChops, ImageDraw, ImageFilter

S = 4096
OUT = 1024
K = S / 1024.0          # design space is 1024; scale to the canvas
CX = 512
ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))


def lerp(a, b, t):
    return tuple(int(a[i] + (b[i] - a[i]) * t) for i in range(3))


def vertical_gradient(size, top, bottom):
    g = Image.new("RGB", (1, 256))
    for y in range(256):
        g.putpixel((0, y), lerp(top, bottom, y / 255))
    return g.resize(size, Image.BICUBIC)


def bezier(p0, p1, p2, p3, n=60):
    pts = []
    for i in range(n + 1):
        t = i / n
        u = 1 - t
        x = u**3 * p0[0] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[0] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[0] + t**3 * p3[0]
        y = u**3 * p0[1] + 3 * u * u * t * p1[1] + 3 * u * t * t * p2[1] + t**3 * p3[1]
        pts.append((x, y))
    return pts


def shield_path(cx, top, w, h):
    """Classic heater shield: gently arched top, straight sides, curved point."""
    l, r = cx - w / 2, cx + w / 2
    shoulder = top + h * 0.10
    side_end = top + h * 0.50
    bottom = top + h
    pts = []
    pts += bezier((l, shoulder), (cx - w * 0.25, top + h * 0.02), (cx - w * 0.08, top - h * 0.015), (cx, top))
    pts += bezier((cx, top), (cx + w * 0.08, top - h * 0.015), (cx + w * 0.25, top + h * 0.02), (r, shoulder))[1:]
    pts += [(r, side_end)]
    pts += bezier((r, side_end), (r, top + h * 0.78), (cx + w * 0.22, top + h * 0.92), (cx, bottom))[1:]
    pts += bezier((cx, bottom), (cx - w * 0.22, top + h * 0.92), (l, top + h * 0.78), (l, side_end))[1:]
    return pts


def scale_path(pts, cx, cy, k):
    return [(cx + (x - cx) * k, cy + (y - cy) * k) for x, y in pts]


def padlock(d, cx, cy, S, accent):
    white = (245, 248, 250)
    bw, bh = S * 0.20, S * 0.16
    body = [cx - bw / 2, cy - bh * 0.15, cx + bw / 2, cy + bh * 0.85]
    # shackle
    sw = S * 0.028
    r = bw * 0.34
    d.arc([cx - r, cy - bh * 0.15 - r * 1.55, cx + r, cy - bh * 0.15 + r * 0.45], 180, 360, fill=white, width=int(sw))
    d.line([cx - r + sw / 2 - 1, cy - bh * 0.15 - r * 0.55, cx - r + sw / 2 - 1, cy - bh * 0.12], fill=white, width=int(sw))
    d.line([cx + r - sw / 2, cy - bh * 0.15 - r * 0.55, cx + r - sw / 2, cy - bh * 0.12], fill=white, width=int(sw))
    d.rounded_rectangle(body, radius=S * 0.028, fill=white)
    # keyhole
    kr = S * 0.022
    ky = cy + bh * 0.28
    d.ellipse([cx - kr, ky - kr, cx + kr, ky + kr], fill=accent)
    d.polygon([(cx - kr * 0.55, ky), (cx + kr * 0.55, ky), (cx + kr * 0.35, ky + kr * 2.3), (cx - kr * 0.35, ky + kr * 2.3)], fill=accent)


def checkmark_with_pulse(d, cx, cy, S, accent):
    white = (245, 248, 250)
    w = int(S * 0.038)
    pts = [(cx - S * 0.105, cy + S * 0.005), (cx - S * 0.03, cy + S * 0.08), (cx + S * 0.12, cy - S * 0.085)]
    d.line(pts, fill=white, width=w, joint="curve")
    for p in (pts[0], pts[-1]):
        d.ellipse([p[0] - w / 2, p[1] - w / 2, p[0] + w / 2, p[1] + w / 2], fill=white)
    # scan arcs above
    for i, rr in enumerate((0.17, 0.215)):
        rad = S * rr
        d.arc([cx - rad, cy - rad - S * 0.02, cx + rad, cy + rad - S * 0.02], 205, 335,
              fill=lerp(white, accent, 0.35 + i * 0.25), width=int(S * 0.012))




def P(pts):
    return [(x * K, y * K) for x, y in pts]


def mirror(half):
    """half: right-side points (dx, y) from top centre to bottom centre."""
    right = [(CX + dx, y) for dx, y in half]
    left = [(CX - dx, y) for dx, y in reversed(half)]
    return right + left


# Right half of a front-facing horned wyvern head, (dx, y) in design units.
HEAD = [
    (0, 150),
    (30, 146),
    (58, 132),                              # brow ridge
    *bezier((58, 132), (100, 92), (160, 52), (230, 40), 14)[1:],      # swept horn
    *bezier((230, 40), (172, 78), (130, 118), (98, 160), 14)[1:],
    (136, 170), (102, 192),                 # frill spike
    (122, 228), (90, 236),                  # cheek spike
    (76, 272),
    (60, 320),
    (46, 366),                              # long snout
    (40, 398), (24, 410),                   # nostril flare
    (0, 416),
]

# Right wing, behind the shield.
WING = [
    (120, 360),
    *bezier((120, 360), (200, 260), (290, 210), (392, 236), 20)[1:],   # wing arm
    (420, 292),                                                         # wing tip claw
    *bezier((420, 292), (408, 380), (388, 470), (360, 560), 12)[1:],
    *bezier((360, 560), (338, 520), (312, 506), (290, 512), 8)[1:],    # membrane scallops
    *bezier((290, 512), (284, 580), (268, 640), (244, 690), 10)[1:],
    *bezier((244, 690), (226, 650), (204, 632), (180, 628), 8)[1:],
    *bezier((180, 628), (176, 680), (168, 720), (150, 760), 8)[1:],
    (130, 600),
]


def eye_polys():
    # angry slanted slits: inner end low, outer end high
    r = [(22, 214), (48, 199), (86, 184), (72, 207), (40, 218)]
    return [[(CX + dx, y) for dx, y in r], [(CX - dx, y) for dx, y in r]]


def mask_of(polys, blur=0.0):
    m = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(m)
    for p in polys:
        d.polygon(P(p), fill=255)
    if blur:
        m = m.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(blur * K))
    return m


def rim(mask, dx, dy, strength):
    """Edge light: the mask minus itself shifted, softened."""
    shifted = ImageChops.offset(mask, int(dx * K), int(dy * K))
    edge = ImageChops.subtract(mask, shifted)
    edge = edge.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(2.0 * K))
    return edge.point(lambda v: min(255, int(v * strength)))


def paint(img, colour, mask):
    return Image.composite(Image.new("RGB", (S, S), colour), img, mask)


def draw_icon(accent, accent_deep, eye, glyph):
    bg_top, bg_bottom = (14, 8, 26), (3, 2, 8)
    img = vertical_gradient((S, S), bg_top, bg_bottom)

    # shadow-smoke aura behind everything
    aura = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    ad = ImageDraw.Draw(aura)
    ad.ellipse(P([(170, 140), (854, 860)]), fill=120)
    ad.ellipse(P([(330, 60), (694, 420)]), fill=90)
    aura = aura.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(120 * K))
    img = paint(img, lerp(accent_deep, (0, 0, 0), 0.25), aura)

    shadow_fill = (9, 5, 16)

    # wings
    wings = [mirror_wing(WING, +1), mirror_wing(WING, -1)]
    wmask = mask_of(wings)
    img = paint(img, shadow_fill, wmask)
    img = paint(img, lerp(accent, (0, 0, 0), 0.35), rim(wmask, 0, 6, 1.4))
    # wing bones
    bones = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    bd = ImageDraw.Draw(bones)
    for sgn in (1, -1):
        root = (CX + sgn * 392, 238)            # wrist: finger bones fan out
        for tip in ((360, 560), (290, 512), (244, 690), (180, 628)):
            bd.line(P([root, (CX + sgn * tip[0], tip[1])]), fill=70, width=int(5 * K))
    bones = Image.composite(bones, Image.new("L", (S, S), 0), wmask)
    img = paint(img, lerp(accent, (0, 0, 0), 0.45), bones)

    # head (behind the shield top, in front of the wings)
    head = mirror(HEAD)
    hmask = mask_of([head])
    hglow = mask_of([head], blur=18).point(lambda v: v // 2)
    img = paint(img, accent_deep, hglow)
    img = paint(img, shadow_fill, hmask)
    img = paint(img, lerp(accent, (255, 255, 255), 0.1), rim(hmask, 0, 5, 1.8))
    img = paint(img, lerp(accent, (0, 0, 0), 0.2), rim(hmask, 4, -2, 1.0))

    # snout ridge
    ridge = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(ridge).line(P([(CX, 236), (CX, 404)]), fill=90, width=int(4 * K))
    img = paint(img, lerp(accent, (0, 0, 0), 0.3), ridge.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(2 * K)))

    # glowing eyes
    eyes = eye_polys()
    img = paint(img, eye, mask_of(eyes, blur=22))
    img = paint(img, eye, mask_of(eyes, blur=8))
    img = paint(img, lerp(eye, (255, 255, 255), 0.75), mask_of(eyes))

    # shield
    cx, top, w, h = S * 0.5, S * 0.425, S * 0.40, S * 0.47
    outer = shield_path(cx, top, w, h)
    sh = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(sh).polygon([(x, y + S * 0.015) for x, y in outer], fill=170)
    img = paint(img, (0, 0, 0), sh.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(S * 0.02)))
    smask = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(smask).polygon(outer, fill=255)
    sglow = smask.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(S * 0.02)).point(lambda v: v // 3)
    img = paint(img, accent, sglow)
    body = vertical_gradient((S, S), lerp(accent, (255, 255, 255), 0.15), accent_deep)
    img = Image.composite(body, img, smask)
    inner = scale_path(outer, cx, top + h * 0.5, 0.84)
    imask = Image.new("L", (S, S), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(imask).polygon(inner, fill=255)
    img = Image.composite(vertical_gradient((S, S), (22, 12, 38), (8, 4, 16)), img, imask)

    d = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    glyph(d, cx, top + h * 0.47, S * 0.68, accent)
    return img.resize((OUT, OUT), Image.LANCZOS).convert("RGB")


def mirror_wing(wing, sgn):
    return [(CX + sgn * dx, y) for dx, y in wing]


def squircle(icon, size):
    m = Image.new("L", (size * 4, size * 4), 0)
    ImageDraw.Draw(m).rounded_rectangle([0, 0, size * 4 - 1, size * 4 - 1], radius=int(size * 4 * 0.2237), fill=255)
    return icon.resize((size, size), Image.LANCZOS), m.resize((size, size), Image.LANCZOS)


def main():
    out = os.path.join(ROOT, "Branding")
    os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)
    jb = draw_icon((178, 96, 255), (88, 30, 160), (196, 130, 255), padlock)
    ic = draw_icon((96, 150, 255), (36, 60, 170), (120, 225, 255), checkmark_with_pulse)
    jb.save(os.path.join(out, "KaiselJBCheck-1024.png"), optimize=True)
    ic.save(os.path.join(out, "KaiselIntegrityCheck-1024.png"), optimize=True)
    for app, img in (("KaiselJBCheck", jb), ("KaiselIntegrityCheck", ic)):
        img.save(os.path.join(ROOT, "Apps", app, "Assets.xcassets/AppIcon.appiconset/AppIcon-1024.png"), optimize=True)

    pv = Image.new("RGB", (1400, 820), (242, 242, 247))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(pv)
    for i, (icon, label) in enumerate(((jb, "Kaisel JB Check"), (ic, "Kaisel Integrity Check"))):
        x0 = 80 + i * 680
        big, m = squircle(icon, 512)
        pv.paste(big, (x0, 50), m)
        for j, sz in enumerate((180, 120, 60)):
            small, sm = squircle(icon, sz)
            pv.paste(small, (x0 + [0, 210, 360][j], 600 + (180 - sz) // 2), sm)
        d.text((x0, 795), label, fill=(30, 30, 35))
    pv.save(os.path.join(out, "KaiselIconPreview.png"))
    print("icons written")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

`Server/appattest.py`:

```py
"""App Attest and Secure Enclave verification (server side).

Implements Apple's documented steps:
https://developer.apple.com/documentation/devicecheck/validating_apps_that_connect_to_your_server
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import cbor2
from cryptography import x509
from cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes, serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import ec

APP_ATTEST_NONCE_OID = x509.ObjectIdentifier("1.2.840.113635.100.8.2")
AAGUID_PRODUCTION = b"appattest" + b"\x00" * 7
AAGUID_DEVELOPMENT = b"appattestdevelop"


class AttestationError(Exception):
    pass


def sha256(b: bytes) -> bytes:
    return hashlib.sha256(b).digest()


@dataclass
class AuthData:
    rp_id_hash: bytes
    flags: int
    counter: int
    aaguid: bytes | None = None
    credential_id: bytes | None = None


def parse_auth_data(ad: bytes) -> AuthData:
    if len(ad) < 37:
        raise AttestationError("authenticatorData too short")
    out = AuthData(rp_id_hash=ad[:32], flags=ad[32], counter=int.from_bytes(ad[33:37], "big"))
    if out.flags & 0x40 and len(ad) >= 37 + 18:          # attested credential data present
        out.aaguid = ad[37:53]
        n = int.from_bytes(ad[53:55], "big")
        out.credential_id = ad[55:55 + n]
    return out


def _nonce_from_extension(cert: x509.Certificate) -> bytes:
    try:
        ext = cert.extensions.get_extension_for_oid(APP_ATTEST_NONCE_OID).value
    except x509.ExtensionNotFound as e:
        raise AttestationError("nonce extension missing") from e
    der = ext.value
    # SEQUENCE { [1] EXPLICIT { OCTET STRING (32) } }
    if len(der) < 2 + 2 + 2 + 32 or der[0] != 0x30 or der[-34] != 0x04 or der[-33] != 0x20:
        raise AttestationError("unexpected nonce extension encoding")
    return der[-32:]


def _check_validity(cert: x509.Certificate, now: datetime) -> None:
    nb = getattr(cert, "not_valid_before_utc", None) or cert.not_valid_before.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    na = getattr(cert, "not_valid_after_utc", None) or cert.not_valid_after.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    if not (nb <= now <= na):
        raise AttestationError("certificate outside validity period")


@dataclass
class AttestedKey:
    key_id: str
    public_key_pem: str
    environment: str          # "production" or "development"
    receipt: bytes


def verify_attestation(attestation_object: bytes, key_id_b64: str, challenge: bytes, app_id: str,
                       root_ca_pem: bytes, allow_development: bool = False,
                       now: datetime | None = None) -> AttestedKey:
    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    obj = cbor2.loads(attestation_object)
    if obj.get("fmt") != "apple-appattest":
        raise AttestationError("fmt is not apple-appattest")
    stmt = obj["attStmt"]
    x5c = stmt["x5c"]
    if len(x5c) < 2:
        raise AttestationError("x5c chain too short")
    cred = x509.load_der_x509_certificate(x5c[0])
    inter = x509.load_der_x509_certificate(x5c[1])
    root = x509.load_pem_x509_certificate(root_ca_pem)

    # 1. Chain: credCert <- intermediate <- Apple App Attest Root CA
    try:
        inter.verify_directly_issued_by(root)
        cred.verify_directly_issued_by(inter)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, InvalidSignature) as e:
        raise AttestationError(f"certificate chain invalid: {e}") from e
    for c in (cred, inter):
        _check_validity(c, now)

    # 2-4. nonce = SHA256(authData || SHA256(challenge)) must equal the cert extension
    auth_data: bytes = obj["authData"]
    nonce = sha256(auth_data + sha256(challenge))
    if _nonce_from_extension(cred) != nonce:
        raise AttestationError("nonce mismatch")

    # 5. SHA256(public key) == keyId
    pub = cred.public_key()
    if not isinstance(pub, ec.EllipticCurvePublicKey):
        raise AttestationError("credential key is not EC")
    raw = pub.public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.X962, serialization.PublicFormat.UncompressedPoint)
    key_id = base64.b64decode(key_id_b64)
    if sha256(raw) != key_id:
        raise AttestationError("keyId does not match credential public key")

    # 6-9. authenticator data
    ad = parse_auth_data(auth_data)
    if ad.rp_id_hash != sha256(app_id.encode()):
        raise AttestationError("RP ID hash does not match app ID")
    if ad.counter != 0:
        raise AttestationError("attestation counter must be 0")
    if ad.aaguid == AAGUID_PRODUCTION:
        env = "production"
    elif ad.aaguid == AAGUID_DEVELOPMENT and allow_development:
        env = "development"
    else:
        raise AttestationError("unexpected AAGUID (environment)")
    if ad.credential_id != key_id:
        raise AttestationError("credentialId does not match keyId")

    pem = pub.public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM, serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo).decode()
    return AttestedKey(key_id=key_id_b64, public_key_pem=pem, environment=env, receipt=stmt.get("receipt", b""))


def verify_assertion(assertion: bytes, client_data_hash: bytes, public_key_pem: str, app_id: str,
                     previous_counter: int) -> int:
    """Returns the new counter. Raises AttestationError on any failure."""
    obj = cbor2.loads(assertion)
    sig: bytes = obj["signature"]
    auth_data: bytes = obj["authenticatorData"]
    nonce = sha256(auth_data + client_data_hash)
    pub = serialization.load_pem_public_key(public_key_pem.encode())
    try:
        pub.verify(sig, nonce, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))
    except InvalidSignature as e:
        raise AttestationError("assertion signature invalid") from e
    ad = parse_auth_data(auth_data)
    if ad.rp_id_hash != sha256(app_id.encode()):
        raise AttestationError("assertion RP ID hash mismatch")
    if ad.counter <= previous_counter:
        raise AttestationError(f"assertion counter not increasing ({ad.counter} <= {previous_counter})")
    return ad.counter


def verify_secure_enclave(signature_der: bytes, public_key_x963: bytes, client_data_hash: bytes) -> None:
    """CryptoKit P256.Signing signature(for: data) = ECDSA-SHA256 over data."""
    pub = ec.EllipticCurvePublicKey.from_encoded_point(ec.SECP256R1(), public_key_x963)
    try:
        pub.verify(signature_der, client_data_hash, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))
    except InvalidSignature as e:
        raise AttestationError("Secure Enclave signature invalid") from e

```

`Server/classification.py`:

```py
"""Evidence classification shared with the SDK (Kaisel 1.4.0).

Loads Server/classification.json, the same manifest Scripts/gen_strings.py
compiles into the C core, and mirrors rasp_image_class() in
Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_classify.c. Tests/Fixtures/image_classification.json
is run against this module, the C classifier and the Swift wrapper, so the
three cannot drift apart silently.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
with open(os.path.join(HERE, "classification.json")) as _f:
    MANIFEST: dict = json.load(_f)

_IMG = MANIFEST["images"]
_INJECTOR = {s.lower() for s in _IMG["jailbreakInjectorBasenames"]}
_JB_PREFIXES = list(_IMG["jailbreakRootPrefixes"])
_JB_COMPONENTS = [s.lower() for s in _IMG["jailbreakRootComponentPrefixes"]]
_INSTR = {s.lower() for s in _IMG["instrumentationBasenames"]}
_INSTR_PREFIXES = [s.lower() for s in _IMG["instrumentationBasenamePrefixes"]]
_MARKERS = [s.lower() for s in _IMG["broadMarkers"]]
_TRUSTED = list(_IMG["trustedPrefixes"])

TRUSTED = "trusted"
JAILBREAK_INJECTOR = "jailbreakInjector"
INSTRUMENTATION = "instrumentation"
MARKER = "marker"
UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL = "unknownExternal"
UNKNOWN = "unknown"

CLASS_CODE = {JAILBREAK_INJECTOR: 200, INSTRUMENTATION: 205, MARKER: 206, UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL: 201}


def normalise(path: str) -> str:
    if path.startswith("/private/var/") or path.startswith("/private/preboot/"):
        return path[len("/private"):]
    return path


def within(path: str, directory: str) -> bool:
    """Directory-boundary prefix test on normalised paths."""
    if not path or not directory:
        return False
    p, d = normalise(path), normalise(directory)
    while len(d) > 1 and d.endswith("/"):
        d = d[:-1]
    if not p.startswith(d):
        return False
    return len(p) == len(d) or p[len(d)] == "/"


def _has_app_component(p: str) -> bool:
    parts = p.split("/")[:-1]          # directory components only
    return any(len(c) > 4 and c.lower().endswith(".app") for c in parts)


def classify_image(raw_path: str | None, bundle: str | None = None, simulator: bool = False,
                   remote_markers: tuple | list = ()) -> str:
    if not raw_path or "/" not in raw_path:
        return UNKNOWN
    p = normalise(raw_path[raw_path.index("/"):])
    base = p.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]
    if not base or len(p) >= 1024:
        return UNKNOWN
    b = base.lower()
    inside = within(p, bundle) if bundle else _has_app_component(p)

    if b in _INSTR or any(b.startswith(x) for x in _INSTR_PREFIXES):
        return INSTRUMENTATION
    injector = b in _INJECTOR
    if injector and inside:
        return INSTRUMENTATION
    if not inside and (injector or any(within(p, x) for x in _JB_PREFIXES)
                       or any(c.lower().startswith(x) for c in p.split("/") for x in _JB_COMPONENTS)):
        return JAILBREAK_INJECTOR
    if inside or simulator:
        return TRUSTED
    if any(within(p, x) for x in _TRUSTED):
        return TRUSTED
    if any(m in b for m in _MARKERS) or any(m.lower() in b for m in remote_markers if m):
        return MARKER
    return UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL


def image_code(raw_path: str | None, bundle: str | None = None) -> int:
    return CLASS_CODE.get(classify_image(raw_path, bundle), 0)

```

`Server/policy.py`:

```py
"""Server-side validation, re-scoring and decision. Mirrors RiskScorer.swift.

The server never trusts the client's verdict or its `informational` flags: it
re-derives both from finding codes, its own weights and its own policy.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if HERE not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
with open(os.path.join(HERE, "weights.json")) as _f:
    CODES: dict[str, dict] = json.load(_f)

import classification as cls  # noqa: E402  (shared manifest, Server/classification.json)

VERDICTS = ["trusted", "low", "elevated", "high", "compromised"]
DEBUGGER_CODES = {"400", "401", "402", "403", "302"}
APP_INFORMATIONAL_BY_DIST = {
    "enterprise": {"900", "603", "903"},
    "adHoc": {"900", "603"},
    "development": {"900", "603", "601"},
}
_NAME_TO_CODE = {m["name"]: c for c, m in CODES.items()}


def _codes(names) -> set:
    missing = [n for n in names if n not in _NAME_TO_CODE]
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(f"classification.json names unknown finding codes: {missing}")
    return {_NAME_TO_CODE[n] for n in names}


# Same rule as JailbreakEvidence in the SDK (both are checked against
# classification.json by Server/tests/test_parity.py): an explicit allow-list
# of evidence a stock, sealed iOS cannot produce. An image is conclusive only
# as suspiciousImage (200) whose path still classifies as a known injector.
CONCLUSIVE = _codes(cls.MANIFEST["conclusiveCodes"])
REVALIDATE_IMAGE = _codes(cls.MANIFEST["conclusiveRequiresImageRevalidation"])
INDICATORS = _codes(cls.MANIFEST["indicatorCodes"])
CORROBORATING = _codes(cls.MANIFEST["corroboratingCodes"])


def is_conclusive(code: str, confidence: int, detail: str) -> bool:
    """`confidence` is accepted for API compatibility; the rule does not use it.

    1.4.0: replaces the 1.3.x negative rule "suspiciousImage or
    imageAddedAtRuntime whose detail does not mention Frida is an injector".
    """
    code = str(code)
    if code not in CONCLUSIVE:
        return False
    if code in REVALIDATE_IMAGE:
        return cls.classify_image(detail or "") == cls.JAILBREAK_INJECTOR
    return True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Report validation (1.4.0)
#
# The expected check set comes from the server's own SDK profile, never from
# the client. Completion bits are self-reported: App Attest proves which app
# instance signed the report, not that the measurements inside a possibly
# compromised process are true. A complete bitmap is therefore necessary for
# "allow", never sufficient on its own.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CHECK_NAMES = ["filesystem", "sandbox", "images", "hooks", "debugger", "frida", "codesign",
               "environment", "devmode", "canary", "selfIntegrity"]          # RASP_CHECK_* bit order
ALL_CHECKS = (1 << len(CHECK_NAMES)) - 1
SDK_PROFILES = {
    # sdkVersion: report schema and the checks IntegrityKit.evaluate() always runs.
    "1.4.0": {"schema": 2, "expectedChecks": ALL_CHECKS, "legacy": False},
    # 1.3.x sent no reportSchemaVersion. Accepted only in lab mode.
    "1.3.0": {"schema": 1, "expectedChecks": ALL_CHECKS, "legacy": True},
    "1.3.1": {"schema": 1, "expectedChecks": ALL_CHECKS, "legacy": True},
}
MAX_SIGNALS = 512
CHECK_SUPPRESSED = _NAME_TO_CODE["checkSuppressed"]


def _is_int(v) -> bool:
    return isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool)


def _check_names(mask: int) -> str:
    return ", ".join(n for i, n in enumerate(CHECK_NAMES) if mask & (1 << i)) or "none"


def validate_report(report, policy: "Policy") -> dict:
    """Structure and completeness checks run before any decision.

    Returns {"action": "allow" | "step_up" | "deny", "reasons": [...]}, where
    "allow" means only "no validation objection".
    """
    deny, step = [], []
    strict = policy.mode == "strict"
    if not isinstance(report, dict):
        return {"action": "deny", "reasons": ["report is not a JSON object"]}

    sdk = report.get("sdkVersion")
    schema = report.get("reportSchemaVersion")
    profile = SDK_PROFILES.get(sdk) if isinstance(sdk, str) else None
    if not isinstance(sdk, str):
        deny.append("sdkVersion missing or not a string")
    elif profile is None:
        deny.append(f"unsupported SDK version {sdk!r}")
    elif profile["legacy"]:
        if schema is not None:
            deny.append(f"SDK {sdk} cannot send reportSchemaVersion {schema!r}")
        elif strict:
            deny.append(f"legacy report format (SDK {sdk}) is accepted in lab mode only")
        else:
            step.append(f"legacy report format (SDK {sdk})")
    elif not _is_int(schema) or schema != profile["schema"]:
        deny.append(f"unsupported reportSchemaVersion {schema!r} for SDK {sdk}")

    signals = report.get("signals")
    codes_seen = set()
    if not isinstance(signals, list):
        deny.append("signals missing or not an array")
        signals = []
    elif len(signals) > MAX_SIGNALS:
        deny.append(f"too many signals ({len(signals)} > {MAX_SIGNALS})")
    for i, s in enumerate(signals[:MAX_SIGNALS]):
        if not isinstance(s, dict):
            deny.append(f"signal {i} is not an object")
            continue
        code, conf, detail = s.get("code"), s.get("confidence"), s.get("detail", "")
        if not _is_int(code) or not 0 <= code <= 0xFFFF:
            deny.append(f"signal {i}: code {code!r} is not a finding code")
            continue
        if not _is_int(conf) or not 0 <= conf <= 100:
            deny.append(f"signal {i}: confidence {conf!r} outside 0...100")
        if not isinstance(detail, str):
            deny.append(f"signal {i}: detail is not a string")
        if str(code) not in CODES:
            (deny if strict else step).append(f"unknown finding code {code}")
        codes_seen.add(str(code))

    done, expected = report.get("checksCompleted"), report.get("checksExpected")
    if not _is_int(done) or not _is_int(expected) or not 0 <= done <= 0xFFFFFFFF or not 0 <= expected <= 0xFFFFFFFF:
        deny.append("checksCompleted/checksExpected missing or not 32-bit unsigned integers")
    elif profile is not None:
        want = profile["expectedChecks"]
        if expected == 0:
            deny.append("checksExpected is 0")
        elif expected != want:
            deny.append(f"checksExpected {expected:#x} differs from the server profile {want:#x} for SDK {sdk}")
        if done & ~want:
            deny.append(f"checksCompleted has bits outside the profile ({done & ~want:#x})")
        if done == 0:
            deny.append("no checks completed (checksCompleted is 0)")
        missing = want & ~done
        if missing:
            step.append(f"checks incomplete: {_check_names(missing)}")
            if CHECK_SUPPRESSED not in codes_seen:
                step.append("incomplete checks reported without a checkSuppressed signal")
        elif CHECK_SUPPRESSED in codes_seen:
            deny.append("contradictory report: checkSuppressed with every check complete")

    action = "deny" if deny else "step_up" if step else "allow"
    return {"action": action, "reasons": deny + step}


@dataclass
class Policy:
    expected_distribution: str | None = "appStore"   # None = app domain informational
    # "strict": fail closed. App Attest is always required, the report must be
    # a supported schema with every expected check complete, and unknown
    # finding codes deny. "lab": explicitly permissive for test benches;
    # missing App Attest and legacy (1.3.x) reports give step_up, never allow.
    mode: str = "strict"
    # Lab mode only: strict mode requires App Attest whatever this says.
    require_app_attest: bool = True
    debugger_expected: bool = False
    damping: float = 0.5
    thresholds: tuple = (15, 35, 60, 85)
    hard_confidence_floor: int = 75
    weight_overrides: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.mode not in ("strict", "lab"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown policy mode {self.mode!r}")

    @property
    def app_attest_required(self) -> bool:
        return self.mode == "strict" or self.require_app_attest


def _informational(code: str, domain: str, policy: Policy) -> bool:
    if domain == "posture":
        return True
    if domain == "app":
        if policy.expected_distribution is None:
            return True
        return code in APP_INFORMATIONAL_BY_DIST.get(policy.expected_distribution, set())
    if domain == "runtime" and policy.debugger_expected and code in DEBUGGER_CODES:
        return True
    return False


def _verdict(score: int, hard: list[dict], policy: Policy) -> str:
    t = policy.thresholds
    v = 0 if score < t[0] else 1 if score < t[1] else 2 if score < t[2] else 3 if score < t[3] else 4
    if hard:
        v = max(v, 3)
    if len({h["category"] for h in hard}) >= 2:
        v = 4
    return VERDICTS[v]


def assess(signals: list[dict], policy: Policy) -> dict:
    scored: dict[str, list[dict]] = {"device": [], "runtime": [], "app": []}
    for s in signals:
        code = str(s.get("code"))
        meta = CODES.get(code)
        if not meta or _informational(code, meta["domain"], policy):
            continue
        conf = max(0, min(100, int(s.get("confidence", 0))))
        w = float(policy.weight_overrides.get(code, meta["weight"]))
        scored[meta["domain"]].append(dict(code=code, category=meta["category"], hard=meta["hard"],
                                           confidence=conf, ew=w * conf / 100.0, title=meta["title"],
                                           detail=s.get("detail", "")))
    # Anonymous exec memory needs corroboration (as in the SDK).
    rt = scored["runtime"]
    if not any(x["code"] in CORROBORATING for x in rt):
        scored["runtime"] = [x for x in rt if x["code"] != "503"]

    out = {}
    for domain, sigs in scored.items():
        by_cat: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
        for x in sigs:
            by_cat.setdefault(x["category"], []).append(x["ew"])
        survive = 1.0
        for ws in by_cat.values():
            cat_survive, factor = 1.0, 1.0
            for w in sorted(ws, reverse=True):
                cat_survive *= 1.0 - min(max(w * factor, 0.0), 1.0)
                factor *= policy.damping
            survive *= cat_survive          # 1 - p_cat == cat_survive
        score = round((1.0 - survive) * 100)
        hard = [x for x in sigs if x["hard"] and x["confidence"] >= policy.hard_confidence_floor]
        out[domain] = dict(score=score, verdict=_verdict(score, hard, policy),
                           signals=[{k: x[k] for k in ("code", "title", "detail", "confidence")} for x in sigs])
    # A conclusive jailbreak signal in any domain (a tweak injector is scored
    # under runtime) makes the device compromised, as in Kaisel JB Check.
    evidence = [x for d in ("device", "runtime") for x in scored[d]
                if is_conclusive(x["code"], x["confidence"], x["detail"])]
    out["device"]["jailbreakEvidence"] = [{k: x[k] for k in ("code", "title", "detail")} for x in evidence]
    if evidence:
        out["device"]["verdict"] = "compromised"
        out["device"]["score"] = max(out["device"]["score"], policy.thresholds[3])
    if policy.expected_distribution is None:
        out["app"]["verdict"] = "not_enforced"
    return out


def history_valid(entry) -> bool:
    """A history entry counts only if its recorded evidence is still conclusive
    under the current rule. Entries from 1.3.0 (plain strings, possibly from the
    dyld false positive on stock iOS 27) are ignored and overwritten, and since
    1.4.0 an image entry must still classify as a known jailbreak injector (a
    1.3.x entry for a broad-marker match such as a "Crane..." framework is ignored)."""
    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
        return False
    return any(is_conclusive(str(e.get("code")), 100, e.get("detail", "")) for e in entry.get("evidence", []))


def apply_history(assessment: dict, first_seen: str | None) -> list[str]:
    """Raises the device verdict to at least `high` when this device key was
    seen jailbroken before but the current report is clean (for example after
    the app was hidden with RootHide or Choicy). Returns extra reasons."""
    dev = assessment["device"]
    if not first_seen or dev["jailbreakEvidence"]:
        return []
    if VERDICTS.index(dev["verdict"]) < VERDICTS.index("high"):
        dev["verdict"] = "high"
    return [f"jailbreak previously seen for this device key ({first_seen})"]


def decide(assessment: dict, app_attest: str, policy: Policy,
           validation: dict | None = None) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    """Returns (decision, reasons). decision: allow | step_up | deny.

    `validation` is the result of validate_report(). Omitting it is only for
    unit tests of the scoring path; the server always passes it.
    """
    reasons = []
    rank = {v: i for i, v in enumerate(VERDICTS)}
    if validation is not None and validation["action"] == "deny":
        return "deny", ["report failed validation"] + validation["reasons"]
    if app_attest != "verified":
        reasons.append(f"App Attest {app_attest}")
        if policy.app_attest_required:
            return "deny", reasons + ["report origin cannot be trusted"]
    worst = "allow"
    for domain in ("device", "runtime", "app"):
        v = assessment[domain]["verdict"]
        if v == "not_enforced":
            continue
        if rank[v] >= rank["high"]:
            reasons.append(f"{domain} {v}")
            worst = "deny"
        elif rank[v] == rank["elevated"] and worst != "deny":
            reasons.append(f"{domain} {v}")
            worst = "step_up"
    if app_attest != "verified" and worst == "allow":
        worst = "step_up"
    if validation is not None and validation["action"] == "step_up":
        reasons += validation["reasons"]
        if worst == "allow":
            worst = "step_up"
    return worst, reasons

```

  "extraURLSchemes": [],
