`Server/rules_tool.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Detection-rules signing tool.

  python3 rules_tool.py genkey  --out keys/            # rules_private.pem, rules_public.pem
  python3 rules_tool.py sign    --key keys/rules_private.pem rules/rules.json   # writes rules/rules.sig
  python3 rules_tool.py verify  --pub keys/rules_public.pem rules/rules.json

Paste rules_public.pem into the app (RulesStore(publicKeyPEM:)). Keep the
private key offline / in your HSM or KMS; never ship it.
"""
import argparse
import base64
import json
import os
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes, serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import ec

REQUIRED = {"version": int, "issuedAt": str, "expiresAt": str, "extraJailbreakPaths": list,
            "extraImageMarkers": list, "extraURLSchemes": list, "extraFridaPorts": list, "weightOverrides": dict}


def validate(rules: dict) -> None:
    for k, t in REQUIRED.items():
        if not isinstance(rules.get(k), t):
            raise SystemExit(f"rules.{k} missing or not {t.__name__}")
    for k in ("issuedAt", "expiresAt"):
        datetime.fromisoformat(rules[k].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    if datetime.fromisoformat(rules["expiresAt"].replace("Z", "+00:00")) <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
        raise SystemExit("rules already expired")
    for p in rules["extraJailbreakPaths"]:
        if not p.startswith("/") or len(p) >= 256:
            raise SystemExit(f"bad path {p!r}")


def sign_bytes(private_pem: bytes, data: bytes) -> str:
    key = serialization.load_pem_private_key(private_pem, password=None)
    return base64.b64encode(key.sign(data, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))).decode()


def verify_bytes(public_pem: bytes, data: bytes, sig_b64: str) -> None:
    pub = serialization.load_pem_public_key(public_pem)
    pub.verify(base64.b64decode(sig_b64), data, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    sub = ap.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
    g = sub.add_parser("genkey"); g.add_argument("--out", default="keys")
    s = sub.add_parser("sign"); s.add_argument("--key", required=True); s.add_argument("rules")
    v = sub.add_parser("verify"); v.add_argument("--pub", required=True); v.add_argument("rules")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)

    if a.cmd == "genkey":
        os.makedirs(a.out, exist_ok=True)
        key = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())
        priv = key.private_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM, serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8,
                                 serialization.NoEncryption())
        pub = key.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM,
                                            serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo)
        with open(os.path.join(a.out, "rules_private.pem"), "wb") as f:
            f.write(priv)
        os.chmod(os.path.join(a.out, "rules_private.pem"), 0o600)
        with open(os.path.join(a.out, "rules_public.pem"), "wb") as f:
            f.write(pub)
        print(pub.decode())
    elif a.cmd == "sign":
        data = open(a.rules, "rb").read()
        validate(json.loads(data))
        sig = sign_bytes(open(a.key, "rb").read(), data)
        out = os.path.splitext(a.rules)[0] + ".sig"
        with open(out, "w") as f:
            f.write(sig + "\n")
        print(f"signed {a.rules} -> {out}")
    elif a.cmd == "verify":
        data = open(a.rules, "rb").read()
        sig = open(os.path.splitext(a.rules)[0] + ".sig").read().strip()
        verify_bytes(open(a.pub, "rb").read(), data, sig)
        print("signature OK")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`Server/server.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""KaiselKit reference server (stdlib HTTP + cryptography + cbor2).

NOT production code: single process, JSON-file storage, no auth, no TLS.
It shows the verification steps your real backend must implement.

  pip install -r requirements.txt
  curl -O https://www.apple.com/certificateauthority/Apple_App_Attestation_Root_CA.pem
  python3 server.py --app-id ABCDE12345.com.dhanesh.kaisel.integritycheck \
      --root-ca Apple_App_Attestation_Root_CA.pem --allow-development \
      --expected-distribution none --port 8080

Endpoints
  POST /v1/challenge                    -> {challengeId, challenge}
  POST /v1/attest  {challengeId, keyId, attestationObject}        -> {ok, environment}
  POST /v1/verify  {challengeId, sealed}                          -> decision

Modes: --mode strict (default) fails closed: App Attest required, supported
report schema, every expected check complete. --mode lab is permissive and
labelled as such in every response; never expose it to production traffic.
  GET  /v1/rules.json, /v1/rules.sig    signed detection rules
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import base64
import json
import os
import secrets
import threading
import time
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

import appattest
import policy as pol

CHALLENGE_TTL = 300


class Store:
    def __init__(self, path: str):
        self.path = path
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.challenges: dict[str, tuple[bytes, float]] = {}
        self.devices: dict = {}
        if os.path.exists(path):
            with open(path) as f:
                self.devices = json.load(f)

    def save(self):
        tmp = self.path + ".tmp"
        with open(tmp, "w") as f:
            json.dump(self.devices, f, indent=1)
        os.replace(tmp, self.path)

    def new_challenge(self) -> tuple[str, bytes]:
        cid, ch = secrets.token_urlsafe(16), secrets.token_bytes(32)
        with self.lock:
            now = time.time()
            self.challenges = {k: v for k, v in self.challenges.items() if v[1] > now}
            self.challenges[cid] = (ch, now + CHALLENGE_TTL)
        return cid, ch

    def take_challenge(self, cid: str) -> bytes:
        """Single use: removed on first read."""
        with self.lock:
            item = self.challenges.pop(cid, None)
        if not item or item[1] < time.time():
            raise ValueError("unknown or expired challenge")
        return item[0]


def b64(x) -> bytes:
    return base64.b64decode(x) if x else b""


def _deny(reason: str, **extra) -> dict:
    return {"decision": "deny", "reasons": [reason], "appAttest": "not checked", "secureEnclave": "not checked",
            "validation": {"action": "deny", "reasons": [reason]}, **extra}


def _policy(cfg) -> pol.Policy:
    # Older Cfg objects have no `mode`: --no-require-app-attest used to mean
    # "permissive", which is lab mode in 1.4.0.
    mode = getattr(cfg, "mode", None) or ("strict" if cfg.require_app_attest else "lab")
    return pol.Policy(expected_distribution=cfg.expected_distribution, mode=mode,
                      require_app_attest=cfg.require_app_attest, debugger_expected=cfg.debugger_expected)


def verify_sealed(sealed: dict, challenge: bytes, store: Store, cfg) -> dict:
    """Verifies one sealed report. Never raises for a bad report: tampering,
    a wrong challenge or a malformed payload return a structured `deny`."""
    policy = _policy(cfg)
    if not isinstance(sealed, dict):
        return _deny("sealed report is not a JSON object", mode=policy.mode)
    try:
        payload, nonce, cdh = b64(sealed.get("payload")), b64(sealed.get("nonce")), b64(sealed.get("clientDataHash"))
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return _deny("sealed report fields are not valid base64", mode=policy.mode)
    if not payload or nonce != challenge:
        return _deny("nonce is not the issued challenge", mode=policy.mode)
    if appattest.sha256(payload + nonce) != cdh:
        return _deny("clientDataHash != SHA256(payload || nonce): payload or hash tampered", mode=policy.mode)

    result = {"appAttest": "missing", "secureEnclave": "missing"}
    key_id = sealed.get("appAttestKeyId")
    device = store.devices.get(key_id) if isinstance(key_id, str) and not key_id.startswith("_") else None
    if sealed.get("appAttestAssertion"):
        if not device:
            result["appAttest"] = "unknown key (not enrolled)"
        else:
            try:
                device["counter"] = appattest.verify_assertion(
                    b64(sealed["appAttestAssertion"]), cdh, device["publicKeyPEM"], cfg.app_id, device["counter"])
                result["appAttest"] = "verified"
            except (appattest.AttestationError, ValueError, TypeError) as e:
                result["appAttest"] = f"failed: {e}"

    # A Secure Enclave signature proves possession of a key the client chose.
    # It is evidence of continuity only once bound to an App Attest-verified
    # instance; an unbound signature never authenticates the report.
    if sealed.get("secureEnclaveSignature") and sealed.get("secureEnclavePublicKey"):
        try:
            se_pub = b64(sealed["secureEnclavePublicKey"])
            appattest.verify_secure_enclave(b64(sealed["secureEnclaveSignature"]), se_pub, cdh)
            if device is not None and result["appAttest"] == "verified":
                bound = device.get("sePublicKey")
                if bound is None:
                    device["sePublicKey"] = base64.b64encode(se_pub).decode()   # bind on first verified use
                    result["secureEnclave"] = "verified (bound)"
                elif bound == base64.b64encode(se_pub).decode():
                    result["secureEnclave"] = "verified"
                else:
                    result["secureEnclave"] = "key changed"
            else:
                result["secureEnclave"] = "signature valid (unbound, not authentication)"
        except (appattest.AttestationError, ValueError, TypeError) as e:
            result["secureEnclave"] = f"failed: {e}"
    with store.lock:
        store.save()

    try:
        report = json.loads(payload)
    except ValueError:
        return _deny("payload is not JSON", mode=policy.mode, **result)
    validation = pol.validate_report(report, policy)
    signals = report.get("signals") if isinstance(report, dict) and isinstance(report.get("signals"), list) else []
    signals = [x for x in signals if isinstance(x, dict) and pol._is_int(x.get("code"))
               and pol._is_int(x.get("confidence"))]
    assessment = pol.assess(signals, policy)

    # Per-device history, keyed only by an App Attest-verified key: an
    # arbitrary self-generated key must not create or clear device history.
    # A 1.3.x entry keyed by a Secure Enclave key is still read when that key
    # is bound to this verified App Attest instance.
    extra_reasons: list = []
    if result["appAttest"] == "verified":
        hist_key = "aa:" + key_id
        legacy_keys = ["se:" + sealed["secureEnclavePublicKey"]] if result["secureEnclave"].startswith("verified") else []
        with store.lock:
            history = store.devices.setdefault("_history", {})
            evidence = assessment["device"]["jailbreakEvidence"]
            if evidence and not pol.history_valid(history.get(hist_key)):
                history[hist_key] = {"firstSeen": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
                                     "evidence": [{"code": e["code"], "detail": e["detail"]} for e in evidence]}
                store.save()
            elif not evidence:
                entry = next((history.get(k) for k in [hist_key] + legacy_keys if pol.history_valid(history.get(k))),
                             None)
                extra_reasons = pol.apply_history(assessment, entry["firstSeen"] if entry else None)
    decision, reasons = pol.decide(assessment, result["appAttest"], policy, validation)
    reasons += extra_reasons
    rep = report if isinstance(report, dict) else {}
    build = rep.get("build") if isinstance(rep.get("build"), dict) else {}
    return {"decision": decision, "reasons": reasons, "mode": policy.mode, "validation": validation, **result,
            "device": assessment["device"], "runtime": assessment["runtime"], "app": assessment["app"],
            "clientVerdict": rep.get("verdict"), "sdkVersion": rep.get("sdkVersion"),
            "rulesVersion": rep.get("rulesVersion"), "textSHA256": build.get("textSHA256")}


def make_handler(store: Store, cfg):
    class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        def _send(self, code: int, body, ctype="application/json"):
            data = body if isinstance(body, bytes) else json.dumps(body, indent=1).encode()
            self.send_response(code)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(data)

        def _json(self) -> dict:
            n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", "0"))
            if n > 2_000_000:
                raise ValueError("body too large")
            return json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")

        def do_GET(self):
            if self.path in ("/v1/rules.json", "/v1/rules.sig"):
                p = os.path.join(cfg.rules_dir, os.path.basename(self.path))
                if os.path.exists(p):
                    return self._send(200, open(p, "rb").read(),
                                      "application/json" if p.endswith(".json") else "text/plain")
            self._send(404, {"error": "not found"})

        def do_POST(self):
            try:
                if self.path == "/v1/challenge":
                    cid, ch = store.new_challenge()
                    return self._send(200, {"challengeId": cid, "challenge": base64.b64encode(ch).decode()})
                body = self._json()
                if self.path == "/v1/attest":
                    ch = store.take_challenge(body["challengeId"])
                    k = appattest.verify_attestation(b64(body["attestationObject"]), body["keyId"], ch, cfg.app_id,
                                                     cfg.root_ca, allow_development=cfg.allow_development)
                    with store.lock:
                        store.devices[k.key_id] = {"publicKeyPEM": k.public_key_pem, "counter": 0,
                                                   "environment": k.environment, "sePublicKey": None,
                                                   "enrolledAt": int(time.time())}
                        store.save()
                    return self._send(200, {"ok": True, "environment": k.environment})
                if self.path == "/v1/verify":
                    try:
                        ch = store.take_challenge(body["challengeId"])
                    except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as e:
                        return self._send(400, _deny(f"challenge rejected: {e}"))
                    return self._send(200, verify_sealed(body.get("sealed"), ch, store, cfg))
                self._send(404, {"error": "not found"})
            except (appattest.AttestationError, ValueError, KeyError) as e:
                self._send(400, {"error": str(e)})

        def log_message(self, fmt, *args):
            print("[server]", fmt % args)

    return H


def main(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--app-id", required=True, help="TEAMID.bundle.identifier")
    ap.add_argument("--root-ca", required=True, help="Apple_App_Attestation_Root_CA.pem")
    ap.add_argument("--allow-development", action="store_true", help="accept the App Attest development environment")
    ap.add_argument("--expected-distribution", default="appStore",
                    choices=["appStore", "enterprise", "adHoc", "development", "none"])
    ap.add_argument("--mode", choices=["strict", "lab"], default="strict",
                    help="strict (default): fail closed. lab: permissive test bench, never for production")
    ap.add_argument("--no-require-app-attest", action="store_true",
                    help="lab mode only: accept reports without App Attest as step_up (implies --mode lab)")
    ap.add_argument("--debugger-expected", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--rules-dir", default=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "rules"))
    ap.add_argument("--db", default="devices.json")
    ap.add_argument("--host", default="0.0.0.0")
    ap.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=8080)
    ap.add_argument("--clear-history", action="store_true",
                    help="forget all 'seen jailbroken' device keys (e.g. after a false positive), then serve")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)

    class Cfg:
        app_id = a.app_id
        root_ca = open(a.root_ca, "rb").read()
        allow_development = a.allow_development
        expected_distribution = None if a.expected_distribution == "none" else a.expected_distribution
        mode = "lab" if a.no_require_app_attest else a.mode
        require_app_attest = not a.no_require_app_attest
        debugger_expected = a.debugger_expected
        rules_dir = a.rules_dir

    store = Store(a.db)
    if a.clear_history:
        n = len(store.devices.pop("_history", {}) or {})
        store.save()
        print(f"cleared jailbreak history for {n} device key(s)")
    srv = ThreadingHTTPServer((a.host, a.port), make_handler(store, Cfg))
    print(f"listening on http://{a.host}:{a.port}  app-id={a.app_id}  mode={Cfg.mode}")
    if Cfg.mode == "lab":
        print("WARNING: lab mode is permissive (no App Attest requirement, legacy reports). Never use it in production.")
    srv.serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

`Server/tests/test_classification.py`:

```py
"""Loaded-image classification on the server (Kaisel 1.4.0, spec P0.1).

Runs the shared fixtures that the C core and the Swift SDK also run.
"""
import json
import os
import sys
import unittest

ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", ".."))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "Server"))
import classification as cls  # noqa: E402
import policy as pol  # noqa: E402

FIXTURES = json.load(open(os.path.join(ROOT, "Tests", "Fixtures", "image_classification.json")))["cases"]
P = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None)


class SharedFixtures(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_every_fixture(self):
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(FIXTURES), 30)
        for c in FIXTURES:
            with self.subTest(c["name"]):
                self.assertEqual(cls.classify_image(c["path"], c["bundle"], c["simulator"], c["remoteMarkers"]),
                                 c["expected"])


class AcceptanceTable(unittest.TestCase):
    """The spec's P0.1 acceptance table, through server scoring."""

    def jb(self, code, detail):
        return pol.assess([{"code": code, "confidence": 95, "detail": detail}], P)["device"]["jailbreakEvidence"]

    def test_benign_framework_names(self):
        for d in ("/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/CraneBank.app/Frameworks/CraneBank.framework/CraneBank",
                  "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/App.app/Frameworks/LibertyFinance.framework/LibertyFinance",
                  "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/App.app/Frameworks/ChoicyAnalytics.framework/ChoicyAnalytics"):
            for code in (200, 203, 206):
                with self.subTest(d=d, code=code):
                    self.assertEqual(self.jb(code, d), [])

    def test_roothide_injector_conclusive(self):
        d = "/private/var/containers/Bundle/Application/.jbroot-7351422000C774F3/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib"
        self.assertEqual(len(self.jb(200, d)), 1)
        self.assertEqual(pol.assess([{"code": 200, "confidence": 95, "detail": d}], P)["device"]["verdict"], "compromised")

    def test_substrate_and_ellekit_preserved(self):
        for d in ("/Library/MobileSubstrate/MobileSubstrate.dylib", "/var/jb/usr/lib/libellekit.dylib",
                  "/var/jb/usr/lib/TweakLoader.dylib", "/usr/lib/libsubstrate.dylib"):
            with self.subTest(d=d):
                self.assertEqual(len(self.jb(200, d)), 1)

    def test_frida_gadget_is_runtime_not_jailbreak(self):
        d = "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/App.app/Frameworks/FridaGadget.dylib"
        a = pol.assess([{"code": 205, "confidence": 95, "detail": d}], P)
        self.assertEqual(a["device"]["jailbreakEvidence"], [])
        self.assertIn(a["runtime"]["verdict"], ("high", "compromised"))
        # A 1.3.x client reporting the gadget as 200 is not a jailbreak either.
        self.assertEqual(self.jb(200, d), [])

    def test_unknown_external_image_is_indicator(self):
        d = "/var/mobile/Library/Caches/libfoo.dylib"
        self.assertEqual(cls.classify_image(d), cls.UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL)
        self.assertEqual(self.jb(201, d), [])
        self.assertEqual(self.jb(200, d), [])     # even if a client mislabels it

    def test_bundle_boundary(self):
        self.assertFalse(cls.within("/var/App.app2/X", "/var/App.app"))
        self.assertTrue(cls.within("/private/var/App.app/X", "/var/App.app"))
        self.assertTrue(cls.within("/var/App.app", "/var/App.app/"))

    def test_remote_marker_never_conclusive(self):
        self.assertEqual(cls.classify_image("/var/mobile/evilhook.dylib", remote_markers=["evilhook"]), cls.MARKER)
        self.assertEqual(self.jb(206, "/var/mobile/evilhook.dylib"), [])

    def test_runtime_image_no_longer_conclusive_by_confidence(self):
        # 1.3.x: imageAddedAtRuntime >= 95 without "frida" was conclusive.
        self.assertEqual(self.jb(203, "/var/jb/usr/lib/libellekit.dylib"), [])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

`Server/tests/test_parity.py`:

```py
"""Client/server classification parity (Kaisel 1.4.0, spec P1.2).

Parses the Swift sources (the same way Server/tools/export_weights.py does) and
checks the conclusive, indicator and corroborating sets against
Server/classification.json, which the server loads directly. Also checks that
weights.json matches Finding.swift and that the committed C string table
matches the manifest, so an indicator-list change cannot silently change
classification semantics.
"""
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import unittest

ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", ".."))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "Server"))
import classification as cls  # noqa: E402
import policy as pol  # noqa: E402

SWIFT = os.path.join(ROOT, "Sources", "KaiselKit")


def swift_set(path: str, name: str) -> set:
    src = open(os.path.join(SWIFT, path)).read()
    m = re.search(r"static let " + name + r": Set<FindingCode> = \[(.*?)\]", src, re.S)
    if not m:
        raise AssertionError(f"{name} not found in {path}")
    return set(re.findall(r"\.(\w+)", m.group(1)))


class Parity(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_conclusive_codes(self):
        self.assertEqual(swift_set("JailbreakDetector.swift", "conclusiveCodes"), set(cls.MANIFEST["conclusiveCodes"]))

    def test_indicator_codes(self):
        self.assertEqual(swift_set("JailbreakDetector.swift", "indicatorCodes"), set(cls.MANIFEST["indicatorCodes"]))

    def test_corroborating_codes(self):
        self.assertEqual(swift_set("IntegrityKit.swift", "corroboratingCodes"), set(cls.MANIFEST["corroboratingCodes"]))

    def test_conclusive_and_indicator_disjoint(self):
        self.assertFalse(set(cls.MANIFEST["conclusiveCodes"]) & set(cls.MANIFEST["indicatorCodes"]))

    def test_server_sets_derive_from_manifest(self):
        names = {c: m["name"] for c, m in pol.CODES.items()}
        self.assertEqual({names[c] for c in pol.CONCLUSIVE}, set(cls.MANIFEST["conclusiveCodes"]))
        self.assertEqual({names[c] for c in pol.CORROBORATING}, set(cls.MANIFEST["corroboratingCodes"]))

    def test_conclusive_set_is_pinned(self):
        # Changing what proves a jailbreak must be a deliberate, reviewed edit of this test too.
        self.assertEqual(set(cls.MANIFEST["conclusiveCodes"]), {
            "jailbreakPathPresent", "rootlessJailbreakRoot", "symlinkedSystemDirectory", "rootVolumeWritable",
            "sandboxWrite", "sandboxFork", "libcSyscallMismatch", "imageListMismatch", "envJailbreak",
            "suspiciousEntitlement", "bundlePathUnexpected", "suspiciousImage"})
        self.assertEqual(cls.MANIFEST["conclusiveRequiresImageRevalidation"], ["suspiciousImage"])

    def test_weights_json_matches_finding_swift(self):
        before = open(os.path.join(ROOT, "Server", "weights.json")).read()
        src = open(os.path.join(SWIFT, "Finding.swift")).read()
        codes = {m.group(1): m.group(2) for m in re.finditer(r"case (\w+)\s*=\s*(\d+)", src)}
        data = json.loads(before)
        self.assertEqual({v["name"]: k for k, v in data.items()}, codes)

    def test_c_string_table_matches_manifest(self):
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(ROOT, "Scripts", "gen_strings.py"), "--verify"],
                           capture_output=True, text=True)
        self.assertEqual(r.returncode, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr)

    def test_c_finding_codes_mirror_swift(self):
        hdr = open(os.path.join(ROOT, "Sources", "CIntegrityCore", "include", "rasp_core.h")).read()
        self.assertIn("RASP_F_INSTRUMENTATION_IMAGE  = 205", hdr)
        self.assertIn("RASP_F_IMAGE_MARKER_MATCH     = 206", hdr)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

`Server/tests/test_server.py`:

```py
"""Offline tests for the reference server. A synthetic CA stands in for Apple's
App Attest root, so the full verification path runs without a device.

  python3 -m unittest discover -s Server/tests
"""
import base64
import datetime as dt
import hashlib
import json
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

import cbor2
from cryptography import x509
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes, serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import ec
from cryptography.x509.oid import NameOID

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
import appattest  # noqa: E402
import policy as pol  # noqa: E402
import rules_tool  # noqa: E402
import server  # noqa: E402

APP_ID = "ABCDE12345.com.dhanesh.kaisel.integritycheck"
sha = lambda b: hashlib.sha256(b).digest()


def _cert(subject, issuer, pub, signer, ca, extensions=()):
    now = dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc)
    b = (x509.CertificateBuilder().subject_name(x509.Name([x509.NameAttribute(NameOID.COMMON_NAME, subject)]))
         .issuer_name(x509.Name([x509.NameAttribute(NameOID.COMMON_NAME, issuer)]))
         .public_key(pub).serial_number(x509.random_serial_number())
         .not_valid_before(now - dt.timedelta(days=1)).not_valid_after(now + dt.timedelta(days=30))
         .add_extension(x509.BasicConstraints(ca=ca, path_length=None), critical=True))
    for e in extensions:
        b = b.add_extension(e, critical=False)
    return b.sign(signer, hashes.SHA256())


class FakeAppleCA:
    def __init__(self):
        self.root_key = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP384R1())
        self.root = _cert("Fake App Attest Root", "Fake App Attest Root", self.root_key.public_key(), self.root_key, True)
        self.int_key = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP384R1())
        self.inter = _cert("Fake App Attest CA 1", "Fake App Attest Root", self.int_key.public_key(), self.root_key, True)
        self.root_pem = self.root.public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM)

    def attest(self, challenge: bytes, app_id=APP_ID, aaguid=appattest.AAGUID_DEVELOPMENT, counter=0):
        cred_key = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())
        raw = cred_key.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.X962, serialization.PublicFormat.UncompressedPoint)
        key_id = sha(raw)
        auth = (sha(app_id.encode()) + bytes([0x41]) + counter.to_bytes(4, "big") + aaguid
                + len(key_id).to_bytes(2, "big") + key_id + b"\xa5cose")
        nonce = sha(auth + sha(challenge))
        ext = x509.UnrecognizedExtension(appattest.APP_ATTEST_NONCE_OID, bytes([0x30, 0x24, 0xA1, 0x22, 0x04, 0x20]) + nonce)
        cred = _cert("cred", "Fake App Attest CA 1", cred_key.public_key(), self.int_key, False, [ext])
        obj = cbor2.dumps({"fmt": "apple-appattest", "authData": auth,
                           "attStmt": {"x5c": [cred.public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.DER),
                                               self.inter.public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.DER)],
                                       "receipt": b"r"}})
        return cred_key, base64.b64encode(key_id).decode(), obj

    @staticmethod
    def assertion(cred_key, client_data_hash: bytes, counter: int, app_id=APP_ID):
        auth = sha(app_id.encode()) + bytes([0x01]) + counter.to_bytes(4, "big")
        sig = cred_key.sign(sha(auth + client_data_hash), ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))
        return cbor2.dumps({"signature": sig, "authenticatorData": auth})


def report_dict(signals, **over):
    """A well-formed 1.4.0 report with every expected check complete."""
    d = {"verdict": "trusted", "sdkVersion": "1.4.0", "reportSchemaVersion": 2,
         "signals": [dict({"detail": ""}, **x) for x in signals],
         "checksCompleted": pol.ALL_CHECKS, "checksExpected": pol.ALL_CHECKS,
         "build": {"textSHA256": "ab"}, "rulesVersion": 1}
    d.update(over)
    return d


def report(signals, **over):
    return json.dumps(report_dict(signals, **over)).encode()


STOCK_IOS27 = [  # iPhone18,1 iOS 27.2.1 Xcode install: all build/posture noise
    {"code": 603, "confidence": 90}, {"code": 601, "confidence": 90}, {"code": 900, "confidence": 90},
    {"code": 800, "confidence": 95}, {"code": 503, "confidence": 55}]
DOPAMINE = [{"code": 105, "confidence": 99}, {"code": 100, "confidence": 95}, {"code": 200, "confidence": 95},
            {"code": 701, "confidence": 95}]
FRIDA = [{"code": 500, "confidence": 95}, {"code": 502, "confidence": 97}, {"code": 503, "confidence": 55}]


class AppAttestTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.ca = FakeAppleCA()

    def test_attestation_ok_and_assertion_counter(self):
        ch = os.urandom(32)
        k, kid, obj = self.ca.attest(ch)
        att = appattest.verify_attestation(obj, kid, ch, APP_ID, self.ca.root_pem, allow_development=True)
        self.assertEqual(att.environment, "development")
        cdh = sha(b"payload" + b"nonce")
        c1 = appattest.verify_assertion(self.ca.assertion(k, cdh, 1), cdh, att.public_key_pem, APP_ID, 0)
        self.assertEqual(c1, 1)
        with self.assertRaises(appattest.AttestationError):   # replay
            appattest.verify_assertion(self.ca.assertion(k, cdh, 1), cdh, att.public_key_pem, APP_ID, c1)
        with self.assertRaises(appattest.AttestationError):   # different payload
            appattest.verify_assertion(self.ca.assertion(k, cdh, 2), sha(b"other"), att.public_key_pem, APP_ID, c1)

    def test_rejects_wrong_challenge_app_env_and_root(self):
        ch = os.urandom(32)
        _, kid, obj = self.ca.attest(ch)
        for kwargs, msg in [(dict(challenge=os.urandom(32)), "nonce"),
                            (dict(app_id="OTHER.app"), "RP ID"),
                            (dict(allow_development=False), "AAGUID"),
                            (dict(root_ca_pem=FakeAppleCA().root_pem), "chain")]:
            args = dict(attestation_object=obj, key_id_b64=kid, challenge=ch, app_id=APP_ID,
                        root_ca_pem=self.ca.root_pem, allow_development=True)
            args.update(kwargs)
            with self.assertRaises(appattest.AttestationError, msg=msg):
                appattest.verify_attestation(**args)

    def test_secure_enclave_signature(self):
        se = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())
        cdh = sha(b"x")
        sig = se.sign(cdh, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))
        pub = se.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.X962, serialization.PublicFormat.UncompressedPoint)
        appattest.verify_secure_enclave(sig, pub, cdh)
        with self.assertRaises(appattest.AttestationError):
            appattest.verify_secure_enclave(sig, pub, sha(b"y"))


class PolicyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_stock_ios27_dev_build_is_allowed(self):
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None)
        a = pol.assess(STOCK_IOS27, p)
        self.assertEqual(a["device"]["verdict"], "trusted")
        self.assertEqual(a["runtime"]["verdict"], "trusted")
        self.assertEqual(pol.decide(a, "verified", p)[0], "allow")

    def test_same_signals_fail_app_store_expectation(self):
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution="appStore")
        a = pol.assess(STOCK_IOS27, p)
        self.assertIn(a["app"]["verdict"], ("elevated", "high", "compromised"))

    def test_jailbreak_and_frida_denied(self):
        p = pol.Policy()
        self.assertEqual(pol.assess(DOPAMINE, p)["device"]["verdict"], "compromised")
        self.assertEqual(pol.decide(pol.assess(DOPAMINE, p), "verified", p)[0], "deny")
        self.assertIn(pol.assess(FRIDA, p)["runtime"]["verdict"], ("high", "compromised"))

    def test_roothide_unhidden_device_is_compromised(self):
        # Lab: iPhone9,3, iOS 15.8.2, Dopamine + RootHide, app not hidden.
        signals = [
            {"code": 200, "confidence": 95, "detail": "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/.jbroot-0/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib"},
            {"code": 304, "confidence": 70, "detail": "csops: no svc #0x80 in first 8 insns"},
            {"code": 905, "confidence": 80, "detail": "sileo://"},
        ]
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, require_app_attest=False)
        a = pol.assess(signals, p)
        self.assertEqual(a["device"]["verdict"], "compromised")
        self.assertEqual(len(a["device"]["jailbreakEvidence"]), 1)
        self.assertEqual(pol.decide(a, "missing", p)[0], "deny")

    def test_frida_gadget_alone_is_not_a_jailbreak(self):
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None)
        a = pol.assess([{"code": 200, "confidence": 95, "detail": "/x/FridaGadget.dylib"}], p)
        self.assertEqual(a["device"]["jailbreakEvidence"], [])
        self.assertNotEqual(a["device"]["verdict"], "compromised")

    def test_history_flags_hidden_rerun(self):
        # Same key, later report is clean because the app was hidden.
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, require_app_attest=False)
        a = pol.assess([], p)
        self.assertEqual(a["device"]["verdict"], "trusted")
        reasons = pol.apply_history(a, "2026-11-21T12:00:00Z")
        self.assertEqual(a["device"]["verdict"], "high")
        self.assertTrue(reasons)
        self.assertEqual(pol.decide(a, "missing", p)[0], "deny")

    def test_stock_ios27_dyld_layout_mismatch_is_not_a_jailbreak(self):
        # Regression: 1.3.0 reported "Jailbroken" on a stock iPhone18,1 / iOS 27.2.1.
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, require_app_attest=False)
        a = pol.assess([{"code": 110, "confidence": 95,
                         "detail": "dyld __text differs from disk: 162671 words, first at +0x0"}], p)
        self.assertEqual(a["device"]["jailbreakEvidence"], [])
        self.assertNotIn(a["device"]["verdict"], ("high", "compromised"))

    def test_history_from_demoted_evidence_is_ignored(self):
        self.assertFalse(pol.history_valid("2026-11-21T16:50:00Z"))
        self.assertFalse(pol.history_valid({"firstSeen": "x", "evidence": [{"code": 110, "detail": "dyld"}]}))
        self.assertTrue(pol.history_valid({"firstSeen": "x", "evidence": [
            {"code": 200, "detail": ".jbroot-0/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib"}]}))

    def test_missing_app_attest_denied_when_required(self):
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, require_app_attest=True)
        self.assertEqual(pol.decide(pol.assess([], p), "missing", p)[0], "deny")
        # 1.4.0: strict mode requires App Attest even if require_app_attest is False.
        p_strict = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, require_app_attest=False)
        self.assertEqual(pol.decide(pol.assess([], p_strict), "missing", p_strict)[0], "deny")
        p2 = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, mode="lab", require_app_attest=False)
        self.assertEqual(pol.decide(pol.assess([], p2), "missing", p2)[0], "step_up")


class RulesTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_sign_verify_and_tamper(self):
        key = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())
        priv = key.private_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM, serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8, serialization.NoEncryption())
        pub = key.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM, serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo)
        data = open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "..", "rules", "rules.json"), "rb").read()
        rules_tool.validate(json.loads(data))
        sig = rules_tool.sign_bytes(priv, data)
        rules_tool.verify_bytes(pub, data, sig)
        with self.assertRaises(Exception):
            rules_tool.verify_bytes(pub, data.replace(b'"version": 1', b'"version": 9'), sig)


class EndToEndTests(unittest.TestCase):
    """Enrol + sealed report through verify_sealed(), as the HTTP handler does."""

    def test_flow(self):
        ca = FakeAppleCA()
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            store = server.Store(os.path.join(d, "db.json"))

            class Cfg:
                app_id = APP_ID; root_ca = ca.root_pem; allow_development = True
                expected_distribution = None; require_app_attest = True; debugger_expected = False

            cid, ch = store.new_challenge()
            k, kid, obj = ca.attest(store.take_challenge(cid))
            att = appattest.verify_attestation(obj, kid, ch, APP_ID, ca.root_pem, allow_development=True)
            store.devices[kid] = {"publicKeyPEM": att.public_key_pem, "counter": 0, "sePublicKey": None}

            se = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())
            se_pub = se.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.X962, serialization.PublicFormat.UncompressedPoint)

            def sealed(signals, counter, nonce):
                payload = report(signals)
                cdh = sha(payload + nonce)
                return {"payload": base64.b64encode(payload).decode(), "nonce": base64.b64encode(nonce).decode(),
                        "clientDataHash": base64.b64encode(cdh).decode(), "appAttestKeyId": kid,
                        "appAttestAssertion": base64.b64encode(ca.assertion(k, cdh, counter)).decode(),
                        "secureEnclaveSignature": base64.b64encode(se.sign(cdh, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))).decode(),
                        "secureEnclavePublicKey": base64.b64encode(se_pub).decode()}

            cid, ch = store.new_challenge()
            r = server.verify_sealed(sealed(STOCK_IOS27, 1, ch), store.take_challenge(cid), store, Cfg)
            self.assertEqual((r["decision"], r["appAttest"], r["secureEnclave"]), ("allow", "verified", "verified (bound)"))

            cid, ch = store.new_challenge()
            r = server.verify_sealed(sealed(DOPAMINE, 2, ch), store.take_challenge(cid), store, Cfg)
            self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")

            # challenge is single use
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                store.take_challenge(cid)
            # payload swapped after sealing -> structured deny (1.4.0; raised ValueError before)
            cid, ch = store.new_challenge()
            s = sealed(DOPAMINE, 3, ch)
            s["payload"] = base64.b64encode(report(STOCK_IOS27)).decode()
            r = server.verify_sealed(s, store.take_challenge(cid), store, Cfg)
            self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")
            self.assertIn("tampered", r["reasons"][0])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`Server/tests/test_validation.py`:

```py
"""Strict-mode report validation and decision tests (Kaisel 1.4.0, spec P0.2).

The server must never answer `allow` in strict mode for an incomplete,
malformed, unsupported, tampered, replayed or unauthenticated report.

  python3 -m unittest discover -s Server/tests
"""
import base64
import copy
import json
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import hashes, serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric import ec

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import appattest  # noqa: E402
import policy as pol  # noqa: E402
import server  # noqa: E402
from test_server import APP_ID, STOCK_IOS27, DOPAMINE, FakeAppleCA, report_dict, sha  # noqa: E402

STRICT = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None)
LAB = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, mode="lab", require_app_attest=False)
ONE_MISSING = pol.ALL_CHECKS & ~(1 << pol.CHECK_NAMES.index("images"))


def decision(rep, policy=STRICT, app_attest="verified"):
    v = pol.validate_report(rep, policy)
    sigs = rep.get("signals") if isinstance(rep, dict) and isinstance(rep.get("signals"), list) else []
    sigs = [x for x in sigs if isinstance(x, dict) and pol._is_int(x.get("code")) and pol._is_int(x.get("confidence"))]
    return pol.decide(pol.assess(sigs, policy), app_attest, policy, v)


class StrictNeverAllows(unittest.TestCase):
    def assertNotAllowed(self, rep, want=None, **kw):
        d, reasons = decision(rep, **kw)
        self.assertNotEqual(d, "allow", reasons)
        if want:
            self.assertEqual(d, want, reasons)
        return reasons

    def test_valid_complete_report_is_allowed(self):
        self.assertEqual(decision(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27))[0], "allow")

    def test_empty_report(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed({}, "deny")

    def test_not_an_object(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed([], "deny")

    def test_checks_completed_zero(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], checksCompleted=0), "deny")

    def test_checks_expected_zero(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], checksExpected=0), "deny")

    def test_client_cannot_shrink_expected_set(self):
        # A client claiming fewer expected checks is contradicting the server profile.
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], checksExpected=ONE_MISSING, checksCompleted=ONE_MISSING), "deny")

    def test_missing_required_check_bit(self):
        rep = report_dict([{"code": 1000, "confidence": 95, "detail": "images: never entered"}],
                          checksCompleted=ONE_MISSING)
        reasons = self.assertNotAllowed(rep)
        self.assertTrue(any("images" in r for r in reasons), reasons)

    def test_missing_bit_without_check_suppressed_signal(self):
        reasons = self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], checksCompleted=ONE_MISSING))
        self.assertTrue(any("without a checkSuppressed" in r for r in reasons))

    def test_unknown_completion_bits(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], checksCompleted=pol.ALL_CHECKS | (1 << 20)), "deny")

    def test_contradictory_check_suppressed(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([{"code": 1000, "confidence": 95, "detail": "x"}]), "deny")

    def test_invalid_bitmap_types(self):
        for bad in ("2047", 2047.0, True, None, -1, 1 << 32, [2047]):
            with self.subTest(bad=bad):
                self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], checksCompleted=bad), "deny")
                self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], checksExpected=bad), "deny")

    def test_unsupported_versions(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], sdkVersion="9.9.9"), "deny")
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], sdkVersion=None), "deny")
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], reportSchemaVersion=3), "deny")
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([], reportSchemaVersion="2"), "deny")

    def test_legacy_131_report_is_lab_only(self):
        legacy = report_dict([], sdkVersion="1.3.1")
        del legacy["reportSchemaVersion"]
        self.assertNotAllowed(legacy, "deny")
        self.assertEqual(decision(legacy, LAB, "missing")[0], "step_up")

    def test_unknown_critical_signal_code(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([{"code": 4242, "confidence": 90}]), "deny")
        self.assertNotEqual(decision(report_dict([{"code": 4242, "confidence": 90}]), LAB)[0], "allow")

    def test_malformed_signals(self):
        for sig in ({"code": "105", "confidence": 90}, {"code": 105, "confidence": 101},
                    {"code": 105, "confidence": "90"}, {"code": 105, "confidence": 90, "detail": 7},
                    {"code": True, "confidence": 90}, "105"):
            with self.subTest(sig=sig):
                rep = report_dict([])
                rep["signals"] = [sig]
                self.assertNotAllowed(rep, "deny")
        rep = report_dict([])
        rep["signals"] = "none"
        self.assertNotAllowed(rep, "deny")
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict([{"code": 800, "confidence": 1}] * 600), "deny")

    def test_missing_app_attest_assertion(self):
        self.assertNotAllowed(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27), "deny", app_attest="missing")

    def test_strict_ignores_require_app_attest_false(self):
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None, require_app_attest=False)
        self.assertEqual(decision(report_dict([]), p, "missing")[0], "deny")

    def test_lab_mode_is_never_allow_without_app_attest(self):
        self.assertEqual(decision(report_dict([]), LAB, "missing")[0], "step_up")

    def test_incomplete_report_in_lab_is_step_up(self):
        rep = report_dict([{"code": 1000, "confidence": 95, "detail": "images: never entered"}],
                          checksCompleted=ONE_MISSING)
        self.assertEqual(decision(rep, LAB, "verified")[0], "deny")   # checkSuppressed is hard -> runtime high
        self.assertNotEqual(decision(report_dict([], checksCompleted=ONE_MISSING), LAB)[0], "allow")


class DevelopmentExceptions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_xcode_debug_build_with_policy_exceptions_is_allowed(self):
        p = pol.Policy(expected_distribution="development", debugger_expected=True)
        sigs = [{"code": 601, "confidence": 90}, {"code": 900, "confidence": 90}, {"code": 603, "confidence": 90},
                {"code": 400, "confidence": 95}, {"code": 401, "confidence": 95}, {"code": 800, "confidence": 95}]
        self.assertEqual(decision(report_dict(sigs), p)[0], "allow")

    def test_same_build_without_exceptions_is_not_allowed(self):
        sigs = [{"code": 400, "confidence": 95}, {"code": 401, "confidence": 95}]
        self.assertNotEqual(decision(report_dict(sigs), pol.Policy(expected_distribution=None))[0], "allow")


class StepUp(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_elevated_risk_steps_up(self):
        # An unknown external image and a URL scheme: indicators, not proof.
        sigs = [{"code": 201, "confidence": 70, "detail": "/var/mobile/x.dylib"}, {"code": 905, "confidence": 80, "detail": "sileo://"}]
        d, reasons = decision(report_dict(sigs))
        self.assertEqual(d, "step_up", reasons)


class SealedFlow(unittest.TestCase):
    """Through server.verify_sealed(), as the HTTP handler calls it."""

    def setUp(self):
        self.ca = FakeAppleCA()
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.store = server.Store(os.path.join(self.tmp.name, "db.json"))

        class Cfg:
            app_id = APP_ID; root_ca = self.ca.root_pem; allow_development = True
            expected_distribution = None; require_app_attest = True; debugger_expected = False; mode = "strict"
        self.Cfg = Cfg
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        self.k, self.kid, obj = self.ca.attest(self.store.take_challenge(cid))
        att = appattest.verify_attestation(obj, self.kid, ch, APP_ID, self.ca.root_pem, allow_development=True)
        self.store.devices[self.kid] = {"publicKeyPEM": att.public_key_pem, "counter": 0, "sePublicKey": None}
        self.counter = 0
        self.se = ec.generate_private_key(ec.SECP256R1())

    def tearDown(self):
        self.tmp.cleanup()

    def sealed(self, rep: dict, nonce: bytes, assertion=True, se=True):
        payload = json.dumps(rep).encode()
        cdh = sha(payload + nonce)
        s = {"payload": base64.b64encode(payload).decode(), "nonce": base64.b64encode(nonce).decode(),
             "clientDataHash": base64.b64encode(cdh).decode()}
        if assertion:
            self.counter += 1
            s["appAttestKeyId"] = self.kid
            s["appAttestAssertion"] = base64.b64encode(self.ca.assertion(self.k, cdh, self.counter)).decode()
        if se:
            pub = self.se.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.X962, serialization.PublicFormat.UncompressedPoint)
            s["secureEnclaveSignature"] = base64.b64encode(self.se.sign(cdh, ec.ECDSA(hashes.SHA256()))).decode()
            s["secureEnclavePublicKey"] = base64.b64encode(pub).decode()
        return s

    def verify(self, rep, **kw):
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        return server.verify_sealed(self.sealed(rep, ch, **kw), self.store.take_challenge(cid), self.store, self.Cfg)

    def test_valid_report_allowed(self):
        r = self.verify(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27))
        self.assertEqual((r["decision"], r["appAttest"], r["mode"]), ("allow", "verified", "strict"))

    def test_incomplete_report_not_allowed(self):
        r = self.verify(report_dict([], checksCompleted=ONE_MISSING))
        self.assertNotEqual(r["decision"], "allow")
        self.assertEqual(r["validation"]["action"], "step_up")

    def test_tampered_payload_and_signature(self):
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        s = self.sealed(report_dict(DOPAMINE), ch)
        s["payload"] = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27)).encode()).decode()
        r = server.verify_sealed(s, self.store.take_challenge(cid), self.store, self.Cfg)
        self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        s = self.sealed(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27), ch)
        sig = bytearray(base64.b64decode(s["appAttestAssertion"]))
        sig[-5] ^= 0xFF
        s["appAttestAssertion"] = base64.b64encode(bytes(sig)).decode()
        r = server.verify_sealed(s, self.store.take_challenge(cid), self.store, self.Cfg)
        self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")
        self.assertTrue(r["appAttest"].startswith("failed"))

    def test_wrong_or_replayed_challenge(self):
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        s = self.sealed(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27), b"\0" * 32)
        r = server.verify_sealed(s, self.store.take_challenge(cid), self.store, self.Cfg)
        self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):          # challenge is single use
            self.store.take_challenge(cid)

    def test_missing_assertion_with_valid_se_signature_is_denied(self):
        r = self.verify(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27), assertion=False)
        self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")
        self.assertIn("unbound", r["secureEnclave"])

    def test_unbound_se_key_never_writes_history(self):
        lab = type("LabCfg", (self.Cfg,), {"mode": "lab", "require_app_attest": False})
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        s = self.sealed(report_dict(DOPAMINE), ch, assertion=False)
        server.verify_sealed(s, self.store.take_challenge(cid), self.store, lab)
        self.assertEqual(self.store.devices.get("_history", {}), {})

    def test_history_keyed_by_app_attest_and_hidden_rerun_denied(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.verify(report_dict(DOPAMINE))["decision"], "deny")
        self.assertIn("aa:" + self.kid, self.store.devices["_history"])
        r = self.verify(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27))        # same instance, app now hidden
        self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")
        self.assertEqual(r["device"]["verdict"], "high")

    def test_stale_history_from_broad_marker_is_ignored(self):
        self.store.devices["_history"] = {"aa:" + self.kid: {"firstSeen": "2026-11-20T00:00:00Z", "evidence": [
            {"code": "200", "detail": "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/CraneBank.app/Frameworks/CraneBank.framework/CraneBank"}]}}
        self.assertEqual(self.verify(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27))["decision"], "allow")

    def test_legacy_se_history_honoured_only_when_bound(self):
        pub = base64.b64encode(self.se.public_key().public_bytes(
            serialization.Encoding.X962, serialization.PublicFormat.UncompressedPoint)).decode()
        self.store.devices["_history"] = {"se:" + pub: {"firstSeen": "2026-11-20T00:00:00Z", "evidence": [
            {"code": "200", "detail": "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/.jbroot-0/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib"}]}}
        r = self.verify(report_dict(STOCK_IOS27))
        self.assertEqual(r["decision"], "deny")
        self.assertTrue(any("previously seen" in x for x in r["reasons"]))

    def test_malformed_sealed_inputs(self):
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        self.assertEqual(server.verify_sealed("x", self.store.take_challenge(cid), self.store, self.Cfg)["decision"], "deny")
        cid, ch = self.store.new_challenge()
        s = self.sealed(report_dict([]), ch)
        bad = copy.deepcopy(s)
        bad["payload"] = "%%%"
        self.assertEqual(server.verify_sealed(bad, self.store.take_challenge(cid), self.store, self.Cfg)["decision"], "deny")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

```

`Server/tools/export_weights.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Exports finding codes, weights, hard flags and domains from Finding.swift to
Server/weights.json so the server scores exactly like the SDK.
Run after changing Finding.swift:  python3 Server/tools/export_weights.py"""
import json, os, re

here = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(here, "..", ".."))
src = open(os.path.join(root, "Sources/KaiselKit/Finding.swift")).read()

codes = {m.group(1): int(m.group(2)) for m in re.finditer(r"case (\w+)\s*=\s*(\d+)", src)}
meta = {}
for m in re.finditer(r"case \.(\w+):\s*return \.init\(category: \.(\w+),\s*weight: ([\d.]+),\s*hard: (true|false),\s*title: \"([^\"]*)\"\)", src):
    meta[m.group(1)] = dict(category=m.group(2), weight=float(m.group(3)), hard=m.group(4) == "true", title=m.group(5))

dom_block = src[src.index("public var domain: IntegrityDomain"):]
domains = {}
for m in re.finditer(r"case ((?:\.\w+,?\s*)+):\s*return \.(\w+)", dom_block):
    for name in re.findall(r"\.(\w+)", m.group(1)):
        domains[name] = m.group(2)

out = {}
for name, raw in codes.items():
    if name not in meta:
        continue
    out[str(raw)] = dict(name=name, domain=domains.get(name, "runtime"), **meta[name])
missing = [n for n in codes if n not in meta or n not in domains]
if missing:
    raise SystemExit(f"unparsed codes: {missing}")
path = os.path.join(root, "Server", "weights.json")
with open(path, "w") as f:
    json.dump(out, f, indent=1, sort_keys=True)
print(f"wrote {len(out)} codes to {path}")

```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/include/rasp_core.h`:

```h
// KaiselKit - low-level C core.
//
// All checks write `rasp_finding_t` records into a caller-owned buffer and set a
// bit in `checks_completed`. The Swift layer owns weighting and scoring; the C
// layer only reports evidence. Finding codes are mirrored in Finding.swift.

#ifndef RASP_CORE_H
#define RASP_CORE_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define RASP_MAX_FINDINGS 96
#define RASP_DETAIL_LEN   160

typedef struct {
    uint16_t code;        // RASP_F_* below
    uint8_t  confidence;  // 0-100, evidence quality as judged by the check
    uint8_t  _pad;
    char     detail[RASP_DETAIL_LEN];
} rasp_finding_t;

typedef struct {
    rasp_finding_t items[RASP_MAX_FINDINGS];
    uint32_t count;
    uint32_t checks_completed;   // bitmask of RASP_CHECK_*
    uint32_t checks_started;     // set on entry; started-but-not-completed => suppressed
    uint32_t dropped;            // findings lost because the buffer was full
} rasp_findings_t;

// ---- Check identifiers (bitmask) ---------------------------------------------
enum {
    RASP_CHECK_FILESYSTEM   = 1u << 0,
    RASP_CHECK_SANDBOX      = 1u << 1,
    RASP_CHECK_IMAGES       = 1u << 2,
    RASP_CHECK_HOOKS        = 1u << 3,
    RASP_CHECK_DEBUGGER     = 1u << 4,
    RASP_CHECK_FRIDA        = 1u << 5,
    RASP_CHECK_CODESIGN     = 1u << 6,
    RASP_CHECK_ENVIRONMENT  = 1u << 7,
    RASP_CHECK_DEVMODE      = 1u << 8,
    RASP_CHECK_CANARY       = 1u << 9,
    RASP_CHECK_SELF         = 1u << 10,
};

// ---- Finding codes ------------------------------------------------------------
enum {
    // Filesystem / jailbreak artefacts
    RASP_F_JB_PATH_PRESENT        = 100,
    RASP_F_JB_PATH_ERRNO_ORACLE   = 101,
    RASP_F_SYMLINKED_SYSTEM_DIR   = 102,
    RASP_F_ROOT_NOT_SEALED        = 103,
    RASP_F_ROOT_WRITABLE          = 104,
    RASP_F_JB_ROOT_SYMLINK        = 105,
    RASP_F_LIBC_SYSCALL_MISMATCH  = 108,
    RASP_F_CANARY_FAILED          = 109,
    RASP_F_DYLD_MODIFIED          = 110,
    RASP_F_USERSPACE_REBOOT       = 111,
    // Sandbox
    RASP_F_SANDBOX_WRITE          = 120,
    RASP_F_SANDBOX_FORK           = 121,
    RASP_F_SANDBOX_READ           = 122,
    // Loaded images / injection
    RASP_F_SUSPICIOUS_IMAGE       = 200,   // 1.4.0: known jailbreak injector only (conclusive)
    RASP_F_UNEXPECTED_IMAGE_PATH  = 201,
    RASP_F_IMAGE_LIST_MISMATCH    = 202,
    RASP_F_IMAGE_ADDED_RUNTIME    = 203,
    RASP_F_INTERPOSE_SECTION      = 204,
    RASP_F_INSTRUMENTATION_IMAGE  = 205,   // 1.4.0: Frida/Cycript, or injector inside the bundle
    RASP_F_IMAGE_MARKER_MATCH     = 206,   // 1.4.0: broad marker in an external image name
    // Hooks
    RASP_F_INLINE_HOOK            = 300,
    RASP_F_SYMBOL_REBOUND         = 301,
    RASP_F_BREAKPOINT_INSN        = 302,
    RASP_F_OBJC_SWIZZLED          = 303,
    RASP_F_SYSCALL_STUB_ALTERED   = 304,
    RASP_F_SYMBOL_FOREIGN_IMAGE   = 305,
    RASP_F_PROLOGUE_CHANGED       = 306,
    // Debugger
    RASP_F_PTRACED                = 400,
    RASP_F_CS_DEBUGGED            = 401,
    RASP_F_EXCEPTION_PORTS        = 402,
    RASP_F_PPID_UNEXPECTED        = 403,
    // Frida / instrumentation
    RASP_F_FRIDA_THREAD           = 500,
    RASP_F_FRIDA_PORT_OPEN        = 501,
    RASP_F_FRIDA_DBUS             = 502,
    RASP_F_ANON_EXEC_MEMORY       = 503,
    RASP_F_FRIDA_MEMORY_MARKER    = 504,
    // Code signing / binary integrity
    RASP_F_CS_INVALID             = 600,
    RASP_F_GET_TASK_ALLOW         = 601,
    RASP_F_SUSPICIOUS_ENTITLEMENT = 602,
    RASP_F_BINARY_DECRYPTED       = 603,
    RASP_F_TEXT_MODIFIED          = 610,
    // Environment
    RASP_F_ENV_INJECTION          = 700,
    RASP_F_ENV_JAILBREAK          = 701,
    RASP_F_ENV_GETENV_MISMATCH    = 702,
    // Developer mode
    RASP_F_DEVMODE_ENABLED        = 800,
    RASP_F_DDI_MOUNTED            = 802,
};

// ---- Context passed from Swift ------------------------------------------------
typedef struct {
    const char *bundle_path;       // Bundle.main.bundlePath (realpath'd by caller)
    const char *executable_path;   // Bundle.main.executablePath
    const char *container_tmp;     // NSTemporaryDirectory()
    const uint16_t *frida_ports;   // loopback TCP ports to probe
    uint32_t frida_port_count;
    uint32_t probe_timeout_ms;     // per-port connect timeout
    bool     allow_fork_probe;     // fork() probe (child _exit()s immediately)
    bool     scan_memory;          // walk VM regions for anonymous exec memory
} rasp_context_t;

// ---- Checks ---------------------------------------------------------------------
void rasp_check_filesystem(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_sandbox(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_images(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_hooks(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_debugger(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_frida(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_codesign(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_environment(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_devmode(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
void rasp_check_canary(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);
// Compares the live main-image __text hash and the first bytes of sensitive
// functions (libsystem and the RASP core) with the baseline taken by
// rasp_integrity_baseline(). Detects code patched in memory after start-up.
void rasp_check_self(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out);

// ---- Self-integrity baseline ------------------------------------------------------
// Call as early as possible (IntegrityKit/JailbreakDetector call it on init).
// Idempotent; the first call wins.
void rasp_integrity_baseline(void);

// ---- Remote rules (additive) -------------------------------------------------------
// Adds jailbreak paths and image-name markers on top of the compiled-in lists.
// Image markers only ever produce RASP_F_IMAGE_MARKER_MATCH (an indicator).
// Strings are copied. Passing new lists replaces the previous *extra* lists;
// the compiled-in lists can never be removed this way.
void rasp_rules_set(const char *const *paths, uint32_t path_count,
                    const char *const *markers, uint32_t marker_count);

// ---- Facts the Swift layer needs for policy decisions ------------------------------
typedef struct {
    uint32_t cs_flags;             // csops(CS_OPS_STATUS)
    int32_t  cs_status_errno;      // 0 on success
    int32_t  cryptid;              // LC_ENCRYPTION_INFO(_64) cryptid, -1 if absent
    int32_t  devmode_sysctl;       // 1 on, 0 off, -1 unavailable
    int32_t  exception_port_count; // task-level exception ports set
    char     text_sha256[65];      // hex SHA-256 of main image __TEXT,__text
} rasp_facts_t;

void rasp_collect_facts(rasp_facts_t *facts);

// ---- Runtime monitoring ----------------------------------------------------------
// Registers a dyld add-image callback. Images loaded after registration are
// queued; drain with rasp_drain_new_images(). Safe to call more than once.
void rasp_monitor_install(void);
// Copies up to `max` newly loaded image paths into `buf`, which holds `max`
// NUL-terminated slots of RASP_DETAIL_LEN bytes each. Returns the number copied.
// Classification is done by rasp_classify_image().
uint32_t rasp_drain_new_images(char *buf, uint32_t max);

// ---- Loaded-image classification (rasp_classify.c) -------------------------------
// Explicit evidence classes. Only RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR is conclusive.
typedef enum {
    RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN = 0,            // no usable path: nothing can be said
    RASP_IMG_TRUSTED,                // system location, or inside the app bundle (identity not matched)
    RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR,     // exact injector basename outside the bundle, or a jailbreak-root path
    RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION,        // Frida/Cycript, or an injector basename inside the bundle (re-signed app)
    RASP_IMG_MARKER,                 // broad marker (built-in or remote rules) in an external image name
    RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL,       // any other image outside the system and the bundle
} rasp_image_class_t;
// `bundle_path` may be NULL (re-validating stored evidence): an image is then
// treated as in-bundle when a directory component ends in ".app".
// `path` may carry a text prefix; classification starts at its first '/'.
rasp_image_class_t rasp_image_class(const char *path, const char *bundle_path);
// Same, with the simulator behaviour explicit (used by tests on any platform).
rasp_image_class_t rasp_image_class_ex(const char *path, const char *bundle_path, bool simulator);
// Finding code for a class: 200, 205, 206, 201, or 0 for trusted/unknown.
uint16_t rasp_image_class_code(rasp_image_class_t c);
uint8_t rasp_image_class_confidence(rasp_image_class_t c);
// Shorthand: rasp_image_class_code(rasp_image_class(path, bundle_path)).
uint16_t rasp_classify_image(const char *path, const char *bundle_path);

// ---- Active countermeasures (opt-in) ------------------------------------------------
// PT_DENY_ATTACH via raw syscall. Prevents later debugger attach on stock kernels;
// trivially bypassed on a jailbroken kernel. Never call this in debug builds.
int rasp_deny_debugger_attach(void);

// ---- Obfuscated strings ---------------------------------------------------------------
// Decodes entry `index` into `out` (NUL-terminated). Returns decoded length or 0.
size_t rasp_obf_decode(uint16_t index, char *out, size_t cap);
void   rasp_secure_zero(void *p, size_t n);

// ---- Inline quick guard ---------------------------------------------------------------
// Header-only, force-inlined check meant to be sprinkled at many call sites
// (before login, payment, key use) so there is no single function to hook.
// Uses raw syscalls only. Returns a bitmask of RASP_GUARD_* (0 = nothing seen).
enum { RASP_GUARD_TRACED = 1u << 0, RASP_GUARD_CS_DEBUGGED = 1u << 1, RASP_GUARD_JB_ROOT = 1u << 2 };

#if defined(__aarch64__) && defined(__APPLE__)
#include <sys/sysctl.h>
static inline __attribute__((always_inline))
long rasp_guard_svc(long num, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3, long a4, long a5, int *failed) {
    register long x0 __asm__("x0") = a0;
    register long x1 __asm__("x1") = a1;
    register long x2 __asm__("x2") = a2;
    register long x3 __asm__("x3") = a3;
    register long x4 __asm__("x4") = a4;
    register long x5 __asm__("x5") = a5;
    register long x16 __asm__("x16") = num;
    long carry;
    __asm__ volatile("svc #0x80\n\tcset %[c], cs\n\t"
                     : "+r"(x0), "+r"(x1), "+r"(x16), [c] "=&r"(carry)
                     : "r"(x2), "r"(x3), "r"(x4), "r"(x5)
                     : "memory", "cc");
    *failed = (int)carry;
    return x0;
}
#endif

static inline __attribute__((always_inline)) uint32_t rasp_quick_guard(void) {
    uint32_t flags = 0;
#if defined(__aarch64__) && defined(__APPLE__)
    int failed = 0;
    int pid = (int)rasp_guard_svc(20 /* getpid */, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, &failed);

    struct kinfo_proc kp;
    size_t len = sizeof kp;
    int mib[4] = { CTL_KERN, KERN_PROC, KERN_PROC_PID, pid };
    __builtin_memset(&kp, 0, sizeof kp);
    rasp_guard_svc(202 /* __sysctl */, (long)mib, 4, (long)&kp, (long)&len, 0, 0, &failed);
    if (!failed && (kp.kp_proc.p_flag & 0x800 /* P_TRACED */)) flags |= RASP_GUARD_TRACED;

    uint32_t cs = 0;
    rasp_guard_svc(169 /* csops */, pid, 0 /* CS_OPS_STATUS */, (long)&cs, sizeof cs, 0, 0, &failed);
    if (!failed && (cs & 0x10000000u /* CS_DEBUGGED */)) flags |= RASP_GUARD_CS_DEBUGGED;

    // "/var/jb" assembled from XOR-ed bytes so it is not a plaintext literal.
    volatile unsigned char enc[8] = { 0x75, 0x2c, 0x3b, 0x28, 0x75, 0x30, 0x38, 0x5a };
    char path[8];
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) path[i] = (char)(enc[i] ^ 0x5a);
    unsigned char st[256];   /* >= sizeof(struct stat) */
    rasp_guard_svc(340 /* lstat64 */, (long)path, (long)st, 0, 0, 0, 0, &failed);
    if (!failed) flags |= RASP_GUARD_JB_ROOT;
#endif
    return flags;
}

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
#endif

```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_classify.c`:

```c
// Loaded-image classification (1.4.0).
//
// Pure string logic with no Mach-O or dyld dependency, so the same code runs
// in the image scan, in the runtime image monitor, when the Swift layer
// re-validates stored evidence, and in the host-side C regression tests
// (Tests/CClassifier).
//
// Classes, in order of evaluation:
//   1. instrumentation      exact Frida/Cycript basenames (or structured
//                           frida-agent-* prefixes), or a jailbreak injector
//                           basename found *inside* the app bundle (a re-signed
//                           app with injected tweaks runs on a stock phone).
//                           Runtime risk; never proof of a jailbreak.
//   2. jailbreak injector   exact injector basename outside the app bundle, or
//                           an image under a structured jailbreak root
//                           (/var/jb/, MobileSubstrate DynamicLibraries,
//                           TweakInject, or a RootHide .jbroot-* component).
//                           The only image class that is conclusive.
//   3. trusted              system locations, and images inside the app bundle.
//                           In-bundle code is classified by identity only:
//                           telling the app's own frameworks from code added
//                           by a re-signer needs a build manifest (roadmap).
//   4. marker               a broad substring (built-in list or signed remote
//                           rules) in the basename of an image outside the
//                           system and the bundle. Indicator only.
//   5. unknown external     any other image outside the system and the bundle.
//
// Broad markers are never applied to the full path or to in-bundle images, so
// CraneBank.framework, LibertyFinance.framework or ChoicyAnalytics.framework
// in an app's own bundle are not evidence of anything.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#ifdef __APPLE__
#include <TargetConditionals.h>
#endif
#include <ctype.h>
#include <string.h>

#define CLS_PATH_MAX 1024

static bool ieq(const char *a, const char *b) {
    for (; *a && *b; a++, b++)
        if (tolower((unsigned char)*a) != tolower((unsigned char)*b)) return false;
    return *a == *b;
}

static bool istarts(const char *s, const char *pre) {
    for (; *pre; s++, pre++)
        if (!*s || tolower((unsigned char)*s) != tolower((unsigned char)*pre)) return false;
    return true;
}

// /private/var/... -> /var/..., /private/preboot/... -> /preboot/...
// Other /private/... paths are left alone.
const char *rasp_path_normalise(const char *path) {
    if (!path) return NULL;
    if (strncmp(path, "/private/var/", 13) == 0 || strncmp(path, "/private/preboot/", 17) == 0)
        return path + 8;
    return path;
}

// Directory-boundary prefix test on normalised paths: "/var/App.app" contains
// "/var/App.app/x" and itself, but not "/var/App.app2/x".
bool rasp_path_within(const char *path, const char *dir) {
    if (!path || !dir || !*dir) return false;
    const char *p = rasp_path_normalise(path), *d = rasp_path_normalise(dir);
    size_t dl = strlen(d);
    while (dl > 1 && d[dl - 1] == '/') dl--;          // tolerate a trailing slash
    if (strncmp(p, d, dl) != 0) return false;
    return p[dl] == '\0' || p[dl] == '/';
}

// Without a known bundle path (re-validation of stored evidence), an image is
// treated as in-bundle when any path component ends in ".app".
static bool has_app_component(const char *p) {
    const char *c = p;
    while (*c) {
        const char *slash = strchr(c, '/');
        size_t n = slash ? (size_t)(slash - c) : strlen(c);
        if (n > 4 && slash) {          // a directory component, not the file name
            const char *e = c + n - 4;
            if (tolower((unsigned char)e[0]) == '.' && tolower((unsigned char)e[1]) == 'a' &&
                tolower((unsigned char)e[2]) == 'p' && tolower((unsigned char)e[3]) == 'p')
                return true;
        }
        if (!slash) break;
        c = slash + 1;
    }
    return false;
}

static bool in_app(const char *p, const char *bundle) {
    if (bundle && *bundle) return rasp_path_within(p, bundle);
    return has_app_component(p);
}

static const char *base_name(const char *p) {
    const char *b = strrchr(p, '/');
    return b ? b + 1 : p;
}

typedef bool (*str_pred)(const char *subject, const char *item);

static bool list_any(uint16_t start, uint16_t count, const char *subject, str_pred pred) {
    char item[RASP_STR_MAX];
    bool hit = false;
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < count && !hit; i++) {
        if (!rasp_obf_decode((uint16_t)(start + i), item, sizeof item)) continue;
        hit = pred(subject, item);
        rasp_secure_zero(item, sizeof item);
    }
    return hit;
}

static bool pred_ieq(const char *s, const char *item) { return ieq(s, item); }
static bool pred_istarts(const char *s, const char *item) { return istarts(s, item); }
static bool pred_icontains(const char *s, const char *item) { return rasp_strcasestr(s, item) != NULL; }
static bool pred_within(const char *s, const char *item) { return rasp_path_within(s, item); }

// True when a path component starts with `item` (".jbroot-" matches
// "/.../Application/.jbroot-7351422000C774F3/usr/lib/x.dylib").
static bool pred_component(const char *s, const char *item) {
    for (const char *c = s; c && *c; ) {
        if (istarts(c, item)) return true;
        c = strchr(c, '/');
        if (c) c++;
    }
    return false;
}

rasp_image_class_t rasp_image_class_ex(const char *raw_path, const char *bundle_path, bool simulator) {
    if (!raw_path || !*raw_path) return RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN;
    // Stored details may carry a prefix such as "exec mapping of /x/y.dylib".
    const char *slash = strchr(raw_path, '/');
    if (!slash) return RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN;
    const char *p = rasp_path_normalise(slash);
    const char *base = base_name(p);
    if (!*base || strlen(p) >= CLS_PATH_MAX) return RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN;
    bool inside = in_app(p, bundle_path);

    if (list_any(RASP_LIST_IMAGE_INSTR_BASENAMES_START, RASP_LIST_IMAGE_INSTR_BASENAMES_COUNT, base, pred_ieq) ||
        list_any(RASP_LIST_IMAGE_INSTR_PREFIXES_START, RASP_LIST_IMAGE_INSTR_PREFIXES_COUNT, base, pred_istarts))
        return RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION;

    bool injector = list_any(RASP_LIST_IMAGE_INJECTOR_BASENAMES_START, RASP_LIST_IMAGE_INJECTOR_BASENAMES_COUNT,
                             base, pred_ieq);
    if (injector && inside) return RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION;   // re-signed app with injected tweaks
    if (!inside && (injector ||
        list_any(RASP_LIST_IMAGE_JB_ROOT_PREFIXES_START, RASP_LIST_IMAGE_JB_ROOT_PREFIXES_COUNT, p, pred_within) ||
        list_any(RASP_LIST_IMAGE_JB_ROOT_COMPONENTS_START, RASP_LIST_IMAGE_JB_ROOT_COMPONENTS_COUNT, p, pred_component)))
        return RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR;

    if (inside) return RASP_IMG_TRUSTED;
    if (simulator) return RASP_IMG_TRUSTED;   // simulator images live under Xcode.app / CoreSimulator
    if (list_any(RASP_LIST_IMAGE_TRUSTED_PREFIXES_START, RASP_LIST_IMAGE_TRUSTED_PREFIXES_COUNT, p, pred_within))
        return RASP_IMG_TRUSTED;

    if (list_any(RASP_LIST_IMAGE_BROAD_MARKERS_START, RASP_LIST_IMAGE_BROAD_MARKERS_COUNT, base, pred_icontains) ||
        rasp_rules_marker_match(base))
        return RASP_IMG_MARKER;
    return RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL;
}

rasp_image_class_t rasp_image_class(const char *path, const char *bundle_path) {
#if defined(TARGET_OS_SIMULATOR) && TARGET_OS_SIMULATOR
    return rasp_image_class_ex(path, bundle_path, true);
#else
    return rasp_image_class_ex(path, bundle_path, false);
#endif
}

uint16_t rasp_image_class_code(rasp_image_class_t c) {
    switch (c) {
    case RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR: return RASP_F_SUSPICIOUS_IMAGE;
    case RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION:    return RASP_F_INSTRUMENTATION_IMAGE;
    case RASP_IMG_MARKER:             return RASP_F_IMAGE_MARKER_MATCH;
    case RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL:   return RASP_F_UNEXPECTED_IMAGE_PATH;
    case RASP_IMG_TRUSTED:
    case RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN:
    default:                          return 0;
    }
}

uint8_t rasp_image_class_confidence(rasp_image_class_t c) {
    switch (c) {
    case RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR: return 95;
    case RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION:    return 95;
    case RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL:   return 70;
    case RASP_IMG_MARKER:             return 60;
    default:                          return 0;
    }
}

uint16_t rasp_classify_image(const char *path, const char *bundle_path) {
    return rasp_image_class_code(rasp_image_class(path, bundle_path));
}
```
