`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_codesign.c`:

```c
// Code-signing, entitlement and binary-integrity checks, plus fact collection.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <CommonCrypto/CommonDigest.h>
#include <mach-o/getsect.h>

#define RASP_CS_OPS_STATUS               0
#define RASP_CS_OPS_ENTITLEMENTS_BLOB    7
#define RASP_CS_OPS_DER_ENTITLEMENTS_BLOB 16
#define RASP_CS_VALID          0x00000001u
#define RASP_CS_GET_TASK_ALLOW 0x00000004u

int32_t rasp_exception_port_count(void);
int32_t rasp_devmode_sysctl(void);

// csops entitlement blobs are prefixed with an 8-byte big-endian header.
static uint8_t g_ent_buf[64 * 1024];

static size_t read_entitlements(int pid) {
    int err = 0;
    memset(g_ent_buf, 0, sizeof g_ent_buf);
    if (rasp_raw_csops(pid, RASP_CS_OPS_ENTITLEMENTS_BLOB, g_ent_buf, sizeof g_ent_buf, &err) == 0) {
        uint32_t len = ((uint32_t)g_ent_buf[4] << 24) | ((uint32_t)g_ent_buf[5] << 16) |
                       ((uint32_t)g_ent_buf[6] << 8) | g_ent_buf[7];
        if (len > 8 && len <= sizeof g_ent_buf) return len;
    }
    memset(g_ent_buf, 0, sizeof g_ent_buf);
    if (rasp_raw_csops(pid, RASP_CS_OPS_DER_ENTITLEMENTS_BLOB, g_ent_buf, sizeof g_ent_buf, &err) == 0) {
        uint32_t len = ((uint32_t)g_ent_buf[4] << 24) | ((uint32_t)g_ent_buf[5] << 16) |
                       ((uint32_t)g_ent_buf[6] << 8) | g_ent_buf[7];
        if (len > 8 && len <= sizeof g_ent_buf) return len;
    }
    return 0;
}

static bool mem_contains(const uint8_t *hay, size_t hl, const char *needle) {
    size_t nl = strlen(needle);
    if (nl == 0 || nl > hl) return false;
    for (size_t i = 0; i + nl <= hl; i++)
        if (hay[i] == (uint8_t)needle[0] && memcmp(hay + i, needle, nl) == 0) return true;
    return false;
}

void rasp_check_codesign(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    (void)ctx;
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_CODESIGN);
    int pid = rasp_raw_getpid();
    uint32_t flags = 0; int err = 0;
    if (rasp_raw_csops(pid, RASP_CS_OPS_STATUS, &flags, sizeof flags, &err) == 0) {
        if (!(flags & RASP_CS_VALID))
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_CS_INVALID, 90, "CS_VALID clear (cs_flags 0x%08x)", flags);
        if (flags & RASP_CS_GET_TASK_ALLOW)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_GET_TASK_ALLOW, 90, "get-task-allow (cs_flags 0x%08x)", flags);
    }

    size_t len = read_entitlements(pid);
    if (len) {
        char key[RASP_STR_MAX];
        for (uint16_t i = 0; i < RASP_LIST_SUSPICIOUS_ENTITLEMENTS_COUNT; i++) {
            if (!rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_SUSPICIOUS_ENTITLEMENTS_START + i, key, sizeof key)) continue;
            if (mem_contains(g_ent_buf + 8, len - 8, key))
                rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SUSPICIOUS_ENTITLEMENT, 90, "%s", key);
            rasp_secure_zero(key, sizeof key);
        }
    }
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_CODESIGN);
}

typedef struct { int32_t cryptid; } crypt_ctx_t;

static bool crypt_visitor(const struct load_command *lc, void *c) {
    crypt_ctx_t *cc = (crypt_ctx_t *)c;
    if (lc->cmd == LC_ENCRYPTION_INFO_64) {
        cc->cryptid = (int32_t)((const struct encryption_info_command_64 *)lc)->cryptid;
        return false;
    }
    if (lc->cmd == LC_ENCRYPTION_INFO) {
        cc->cryptid = (int32_t)((const struct encryption_info_command *)lc)->cryptid;
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

void rasp_collect_facts(rasp_facts_t *facts) {
    if (!facts) return;
    memset(facts, 0, sizeof *facts);
    int pid = rasp_raw_getpid();
    int err = 0;
    uint32_t flags = 0;
    facts->cs_status_errno = rasp_raw_csops(pid, RASP_CS_OPS_STATUS, &flags, sizeof flags, &err) == 0 ? 0 : err;
    facts->cs_flags = flags;
    facts->exception_port_count = rasp_exception_port_count();
    facts->devmode_sysctl = rasp_devmode_sysctl();

    intptr_t slide = 0;
    const struct mach_header_64 *mh = rasp_main_header(&slide);
    crypt_ctx_t cc = { .cryptid = -1 };
    rasp_for_each_load_command(mh, crypt_visitor, &cc);
    facts->cryptid = cc.cryptid;

    // SHA-256 of the main executable's __TEXT,__text as mapped (post-decryption).
    // Compare server-side against the value recorded at build time.
    unsigned long size = 0;
    const uint8_t *text = mh ? getsectiondata(mh, SEG_TEXT, SECT_TEXT, &size) : NULL;
    if (text && size) {
        unsigned char digest[CC_SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH];
        CC_SHA256(text, (CC_LONG)size, digest);
        static const char hex[] = "0123456789abcdef";
        for (int i = 0; i < CC_SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH; i++) {
            facts->text_sha256[i * 2] = hex[digest[i] >> 4];
            facts->text_sha256[i * 2 + 1] = hex[digest[i] & 0xF];
        }
        facts->text_sha256[64] = '\0';
    }
}
```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_debug.c`:

```c
// Debugger and developer-mode checks.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <sys/sysctl.h>
#include <mach/mach.h>

#ifndef P_TRACED
#define P_TRACED 0x00000800
#endif
#define RASP_CS_OPS_STATUS 0
#define RASP_CS_DEBUGGED   0x10000000u
#define RASP_PT_DENY_ATTACH 31

static const exception_mask_t kDebugMasks =
    EXC_MASK_BREAKPOINT | EXC_MASK_BAD_ACCESS | EXC_MASK_BAD_INSTRUCTION |
    EXC_MASK_SOFTWARE | EXC_MASK_ARITHMETIC;

int32_t rasp_exception_port_count(void);
int32_t rasp_exception_port_count(void) {
    exception_mask_t masks[EXC_TYPES_COUNT];
    mach_port_t ports[EXC_TYPES_COUNT];
    exception_behavior_t behaviours[EXC_TYPES_COUNT];
    thread_state_flavor_t flavours[EXC_TYPES_COUNT];
    mach_msg_type_number_t count = EXC_TYPES_COUNT;
    if (task_get_exception_ports(mach_task_self(), kDebugMasks, masks, &count, ports, behaviours, flavours) != KERN_SUCCESS)
        return -1;
    int32_t n = 0;
    for (mach_msg_type_number_t i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        if (MACH_PORT_VALID(ports[i])) {
            n++;
            mach_port_deallocate(mach_task_self(), ports[i]);
        }
    }
    return n;
}

void rasp_check_debugger(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    (void)ctx;
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_DEBUGGER);
    int pid = rasp_raw_getpid();

    // 1. P_TRACED via raw __sysctl (libc sysctl is a favourite hook target).
    {
        int mib[4] = { CTL_KERN, KERN_PROC, KERN_PROC_PID, pid };
        struct kinfo_proc kp;
        size_t len = sizeof kp;
        memset(&kp, 0, sizeof kp);
        int err = 0;
        if (rasp_raw_sysctl(mib, 4, &kp, &len, NULL, 0, &err) == 0 && (kp.kp_proc.p_flag & P_TRACED))
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_PTRACED, 95, "P_TRACED set (p_flag 0x%x)", (unsigned)kp.kp_proc.p_flag);
    }

    // 2. Code-signing status: the kernel sets CS_DEBUGGED once a debugger has
    //    been attached or invalid pages have been allowed (e.g. by a debugger
    //    writing breakpoints). It is sticky for the life of the process.
    {
        uint32_t flags = 0; int err = 0;
        if (rasp_raw_csops(pid, RASP_CS_OPS_STATUS, &flags, sizeof flags, &err) == 0 && (flags & RASP_CS_DEBUGGED))
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_CS_DEBUGGED, 85, "CS_DEBUGGED (cs_flags 0x%08x)", flags);
    }

    // 3. Task-level exception ports. LLDB/debugserver and Frida register these;
    //    so do some crash reporters, hence medium confidence and a Swift-side
    //    allowance (Configuration.allowedExceptionPorts).
    {
        int32_t n = rasp_exception_port_count();
        if (n > 0) rasp_add(out, RASP_F_EXCEPTION_PORTS, 60, "%d task exception port(s) set", n);
    }

    // 4. Parent process. Apps are spawned by launchd (pid 1). A debugger or
    //    `frida -f` spawn makes the parent debugserver/frida-server.
    {
        int ppid = rasp_raw_getppid();
        if (ppid != 1) rasp_add(out, RASP_F_PPID_UNEXPECTED, 70, "ppid %d", ppid);
    }
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_DEBUGGER);
}

int rasp_deny_debugger_attach(void) {
    int err = 0;
    long r = rasp_sys4(RASP_SYS_ptrace, RASP_PT_DENY_ATTACH, 0, 0, 0, &err);
    return r == 0 ? 0 : err;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Developer Mode (iOS 16+).
// There is no public API. Two observable proxies:
//   * sysctl "security.mac.amfi.developer_mode_status" (int). Readable from the
//     app sandbox on the iOS versions we have seen; may become unavailable.
//   * A mounted Developer Disk Image exposes debugserver at /Developer (<=16)
//     or /System/Developer (17+, personalised DDI).
// A third, inferential proxy (get-task-allow set and running on 16+ implies
// Developer Mode is on) is applied in Swift.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
int32_t rasp_devmode_sysctl(void);
int32_t rasp_devmode_sysctl(void) {
    char name[64];
    if (!rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_DEVMODE_SYSCTL), name, sizeof name)) return -1;
    int mib[CTL_MAXNAME];
    size_t miblen = CTL_MAXNAME;
    int rc = sysctlnametomib(name, mib, &miblen);
    rasp_secure_zero(name, sizeof name);
    if (rc != 0) return -1;
    int32_t value = -1;
    size_t len = sizeof value;
    int err = 0;
    if (rasp_raw_sysctl(mib, (unsigned)miblen, &value, &len, NULL, 0, &err) != 0) return -1;
    return value ? 1 : 0;
}

void rasp_check_devmode(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    (void)ctx;
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_DEVMODE);
    int32_t dm = rasp_devmode_sysctl();
    if (dm == 1) rasp_add(out, RASP_F_DEVMODE_ENABLED, 95, "amfi developer_mode_status=1");

    char path[RASP_STR_MAX];
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < RASP_LIST_DDI_PATHS_COUNT; i++) {
        if (!rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_DDI_PATHS_START + i, path, sizeof path)) continue;
        struct stat st; int err = 0;
        if (rasp_raw_lstat(path, &st, &err) == 0)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_DDI_MOUNTED, 85, "%s", path);
        rasp_secure_zero(path, sizeof path);
    }
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_DEVMODE);
}
```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_env.c`:

```c
// Environment checks. The environment block is walked directly through
// _NSGetEnviron() and then cross-checked against getenv(): a tweak that hooks
// getenv to hide DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES produces a disagreement.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#if __has_include(<crt_externs.h>)
#include <crt_externs.h>
#define RASP_ENVIRON() (_NSGetEnviron() ? *_NSGetEnviron() : NULL)
#else
extern char **environ;
#define RASP_ENVIRON() (environ)
#endif

static void scan_list(rasp_findings_t *out, char **env, uint16_t start, uint16_t count, uint16_t code) {
    char name[RASP_STR_MAX];
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        size_t nl = rasp_obf_decode(start + i, name, sizeof name);
        if (!nl) continue;
        for (char **e = env; e && *e; e++) {
            if (strncmp(*e, name, nl) == 0 && (*e)[nl] == '=') {
                rasp_add(out, code, 95, "%s", name);
                if (getenv(name) == NULL)
                    rasp_add(out, RASP_F_ENV_GETENV_MISMATCH, 95, "getenv hides %s", name);
                break;
            }
        }
        rasp_secure_zero(name, sizeof name);
    }
}

void rasp_check_environment(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    (void)ctx;
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_ENVIRONMENT);
    char **env = RASP_ENVIRON();
    scan_list(out, env, RASP_LIST_ENV_INJECTION_START, RASP_LIST_ENV_INJECTION_COUNT, RASP_F_ENV_INJECTION);
    scan_list(out, env, RASP_LIST_ENV_JB_START, RASP_LIST_ENV_JB_COUNT, RASP_F_ENV_JAILBREAK);
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_ENVIRONMENT);
}
```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_frida.c`:

```c
// Frida and generic dynamic-instrumentation detection.
//
//  1. Thread names: Frida's runtime starts GLib/gum threads ("gum-js-loop",
//     "gmain", "gdbus", "pool-frida") inside the target. Read through
//     thread_info(THREAD_EXTENDED_INFO), not pthread APIs.
//  2. frida-server on loopback: TCP connect to configured ports, then a D-Bus
//     SASL "AUTH" probe. Frida speaks D-Bus and answers "REJECTED ..."; an
//     arbitrary service on the same port will not.
//  3. Memory map: executable regions at the top level of the address space that
//     belong to no dyld image and are not file-backed. Frida's agent is mapped
//     by its own Mach-O loader (no dyld record) and Interceptor allocates r-x
//     trampoline pages. Those regions are then scanned for agent markers.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <poll.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <arpa/inet.h>
#include <netinet/in.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#include <mach/mach.h>
#include <mach/vm_map.h>
#include <mach-o/dyld.h>

static void check_threads(rasp_findings_t *out) {
    thread_act_array_t threads = NULL;
    mach_msg_type_number_t count = 0;
    if (task_threads(mach_task_self(), &threads, &count) != KERN_SUCCESS) return;

    char marker[64];
    for (mach_msg_type_number_t t = 0; t < count; t++) {
        thread_extended_info_data_t info;
        mach_msg_type_number_t ic = THREAD_EXTENDED_INFO_COUNT;
        if (thread_info(threads[t], THREAD_EXTENDED_INFO, (thread_info_t)&info, &ic) == KERN_SUCCESS &&
            info.pth_name[0]) {
            for (uint16_t i = 0; i < RASP_LIST_FRIDA_THREADS_COUNT; i++) {
                size_t ml = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_FRIDA_THREADS_START + i, marker, sizeof marker);
                if (!ml) continue;
                // Short generic names must match exactly; longer ones as substrings.
                bool hit = (strcmp(info.pth_name, marker) == 0) ||
                           (ml > 5 && strstr(info.pth_name, marker) != NULL);
                rasp_secure_zero(marker, sizeof marker);
                if (hit) {
                    rasp_add(out, RASP_F_FRIDA_THREAD, 95, "thread \"%.48s\"", info.pth_name);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        mach_port_deallocate(mach_task_self(), threads[t]);
    }
    vm_deallocate(mach_task_self(), (vm_address_t)threads, count * sizeof(thread_act_t));
}

static int connect_loopback(uint16_t port, uint32_t timeout_ms) {
    int fd = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, 0);
    if (fd < 0) return -1;
    int one = 1;
    setsockopt(fd, SOL_SOCKET, SO_NOSIGPIPE, &one, sizeof one);
    fcntl(fd, F_SETFL, fcntl(fd, F_GETFL, 0) | O_NONBLOCK);
    struct sockaddr_in sa;
    memset(&sa, 0, sizeof sa);
    sa.sin_len = sizeof sa;
    sa.sin_family = AF_INET;
    sa.sin_port = htons(port);
    sa.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(INADDR_LOOPBACK);
    int rc = connect(fd, (struct sockaddr *)&sa, sizeof sa);
    if (rc != 0 && errno != EINPROGRESS) { close(fd); return -1; }
    if (rc != 0) {
        struct pollfd p = { .fd = fd, .events = POLLOUT };
        if (poll(&p, 1, (int)timeout_ms) != 1) { close(fd); return -1; }
        int soerr = 0; socklen_t sl = sizeof soerr;
        if (getsockopt(fd, SOL_SOCKET, SO_ERROR, &soerr, &sl) != 0 || soerr != 0) { close(fd); return -1; }
    }
    return fd;
}

static void check_ports(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    if (!ctx || !ctx->frida_ports) return;
    uint32_t timeout = ctx->probe_timeout_ms ? ctx->probe_timeout_ms : 150;
    char auth[16], rejected[16];
    size_t al = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_DBUS_AUTH), auth, sizeof auth);
    size_t rl = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_DBUS_REJECTED), rejected, sizeof rejected);

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < ctx->frida_port_count; i++) {
        uint16_t port = ctx->frida_ports[i];
        int fd = connect_loopback(port, timeout);
        if (fd < 0) continue;
        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_FRIDA_PORT_OPEN, 60, "127.0.0.1:%u accepts connections", port);
        if (al && send(fd, auth, al, 0) == (ssize_t)al) {
            struct pollfd p = { .fd = fd, .events = POLLIN };
            if (poll(&p, 1, (int)timeout) == 1) {
                char reply[64] = {0};
                ssize_t n = recv(fd, reply, sizeof reply - 1, 0);
                if (n >= (ssize_t)rl && rl && memcmp(reply, rejected, rl) == 0)
                    rasp_add(out, RASP_F_FRIDA_DBUS, 97, "D-Bus endpoint on 127.0.0.1:%u", port);
            }
        }
        close(fd);
    }
    rasp_secure_zero(auth, sizeof auth);
    rasp_secure_zero(rejected, sizeof rejected);
}

typedef int (*proc_regionfilename_fn)(int, uint64_t, void *, uint32_t);

static bool region_contains_marker(vm_address_t addr, vm_size_t size) {
    char marker[64];
    bool hit = false;
    const uint8_t *base = (const uint8_t *)addr;
    for (uint16_t m = 0; m < RASP_LIST_FRIDA_MEM_MARKERS_COUNT && !hit; m++) {
        size_t ml = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_FRIDA_MEM_MARKERS_START + m, marker, sizeof marker);
        if (!ml || ml > size) continue;
        for (vm_size_t i = 0; i + ml <= size; i++)
            if (base[i] == (uint8_t)marker[0] && memcmp(base + i, marker, ml) == 0) { hit = true; break; }
        rasp_secure_zero(marker, sizeof marker);
    }
    return hit;
}

// Some executable regions are legitimate copies of image pages that are not
// in any image's address range: libobjc-trampolines remaps its trampoline
// page for imp_implementationWithBlock(), and other runtimes do similar
// remaps. If the region's first page is byte-identical to a page of a loaded
// image's __TEXT, it is a remap of signed code, not injected code.
// Only images outside the dyld shared cache, plus libobjc-trampolines, are
// compared, which keeps the cost small.
#define RASP_MH_DYLIB_IN_CACHE 0x80000000u

typedef struct { uintptr_t lo; uint64_t size; intptr_t slide; } text_ctx_t;
static bool text_visitor(const struct load_command *lc, void *c) {
    if (lc->cmd != LC_SEGMENT_64) return true;
    const struct segment_command_64 *seg = (const struct segment_command_64 *)lc;
    if (strcmp(seg->segname, SEG_TEXT) != 0) return true;
    text_ctx_t *t = (text_ctx_t *)c;
    t->lo = (uintptr_t)(seg->vmaddr + t->slide);
    t->size = seg->vmsize;
    return false;
}

static bool is_remap_of_image_text(vm_address_t addr, vm_size_t size) {
    const vm_size_t pg = vm_page_size;
    if (size < pg) return false;
    uint32_t n = _dyld_image_count();
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const struct mach_header_64 *mh = (const struct mach_header_64 *)_dyld_get_image_header(i);
        const char *name = _dyld_get_image_name(i);
        if (!mh || !name) continue;
        bool in_cache = (mh->flags & RASP_MH_DYLIB_IN_CACHE) != 0;
        if (in_cache && !strstr(name, "libobjc-trampolines")) continue;
        text_ctx_t t = { .slide = _dyld_get_image_vmaddr_slide(i) };
        rasp_for_each_load_command(mh, text_visitor, &t);
        if (!t.lo || t.size > 64u * 1024 * 1024) continue;
        for (uint64_t off = 0; off + pg <= t.size; off += pg)
            if (memcmp((const void *)addr, (const void *)(t.lo + off), pg) == 0) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

static void check_memory(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    proc_regionfilename_fn regionfilename =
        (proc_regionfilename_fn)dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, "proc_regionfilename");
    int pid = rasp_raw_getpid();
    vm_address_t addr = 0;
    uint32_t reported = 0;
    const vm_size_t kScanCap = 16u * 1024 * 1024;

    for (;;) {
        vm_size_t size = 0;
        natural_t depth = 0;
        struct vm_region_submap_info_64 info;
        mach_msg_type_number_t cnt = VM_REGION_SUBMAP_INFO_COUNT_64;
        kern_return_t kr = vm_region_recurse_64(mach_task_self(), &addr, &size, &depth,
                                                (vm_region_recurse_info_t)&info, &cnt);
        if (kr != KERN_SUCCESS) break;

        // Do not descend into submaps: the dyld shared cache lives there and
        // contains stub islands that belong to no individual image.
        if (!info.is_submap && (info.protection & VM_PROT_EXECUTE)) {
            // Skip the commpage window.
            bool commpage = addr >= 0x0000000FFFFF0000ull && addr < 0x0000001000000000ull;
            if (!commpage && !rasp_addr_in_any_image((uintptr_t)addr)) {
                char file[RASP_DETAIL_LEN] = {0};
                int fl = regionfilename ? regionfilename(pid, (uint64_t)addr, file, sizeof file - 1) : 0;
                if (fl > 0) {
                    // File-backed remap (e.g. libobjc-trampolines). Only report
                    // if the backing file itself is suspicious.
                    uint16_t c = rasp_classify_image(file, ctx ? ctx->bundle_path : NULL);
                    if (c == RASP_F_SUSPICIOUS_IMAGE || c == RASP_F_INSTRUMENTATION_IMAGE)
                        rasp_add(out, c, 90, "exec mapping of %s", file);
                } else if (reported < 8 && (info.protection & VM_PROT_READ) && is_remap_of_image_text(addr, size)) {
                    // Remapped signed code (e.g. objc block trampolines): benign.
                } else if (reported < 8) {
                    reported++;
                    bool marker = (info.protection & VM_PROT_READ) && size <= kScanCap && region_contains_marker(addr, size);
                    // On its own this is a weak signal: runtimes and future OS
                    // releases can legitimately create such regions. It becomes
                    // strong only with an agent marker or other instrumentation
                    // evidence in the same report.
                    rasp_add(out, RASP_F_ANON_EXEC_MEMORY, 55,
                             "anonymous r%sx region 0x%lx+0x%lx tag=%u share=%u maxprot=0x%x",
                             (info.protection & VM_PROT_WRITE) ? "w" : "-", (unsigned long)addr, (unsigned long)size,
                             (unsigned)info.user_tag, (unsigned)info.share_mode, (unsigned)info.max_protection);
                    if (marker)
                        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_FRIDA_MEMORY_MARKER, 97, "agent marker at 0x%lx", (unsigned long)addr);
                }
            }
        }
        if (addr + size < addr) break;  // wrapped
        addr += size;
    }
}

void rasp_check_frida(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_FRIDA);
    check_threads(out);
    check_ports(ctx, out);
    if (!ctx || ctx->scan_memory) check_memory(ctx, out);
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_FRIDA);
}

```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_fs.c`:

```c
// Filesystem, sandbox and canary checks.
//
// Not a path grep: every lookup is performed twice, once through a force-inlined
// raw `svc #0x80` and once through libc, and the *disagreement* is itself a
// signal (hiding tweaks such as Shadow/ABypass/vnodebypass-style libc hooks make
// libc lie while the raw syscall tells the truth). EPERM/EACCES is treated as an
// existence oracle only after calibrating the parent directory with a random
// non-existent sibling, which removes false positives from opaque directories.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <sys/sysctl.h>

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Parent-directory calibration cache for the errno oracle.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
typedef struct { char dir[RASP_STR_MAX]; int probe_errno; } calib_t;
#define CALIB_MAX 24

static int calibrate_parent(calib_t *cache, int *n, const char *path) {
    char dir[RASP_STR_MAX];
    strlcpy(dir, path, sizeof dir);
    char *slash = strrchr(dir, '/');
    if (!slash) return -1;
    if (slash == dir) slash[1] = '\0'; else *slash = '\0';

    for (int i = 0; i < *n; i++)
        if (strcmp(cache[i].dir, dir) == 0) return cache[i].probe_errno;

    char token[17], probe[RASP_STR_MAX];
    rasp_random_token(token);
    snprintf(probe, sizeof probe, "%s%s.%s", dir, (dir[1] == '\0') ? "" : "/", token);
    struct stat st; int err = 0;
    if (rasp_raw_lstat(probe, &st, &err) == 0) err = 0;   // random name exists?! treat as uninformative
    if (*n < CALIB_MAX) {
        strlcpy(cache[*n].dir, dir, sizeof cache[*n].dir);
        cache[*n].probe_errno = err;
        (*n)++;
    }
    return err;
}

static void probe_path(rasp_findings_t *out, const char *path, calib_t *cache, int *ncache) {
    struct stat st; int raw_err = 0;
    int raw = rasp_raw_lstat(path, &st, &raw_err);

    errno = 0;
    struct stat st2;
    int lib = lstat(path, &st2);
    int lib_err = (lib == 0) ? 0 : errno;

    if (raw == 0) {
        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_JB_PATH_PRESENT, 95, "%s", path);
        if (lib != 0 && lib_err == ENOENT)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_LIBC_SYSCALL_MISMATCH, 95, "lstat hidden: %s", path);
    } else if (raw_err == EPERM || raw_err == EACCES) {
        int probe = calibrate_parent(cache, ncache, path);
        if (probe == ENOENT) {
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_JB_PATH_ERRNO_ORACLE, 75, "%s (errno %d)", path, raw_err);
            if (lib_err == ENOENT)
                rasp_add(out, RASP_F_LIBC_SYSCALL_MISMATCH, 85, "errno masked: %s", path);
        }
    } else if (raw_err == ENOENT && lib == 0) {
        // libc claims existence the kernel denies: a hook returning fake data.
        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_LIBC_SYSCALL_MISMATCH, 70, "libc phantom: %s", path);
    }
}

void rasp_check_filesystem(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    (void)ctx;
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_FILESYSTEM);
    char path[RASP_STR_MAX];
    calib_t cache[CALIB_MAX];
    int ncache = 0;

    // 1. Artefact paths, raw vs libc, plus errno oracle. Compiled-in list
    //    first, then any extra paths from signed remote rules.
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < RASP_LIST_JB_PATHS_COUNT; i++) {
        if (!rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_JB_PATHS_START + i, path, sizeof path)) continue;
        probe_path(out, path, cache, &ncache);
        rasp_secure_zero(path, sizeof path);
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0, n = 1; i < n; i++) {
        n = rasp_rules_copy_path(i, path, sizeof path);
        if (i >= n) break;
        probe_path(out, path, cache, &ncache);
        rasp_secure_zero(path, sizeof path);
    }

    // 2. Rootless jailbreak root: /var/jb -> /private/preboot/<hash>/<jb>-<rand>/procursus
    {
        char jb[32], target[RASP_STR_MAX];
        if (!rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_VAR_JB), jb, sizeof jb)) jb[0] = '\0';
        if (jb[0]) {
            int err = 0;
            long n = rasp_raw_readlink(jb, target, sizeof target - 1, &err);
            if (n > 0) {
                target[n] = '\0';
                rasp_add(out, RASP_F_JB_ROOT_SYMLINK, 99, "%s -> %s", jb, target);
            }
        }
        rasp_secure_zero(jb, sizeof jb);
    }

    // 3. Rootful "stashing": system directories replaced with symlinks.
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < RASP_LIST_SYMLINK_DIRS_COUNT; i++) {
        if (!rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_SYMLINK_DIRS_START + i, path, sizeof path)) continue;
        struct stat st; int err = 0;
        if (rasp_raw_lstat(path, &st, &err) == 0 && S_ISLNK(st.st_mode))
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SYMLINKED_SYSTEM_DIR, 90, "%s", path);
        rasp_secure_zero(path, sizeof path);
    }

    // 4. Signed System Volume: since iOS 15 the root FS is a sealed, read-only
    //    APFS snapshot ("com.apple.os.update-<hash>@/dev/diskXsY"). Rootful
    //    jailbreaks remount it read-write or boot from a non-sealed snapshot/fakefs.
    {
        struct statfs sfs; int err = 0;
        if (rasp_raw_statfs("/", &sfs, &err) == 0) {
            if (!(sfs.f_flags & MNT_RDONLY))
                rasp_add(out, RASP_F_ROOT_WRITABLE, 90, "/ mounted rw (flags 0x%x)", (unsigned)sfs.f_flags);
            char prefix[64];
            size_t pl = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_SNAPSHOT_PREFIX), prefix, sizeof prefix);
            if (pl && strncmp(sfs.f_mntfromname, prefix, pl) != 0)
                rasp_add(out, RASP_F_ROOT_NOT_SEALED, 60, "/ from %.96s", sfs.f_mntfromname);
            rasp_secure_zero(prefix, sizeof prefix);
        }
    }
    // 5. Userspace reboot. Dopamine (and other jailbreaks that restart
    //    userspace after the kernel exploit) leave kern.userspacereboottime
    //    non-zero while the kernel keeps its original boot time. This survives
    //    per-app hiding because nothing in the app's process is involved.
    //    Stock iOS can also do this rarely, so it is an indicator only.
    {
        char name[40];
        if (rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_USERSPACE_REBOOT_SYSCTL), name, sizeof name)) {
            uint64_t t = 0; size_t len = sizeof t;
            if (sysctlbyname(name, &t, &len, NULL, 0) == 0 && len == sizeof t && t != 0) {
                struct timeval bt = {0, 0}; size_t bl = sizeof bt; int err = 0;
                int mib[2] = { CTL_KERN, KERN_BOOTTIME };
                rasp_raw_sysctl(mib, 2, &bt, &bl, NULL, 0, &err);
                rasp_add(out, RASP_F_USERSPACE_REBOOT, 70, "userspace reboot value %llu, kernel boot %ld",
                         (unsigned long long)t, (long)bt.tv_sec);
            }
            rasp_secure_zero(name, sizeof name);
        }
    }
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_FILESYSTEM);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Sandbox integrity: a stock App Sandbox forbids writes outside the container,
// forbids fork(), and denies reading other domains' preference directories.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
void rasp_check_sandbox(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_SANDBOX);
    char root[32], probe[RASP_STR_MAX], token[17];

    // Write outside the container.
    if (rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_PRIVATE), root, sizeof root)) {
        rasp_random_token(token);
        snprintf(probe, sizeof probe, "%s.%s", root, token);
        int err = 0;
        int fd = rasp_raw_open(probe, O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_WRONLY, 0600, &err);
        if (fd >= 0) {
            rasp_raw_close(fd);
            rasp_raw_unlink(probe, &err);
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SANDBOX_WRITE, 98, "created %s", probe);
        }
    }

    // Read another domain's directory.
    if (rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_PREFS_DIR), probe, sizeof probe)) {
        int err = 0;
        int fd = rasp_raw_open(probe, O_RDONLY | O_DIRECTORY, 0, &err);
        if (fd >= 0) {
            rasp_raw_close(fd);
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SANDBOX_READ, 60, "opened %s", probe);
        }
    }

    // fork(): resolved dynamically so the binary has no fork import. The child
    // performs nothing but a raw exit syscall.
    if (ctx && ctx->allow_fork_probe) {
        char name[16];
        if (rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_FORK), name, sizeof name)) {
            pid_t (*fk)(void) = (pid_t (*)(void))dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, name);
            if (fk) {
                pid_t pid = fk();
                if (pid == 0) {
                    int e; rasp_sys1(1 /* SYS_exit */, 0, &e);
                    _exit(0);
                } else if (pid > 0) {
                    int status;
                    waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
                    rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SANDBOX_FORK, 95, "fork() returned %d", (int)pid);
                }
            }
        }
        rasp_secure_zero(name, sizeof name);
    }
    rasp_secure_zero(root, sizeof root);
    rasp_secure_zero(probe, sizeof probe);
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_SANDBOX);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Canary: positive and negative controls. If a blanket hook makes *everything*
// look absent (or present), the controls fail and we say so instead of
// silently reporting "clean".
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
void rasp_check_canary(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_CANARY);
    struct stat st; int err = 0;

    if (ctx && ctx->executable_path) {
        int raw = rasp_raw_lstat(ctx->executable_path, &st, &err);
        errno = 0;
        int lib = lstat(ctx->executable_path, &st);
        if (raw != 0)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_CANARY_FAILED, 70, "raw lstat(own executable) errno %d", err);
        if (lib != 0)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_CANARY_FAILED, 85, "libc lstat(own executable) errno %d", errno);
    }

    if (ctx && ctx->container_tmp) {
        char token[17], probe[RASP_STR_MAX];
        rasp_random_token(token);
        snprintf(probe, sizeof probe, "%s/.%s", ctx->container_tmp, token);
        int raw = rasp_raw_lstat(probe, &st, &err);
        int raw_err = err;
        errno = 0;
        int lib = lstat(probe, &st);
        if (raw == 0 || raw_err != ENOENT)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_CANARY_FAILED, 70, "raw negative control errno %d", raw_err);
        if (lib == 0)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_CANARY_FAILED, 85, "libc reports a random path exists");
    }
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_CANARY);
}
```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_hooks.c`:

```c
// Hook integrity: inline trampolines, symbol rebinding (fishhook / dyld
// interposing), syscall-stub tampering, software breakpoints and Objective-C
// method swizzling on the functions RASP checks depend on.
//
// AArch64 encodings used below:
//   LDR Xt, #lit     0x58000000 | imm19<<5 | Rt            mask 0xFF000000
//   ADRP Xd, #pg     0x90000000 | ...                       mask 0x9F000000
//   BR Xn            0xD61F0000 | Rn<<5                     mask 0xFFFFFC1F
//   BRAAZ/BRABZ Xn   0xD61F081F / 0xD61F0C1F | Rn<<5        mask 0xFFFFFC1F
//   B imm26          0x14000000                             mask 0xFC000000
//   BRK #imm16       0xD4200000                             mask 0xFFE0001F
//   SVC #0x80        0xD4001001

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/sysctl.h>
#include <mach/mach.h>
#include <mach-o/dyld.h>
#include <objc/runtime.h>
#include <objc/message.h>
#if __has_include(<ptrauth.h>)
#include <ptrauth.h>
#endif

static inline const void *strip(const void *p) {
#if __has_feature(ptrauth_intrinsics)
    return ptrauth_strip(p, ptrauth_key_function_pointer);
#else
    return p;
#endif
}

#if defined(__aarch64__)
#define IS_LDR_LIT(i)   (((i) & 0xFF000000u) == 0x58000000u)
#define IS_ADRP(i)      (((i) & 0x9F000000u) == 0x90000000u)
#define IS_BR(i)        (((i) & 0xFFFFFC1Fu) == 0xD61F0000u || ((i) & 0xFFFFFC1Fu) == 0xD61F081Fu || \
                         ((i) & 0xFFFFFC1Fu) == 0xD61F0C1Fu)
#define BR_REG(i)       (((i) >> 5) & 0x1F)
#define IS_B(i)         (((i) & 0xFC000000u) == 0x14000000u)
#define IS_BRK(i)       (((i) & 0xFFE0001Fu) == 0xD4200000u)
#define IS_SVC80(i)     ((i) == 0xD4001001u)

typedef enum { PRO_OK = 0, PRO_TRAMPOLINE, PRO_FAR_BRANCH, PRO_BRK } prologue_t;

static prologue_t inspect_prologue(const void *fn, char *why, size_t whylen) {
    const uint32_t *p = (const uint32_t *)strip(fn);
    uint32_t w[6];
    memcpy(w, p, sizeof w);

    if (IS_BRK(w[0])) { snprintf(why, whylen, "brk at entry"); return PRO_BRK; }

    const char *src = rasp_image_name_for(p);

    // LDR Xt, #lit ; [..] ; BR Xt   (Substrate, Frida, ElleKit, Dobby absolute jumps)
    // The literal holds the jump target; it must not leave the function's image.
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
        if (IS_LDR_LIT(w[i])) {
            uint32_t rt = w[i] & 0x1F;
            for (int j = i + 1; j < i + 3 && j < 6; j++)
                if (IS_BR(w[j]) && BR_REG(w[j]) == rt) {
                    int32_t imm19 = (int32_t)(w[i] << 8) >> 13;
                    const uint64_t *lit = (const uint64_t *)((uintptr_t)(p + i) + (intptr_t)imm19 * 4);
                    const void *target = strip((const void *)(uintptr_t)*lit);
                    const char *dst = rasp_image_name_for(target);
                    if (!src || !dst || strcmp(src, dst) != 0) {
                        snprintf(why, whylen, "ldr x%u,#lit; br x%u -> %s", rt, rt, dst ? dst : "anonymous memory");
                        return PRO_TRAMPOLINE;
                    }
                }
        }
    }
    // ADRP Xd ; ... ; BR Xd   (page-relative trampolines). dyld4's libdyld
    // forwards through a vtable with a similar shape, so only report when the
    // ADRP page lies outside the function's own image.
    if (IS_ADRP(w[0])) {
        uint32_t rd = w[0] & 0x1F;
        for (int j = 1; j < 4; j++)
            if (IS_BR(w[j]) && BR_REG(w[j]) == rd) {
                int64_t immlo = (w[0] >> 29) & 0x3, immhi = (w[0] >> 5) & 0x7FFFF;
                int64_t imm = (int64_t)((uint64_t)((immhi << 2) | immlo) << 43) >> 43;   // sign-extend 21 bits
                uintptr_t page = ((uintptr_t)p & ~(uintptr_t)0xFFF) + (uintptr_t)(imm << 12);
                const char *dst = rasp_image_name_for((const void *)page);
                if (!src || !dst || strcmp(src, dst) != 0) {
                    snprintf(why, whylen, "adrp x%u -> %s; br x%u", rd, dst ? dst : "anonymous memory", rd);
                    return PRO_TRAMPOLINE;
                }
            }
    }
    // B imm26 at entry whose target leaves the function's own image.
    if (IS_B(w[0])) {
        int32_t imm = (int32_t)(w[0] << 6) >> 6;
        uintptr_t target = (uintptr_t)p + (intptr_t)imm * 4;
        const char *dst = rasp_image_name_for((const void *)target);
        if (!dst || !src || strcmp(src, dst) != 0) {
            snprintf(why, whylen, "b -> %s", dst ? dst : "unmapped/anonymous");
            return PRO_FAR_BRANCH;
        }
    }
    return PRO_OK;
}

// A raw syscall stub traps within its first instructions. If the entry is an
// unconditional branch that stays inside the same image (a legitimate
// wrapper in some OS builds), follow it once before deciding. On failure the
// first four instruction words go into `why` so lab runs can be diagnosed.
static bool stub_has_svc(const void *fn, char *why, size_t whylen) {
    const uint32_t *p = (const uint32_t *)strip(fn);
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (IS_SVC80(p[i])) return true;
    if (IS_B(p[0])) {
        int32_t imm = (int32_t)(p[0] << 6) >> 6;
        const uint32_t *t = (const uint32_t *)((uintptr_t)p + (intptr_t)imm * 4);
        const char *src = rasp_image_name_for(p), *dst = rasp_image_name_for(t);
        if (src && dst && strcmp(src, dst) == 0)
            for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (IS_SVC80(t[i])) return true;
    }
    snprintf(why, whylen, "%08x %08x %08x %08x", p[0], p[1], p[2], p[3]);
    return false;
}
#endif

static bool is_system_image(const char *path) {
    if (!path) return false;
    char a[32], b[32];
    size_t na = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_USR_LIB), a, sizeof a);
    size_t nb = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_SYSTEM), b, sizeof b);
    bool r = (na && strncmp(path, a, na) == 0) || (nb && strncmp(path, b, nb) == 0);
    rasp_secure_zero(a, sizeof a); rasp_secure_zero(b, sizeof b);
    return r;
}

// Direct references resolved through *our own* GOT. fishhook and dyld
// interposing rewrite these pointers; dlsym() walks export tries instead.
typedef struct { const char *label; const void *bound; } bound_ref_t;

static void check_rebinding(rasp_findings_t *out) {
    const bound_ref_t refs[] = {
        { "stat",                  (const void *)&stat },
        { "lstat",                 (const void *)&lstat },
        { "access",                (const void *)&access },
        { "getenv",                (const void *)&getenv },
        { "fopen",                 (const void *)&fopen },
        { "sysctl",                (const void *)&sysctl },
        { "sysctlbyname",          (const void *)&sysctlbyname },
        { "_dyld_image_count",     (const void *)&_dyld_image_count },
        { "_dyld_get_image_name",  (const void *)&_dyld_get_image_name },
        { "task_info",             (const void *)&task_info },
    };
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof refs / sizeof refs[0]; i++) {
        const void *viaGot = strip(refs[i].bound);
        const void *viaDlsym = strip(dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, refs[i].label));
        if (viaDlsym && viaGot != viaDlsym) {
            const char *img = rasp_image_name_for(viaGot);
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SYMBOL_REBOUND, 95, "%s bound to %s", refs[i].label, img ? img : "anonymous memory");
        }
        if (!is_system_image(rasp_image_name_for(viaGot))) {
            const char *img = rasp_image_name_for(viaGot);
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SYMBOL_FOREIGN_IMAGE, 90, "%s resolves into %s", refs[i].label, img ? img : "anonymous memory");
        }
    }
}

// Objective-C: the IMP of security-relevant methods must live in the framework
// that defines them. Frida's ObjC.implementation, Substrate MSHookMessageEx and
// plain method_exchangeImplementations all move it elsewhere.
typedef struct { const char *cls; const char *sel; int expected_misc; bool is_class_method; } objc_target_t;

static void check_objc(rasp_findings_t *out) {
    const objc_target_t targets[] = {
        { "NSFileManager", "fileExistsAtPath:",              RASP_MISC_FOUNDATION, false },
        { "NSFileManager", "fileExistsAtPath:isDirectory:",  RASP_MISC_FOUNDATION, false },
        { "NSFileManager", "isReadableFileAtPath:",          RASP_MISC_FOUNDATION, false },
        { "NSFileManager", "contentsOfDirectoryAtPath:error:", RASP_MISC_FOUNDATION, false },
        { "NSProcessInfo", "environment",                    RASP_MISC_FOUNDATION, false },
        { "NSBundle",      "bundleIdentifier",               RASP_MISC_FOUNDATION, false },
        { "NSBundle",      "infoDictionary",                 RASP_MISC_FOUNDATION, false },
        { "UIApplication", "canOpenURL:",                    RASP_MISC_UIKITCORE,  false },
        { "UIDevice",      "systemVersion",                  RASP_MISC_UIKITCORE,  false },
    };
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof targets / sizeof targets[0]; i++) {
        Class c = (Class)objc_getClass(targets[i].cls);
        if (!c) continue;
        if (targets[i].is_class_method) c = object_getClass((id)c);
        Method m = class_getInstanceMethod(c, sel_registerName(targets[i].sel));
        if (!m) continue;
        IMP imp = method_getImplementation(m);
        const char *img = rasp_image_name_for(strip((const void *)imp));
        char expect[32];
        rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(targets[i].expected_misc), expect, sizeof expect);
        if (!img || !strstr(img, expect))
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_OBJC_SWIZZLED, 90, "-[%s %s] IMP in %s", targets[i].cls, targets[i].sel,
                     img ? img : "anonymous memory");
#if defined(__aarch64__)
        else {
            char why[64];
            if (inspect_prologue((const void *)imp, why, sizeof why) != PRO_OK)
                rasp_add(out, RASP_F_INLINE_HOOK, 85, "-[%s %s]: %s", targets[i].cls, targets[i].sel, why);
        }
#endif
        rasp_secure_zero(expect, sizeof expect);
    }
}

void rasp_check_hooks(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    (void)ctx;
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_HOOKS);
    char name[64];

    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < RASP_LIST_HOOK_TARGETS_COUNT; i++) {
        size_t n = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_HOOK_TARGETS_START + i, name, sizeof name);
        if (!n) continue;
        bool syscall_stub = (n > 2 && name[0] == 'S' && name[1] == ':');
        const char *sym = syscall_stub ? name + 2 : name;
        const void *fn = dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, sym);
        if (!fn) { rasp_secure_zero(name, sizeof name); continue; }

        const char *img = rasp_image_name_for(strip(fn));
        if (!is_system_image(img))
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SYMBOL_FOREIGN_IMAGE, 90, "dlsym(%s) -> %s", sym, img ? img : "anonymous memory");

#if defined(__aarch64__)
        char why[96];
        switch (inspect_prologue(fn, why, sizeof why)) {
            case PRO_TRAMPOLINE: rasp_add(out, RASP_F_INLINE_HOOK, 95, "%s: %s", sym, why); break;
            case PRO_FAR_BRANCH: rasp_add(out, RASP_F_INLINE_HOOK, 90, "%s: %s", sym, why); break;
            case PRO_BRK:        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_BREAKPOINT_INSN, 80, "%s: %s", sym, why); break;
            case PRO_OK: break;
        }
        char stubwhy[40];
        if (syscall_stub && !stub_has_svc(fn, stubwhy, sizeof stubwhy))
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_SYSCALL_STUB_ALTERED, 60, "%s: no svc #0x80 (entry %s)", sym, stubwhy);
#endif
        rasp_secure_zero(name, sizeof name);
    }

#if defined(__aarch64__)
    // Self-integrity: the RASP entry points themselves. Hooking a check to
    // return early also leaves its "started" bit unset, which Swift reports.
    const struct { const char *n; const void *f; } self_fns[] = {
        { "fs",       (const void *)&rasp_check_filesystem },
        { "sandbox",  (const void *)&rasp_check_sandbox },
        { "images",   (const void *)&rasp_check_images },
        { "debugger", (const void *)&rasp_check_debugger },
        { "frida",    (const void *)&rasp_check_frida },
        { "codesign", (const void *)&rasp_check_codesign },
        { "env",      (const void *)&rasp_check_environment },
        { "classify", (const void *)&rasp_classify_image },
    };
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof self_fns / sizeof self_fns[0]; i++) {
        char why[96];
        prologue_t r = inspect_prologue(self_fns[i].f, why, sizeof why);
        if (r == PRO_TRAMPOLINE || r == PRO_FAR_BRANCH)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_INLINE_HOOK, 99, "RASP core [%s]: %s", self_fns[i].n, why);
        else if (r == PRO_BRK)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_BREAKPOINT_INSN, 90, "RASP core [%s]: %s", self_fns[i].n, why);
    }
#endif

    check_rebinding(out);
    check_objc(out);
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_HOOKS);
}

```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_images.c`:

```c
// Loaded-image enumeration and injection detection.
//
// Two independent sources are compared:
//   A. the public dyld API (_dyld_image_count / _dyld_get_image_name), which
//      hiding tweaks commonly hook to drop their own entries;
//   B. dyld's own `dyld_all_image_infos` structure, located through
//      task_info(TASK_DYLD_INFO) and read directly from memory.
// If B lists more images than A, someone is filtering the dyld API.
// Every image path is classified by rasp_image_class() (rasp_classify.c):
// exact identities for injectors and instrumentation, allow-listed locations
// for system and bundle code, and broad markers only as indicators.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <TargetConditionals.h>
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <mach/mach.h>
#include <mach-o/dyld.h>
#include <mach-o/dyld_images.h>
#include <mach-o/getsect.h>
#include <mach-o/fat.h>
#include <mach-o/loader.h>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <os/lock.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <unistd.h>

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mach-O helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
void rasp_for_each_load_command(const struct mach_header_64 *mh, rasp_lc_visitor v, void *ctx) {
    if (!mh || mh->magic != MH_MAGIC_64) return;
    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)(mh + 1);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < mh->ncmds; i++) {
        const struct load_command *lc = (const struct load_command *)p;
        if (lc->cmdsize < sizeof *lc) return;
        if (!v(lc, ctx)) return;
        p += lc->cmdsize;
    }
}

const struct mach_header_64 *rasp_main_header(intptr_t *slide) {
    uint32_t n = _dyld_image_count();
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        const struct mach_header *h = _dyld_get_image_header(i);
        if (h && h->filetype == MH_EXECUTE) {
            if (slide) *slide = _dyld_get_image_vmaddr_slide(i);
            return (const struct mach_header_64 *)h;
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

const char *rasp_image_name_for(const void *addr) {
    Dl_info info;
    if (addr && dladdr(addr, &info) && info.dli_fname) return info.dli_fname;
    return NULL;
}

static const struct dyld_all_image_infos *all_image_infos(void) {
    struct task_dyld_info info;
    mach_msg_type_number_t count = TASK_DYLD_INFO_COUNT;
    if (task_info(mach_task_self(), TASK_DYLD_INFO, (task_info_t)&info, &count) != KERN_SUCCESS)
        return NULL;
    return (const struct dyld_all_image_infos *)(uintptr_t)info.all_image_info_addr;
}

typedef struct { uintptr_t addr; uintptr_t load; intptr_t slide; bool hit; bool have_slide; } range_ctx_t;

static bool range_visitor(const struct load_command *lc, void *c) {
    range_ctx_t *r = (range_ctx_t *)c;
    if (lc->cmd != LC_SEGMENT_64) return true;
    const struct segment_command_64 *seg = (const struct segment_command_64 *)lc;
    if (!r->have_slide && strcmp(seg->segname, SEG_TEXT) == 0) {
        r->slide = (intptr_t)(r->load - seg->vmaddr);
        r->have_slide = true;
    }
    if (!r->have_slide || strcmp(seg->segname, SEG_PAGEZERO) == 0) return true;
    uintptr_t lo = (uintptr_t)(seg->vmaddr + r->slide);
    if (r->addr >= lo && r->addr < lo + seg->vmsize) { r->hit = true; return false; }
    return true;
}

static bool header_contains(const struct mach_header *h, uintptr_t addr) {
    if (!h) return false;
    range_ctx_t r = { .addr = addr, .load = (uintptr_t)h };
    rasp_for_each_load_command((const struct mach_header_64 *)h, range_visitor, &r);
    return r.hit;
}

bool rasp_addr_in_any_image(uintptr_t addr) {
    const struct dyld_all_image_infos *all = all_image_infos();
    if (all) {
        if (header_contains(all->dyldImageLoadAddress, addr)) return true;
        const struct dyld_image_info *arr = all->infoArray;
        if (arr) {
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < all->infoArrayCount; i++)
                if (header_contains(arr[i].imageLoadAddress, addr)) return true;
        }
    }
    // Fallback to the public API.
    uint32_t n = _dyld_image_count();
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i++)
        if (header_contains(_dyld_get_image_header(i), addr)) return true;
    return false;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Classification
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
static bool has_prefix_obf(const char *path, int misc) {
    char pre[64];
    size_t n = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(misc), pre, sizeof pre);
    bool r = n && strncmp(path, pre, n) == 0;
    rasp_secure_zero(pre, sizeof pre);
    return r;
}

// Image classification lives in rasp_classify.c (rasp_image_class).

static bool already_reported(const rasp_findings_t *out, uint16_t code, const char *detail) {
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < out->count; i++)
        if (out->items[i].code == code && strncmp(out->items[i].detail, detail, RASP_DETAIL_LEN - 1) == 0)
            return true;
    return false;
}

// Detail is the image path. A path longer than the detail buffer keeps its
// tail, so the basename (what re-validation needs) survives truncation.
void rasp_image_detail(char d[RASP_DETAIL_LEN], const char *path) {
    size_t n = strlen(path);
    if (n < RASP_DETAIL_LEN) snprintf(d, RASP_DETAIL_LEN, "%s", path);
    else snprintf(d, RASP_DETAIL_LEN, "...%s", path + n - (RASP_DETAIL_LEN - 4));
}

static void report_image(rasp_findings_t *out, const char *path, const char *bundle) {
    rasp_image_class_t cls = rasp_image_class(path, bundle);
    uint16_t c = rasp_image_class_code(cls);
    if (!c) return;
    char d[RASP_DETAIL_LEN];
    rasp_image_detail(d, path);
    if (!already_reported(out, c, d))
        rasp_add(out, c, rasp_image_class_confidence(cls), "%s", d);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// dyld integrity
//
// Per-app hiding (RootHide's Blacklist, Choicy, Shadow) keeps tweaks out of the
// app, but a jailbreak that patches dyld affects every process it spawns.
//
// Only sound when dyld runs from /usr/lib/dyld itself. On iOS 16 and later
// dyld usually re-executes from its copy inside the dyld shared cache
// (MH_DYLIB_IN_CACHE): same LC_UUID, different code layout, so a byte compare
// with the file is meaningless. That case is skipped (it caused a false
// "Jailbroken" on a stock iPhone18,1 / iOS 27.2.1 in 1.3.0). A large diff is
// also treated as a layout mismatch, not a hook. Indicator only, never
// conclusive.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
#if defined(__aarch64__)
typedef struct { const struct section_64 *text; uint64_t text_vmaddr; const uint8_t *uuid; } dyld_layout_t;

static bool dyld_lc_visit(const struct load_command *lc, void *vctx) {
    dyld_layout_t *L = (dyld_layout_t *)vctx;
    if (lc->cmd == LC_UUID) L->uuid = ((const struct uuid_command *)lc)->uuid;
    if (lc->cmd == LC_SEGMENT_64) {
        const struct segment_command_64 *seg = (const struct segment_command_64 *)lc;
        if (strncmp(seg->segname, "__TEXT", 16) == 0) {
            L->text_vmaddr = seg->vmaddr;
            const struct section_64 *s = (const struct section_64 *)(seg + 1);
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < seg->nsects; i++)
                if (strncmp(s[i].sectname, "__text", 16) == 0) L->text = &s[i];
        }
    }
    return true;
}

static uint32_t be32(uint32_t v) { return __builtin_bswap32(v); }

// Finds the slice of `fd` matching cputype/subtype and its LC_UUID.
static bool dyld_file_slice(int fd, const struct mach_header_64 *mem, uint64_t *slice, uint8_t uuid[16]) {
    uint8_t hdr[4096]; int err = 0;
    if (rasp_raw_pread(fd, hdr, sizeof hdr, 0, &err) < (long)sizeof(struct mach_header_64)) return false;
    *slice = 0;
    uint32_t magic; memcpy(&magic, hdr, 4);
    if (magic == FAT_CIGAM) {
        uint32_t n = be32(((const struct fat_header *)hdr)->nfat_arch);
        const struct fat_arch *fa = (const struct fat_arch *)(hdr + sizeof(struct fat_header));
        bool found = false;
        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n && (const uint8_t *)(fa + i + 1) <= hdr + sizeof hdr; i++) {
            if ((cpu_type_t)be32((uint32_t)fa[i].cputype) == mem->cputype &&
                ((cpu_subtype_t)be32((uint32_t)fa[i].cpusubtype) & ~CPU_SUBTYPE_MASK) ==
                    (mem->cpusubtype & ~CPU_SUBTYPE_MASK)) {
                *slice = be32(fa[i].offset); found = true; break;
            }
        }
        if (!found) return false;
        if (rasp_raw_pread(fd, hdr, sizeof hdr, (int64_t)*slice, &err) < (long)sizeof(struct mach_header_64)) return false;
    }
    const struct mach_header_64 *fh = (const struct mach_header_64 *)hdr;
    if (fh->magic != MH_MAGIC_64 || fh->sizeofcmds > sizeof hdr - sizeof *fh) return false;
    const uint8_t *p = (const uint8_t *)(fh + 1);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < fh->ncmds; i++) {
        const struct load_command *lc = (const struct load_command *)p;
        if (lc->cmdsize < sizeof *lc || p + lc->cmdsize > hdr + sizeof hdr) return false;
        if (lc->cmd == LC_UUID) { memcpy(uuid, ((const struct uuid_command *)lc)->uuid, 16); return true; }
        p += lc->cmdsize;
    }
    return false;
}

static void check_dyld(rasp_findings_t *out, const struct dyld_all_image_infos *all) {
    const struct mach_header_64 *mh = (const struct mach_header_64 *)all->dyldImageLoadAddress;
    if (!mh || mh->magic != MH_MAGIC_64) return;
    char path[32];
    if (!rasp_obf_decode(RASP_MISC(RASP_MISC_DYLD), path, sizeof path)) return;

    // dyld_all_image_infos v15+ records the file dyld was loaded from.
    // dyld running from the shared cache: nothing on disk to compare against.
    if (mh->flags & 0x80000000u /* MH_DYLIB_IN_CACHE */) goto done;

    // Cryptex and /System copies are Apple-signed locations, not evidence.
    if (all->version >= 15 && all->dyldPath && all->dyldPath[0] == '/' && strcmp(all->dyldPath, path) != 0 &&
        !has_prefix_obf(all->dyldPath, RASP_MISC_CRYPTEXES) && !has_prefix_obf(all->dyldPath, RASP_MISC_SYSTEM) &&
        !has_prefix_obf(all->dyldPath, RASP_MISC_USR_LIB))
        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_DYLD_MODIFIED, 60, "dyld loaded from %.160s", all->dyldPath);

    dyld_layout_t L = { NULL, 0, NULL };
    rasp_for_each_load_command(mh, dyld_lc_visit, &L);
    if (!L.text || !L.uuid || L.text->size == 0 || L.text->size > (64u << 20)) goto done;

    int err = 0;
    int fd = rasp_raw_open(path, O_RDONLY, 0, &err);
    if (fd < 0) goto done;                         // unreadable: no evidence either way
    uint64_t slice = 0; uint8_t fuuid[16];
    // A different build on disk (e.g. an OS that ships dyld elsewhere) is not
    // evidence; only compare bytes when the UUIDs match.
    if (dyld_file_slice(fd, mh, &slice, fuuid) && memcmp(fuuid, L.uuid, 16) == 0) {
        const uint8_t *mem = (const uint8_t *)mh + (L.text->addr - L.text_vmaddr);
        uint8_t buf[4096];
        uint64_t size = L.text->size, first = UINT64_MAX;
        uint32_t diff = 0, brk = 0;
        for (uint64_t off = 0; off < size; off += sizeof buf) {
            size_t n = (size_t)((size - off) < sizeof buf ? (size - off) : sizeof buf);
            if (rasp_raw_pread(fd, buf, n, (int64_t)(slice + L.text->offset + off), &err) != (long)n) { diff = 0; break; }
            if (memcmp(buf, mem + off, n) == 0) continue;
            for (size_t w = 0; w + 4 <= n; w += 4) {
                uint32_t a, b;
                memcpy(&a, buf + w, 4); memcpy(&b, mem + off + w, 4);
                if (a == b) continue;
                if (first == UINT64_MAX) first = off + w;
                diff++;
                if ((b & 0xFFE0001Fu) == 0xD4200000u) brk++;
            }
        }
        if (diff && diff == brk && brk <= 8)
            // A debugger (lldb sets breakpoints inside dyld for image events).
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_BREAKPOINT_INSN, 80, "dyld: %u software breakpoint(s), first at +0x%llx",
                     brk, (unsigned long long)first);
        else if (diff && diff <= 64)
            // A handful of rewritten instructions looks like hooks. Hundreds or
            // thousands means we are not comparing the same code: no evidence.
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_DYLD_MODIFIED, 70, "dyld __text differs from disk: %u words, first at +0x%llx",
                     diff, (unsigned long long)first);
    }
    rasp_raw_close(fd);
done:
    rasp_secure_zero(path, sizeof path);
}
#endif

void rasp_check_images(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_IMAGES);
    const char *bundle = ctx ? ctx->bundle_path : NULL;

    // Source B: dyld_all_image_infos. infoArray is NULL while dyld is mutating
    // the list; retry briefly.
    uint32_t count_b = 0;
    const struct dyld_all_image_infos *all = all_image_infos();
    if (all) {
        const struct dyld_image_info *arr = NULL;
        for (int tries = 0; tries < 50 && !(arr = all->infoArray); tries++) usleep(200);
        if (arr) {
            count_b = all->infoArrayCount;
            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count_b; i++) {
                const char *p = arr[i].imageFilePath;
                if (p) report_image(out, p, bundle);
                // __interpose in a non-system image = DYLD interposing hook library.
                const struct mach_header_64 *mh = (const struct mach_header_64 *)arr[i].imageLoadAddress;
                if (p && mh && !has_prefix_obf(p, RASP_MISC_SYSTEM) && !has_prefix_obf(p, RASP_MISC_USR_LIB)) {
                    unsigned long sz = 0;
                    if (getsectiondata(mh, "__DATA", "__interpose", &sz) ||
                        getsectiondata(mh, "__DATA_CONST", "__interpose", &sz))
                        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_INTERPOSE_SECTION, 75, "%s (%lu bytes)", p, sz);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Source A: public dyld API.
    uint32_t count_a = _dyld_image_count();
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count_a; i++) {
        const char *p = _dyld_get_image_name(i);
        if (p) report_image(out, p, bundle);
    }

    if (count_b && count_b > count_a)
        rasp_add(out, RASP_F_IMAGE_LIST_MISMATCH, 90, "dyld_all_image_infos=%u _dyld_image_count=%u", count_b, count_a);

#if defined(__aarch64__)
    if (all) check_dyld(out, all);
#endif
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_IMAGES);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Runtime monitor: dyld add-image callback.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
#define MON_RING 32
static os_unfair_lock g_mon_lock = OS_UNFAIR_LOCK_INIT;
static char g_mon_ring[MON_RING][RASP_DETAIL_LEN];
static uint32_t g_mon_head, g_mon_count;
static volatile int g_mon_armed;     // 0 while dyld replays already-loaded images
static int g_mon_installed;

static void on_add_image(const struct mach_header *mh, intptr_t slide) {
    (void)slide;
    if (!__atomic_load_n(&g_mon_armed, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return;
    const char *name = rasp_image_name_for(mh);
    os_unfair_lock_lock(&g_mon_lock);
    strlcpy(g_mon_ring[g_mon_head], name ? name : "<anonymous image>", RASP_DETAIL_LEN);
    g_mon_head = (g_mon_head + 1) % MON_RING;
    if (g_mon_count < MON_RING) g_mon_count++;
    os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_mon_lock);
}

void rasp_monitor_install(void) {
    if (__atomic_exchange_n(&g_mon_installed, 1, __ATOMIC_ACQ_REL)) return;
    _dyld_register_func_for_add_image(on_add_image);   // replays existing images synchronously
    __atomic_store_n(&g_mon_armed, 1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
}

uint32_t rasp_drain_new_images(char *buf, uint32_t max) {
    if (!buf) return 0;
    os_unfair_lock_lock(&g_mon_lock);
    uint32_t n = g_mon_count < max ? g_mon_count : max;
    uint32_t start = (g_mon_head + MON_RING - g_mon_count) % MON_RING;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i++)
        strlcpy(buf + (size_t)i * RASP_DETAIL_LEN, g_mon_ring[(start + i) % MON_RING], RASP_DETAIL_LEN);
    g_mon_count -= n;
    os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_mon_lock);
    return n;
}

```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_internal.h`:

```h
// Internal helpers shared by the C core. Not part of the module's public API.
#ifndef RASP_INTERNAL_H
#define RASP_INTERNAL_H

#include "rasp_core.h"
#include "rasp_strings_table.h"
#include <errno.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/mount.h>
#ifndef __APPLE__
#include <sys/vfs.h>   // struct statfs for host-side tests (Tests/CClassifier)
#endif

#define RASP_INLINE static inline __attribute__((always_inline))

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Raw Darwin syscalls.
//
// On arm64 the call number goes in x16, arguments in x0-x5, `svc #0x80` traps,
// and the kernel sets the carry flag on error with the errno in x0. Each call
// site is force-inlined so there is no single wrapper function for an attacker
// to hook: the `svc` instruction is emitted inside every check. A hook on the
// libc symbol (fishhook, Substrate, Frida Interceptor) does not see these calls.
//
// On x86_64 (Intel simulator) we fall back to libc's syscall(2); the simulator
// is not a security boundary anyway.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
#define RASP_SYS_read        3
#define RASP_SYS_write       4
#define RASP_SYS_open        5
#define RASP_SYS_close       6
#define RASP_SYS_unlink      10
#define RASP_SYS_getpid      20
#define RASP_SYS_ptrace      26
#define RASP_SYS_access      33
#define RASP_SYS_getppid     39
#define RASP_SYS_readlink    58
#define RASP_SYS_pread       153
#define RASP_SYS_csops       169
#define RASP_SYS___sysctl    202
#define RASP_SYS_stat64      338
#define RASP_SYS_lstat64     340
#define RASP_SYS_statfs64    345

#if defined(__aarch64__) && defined(__APPLE__)
RASP_INLINE long rasp_svc(long num, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3, long a4, long a5, int *err) {
    register long x0 __asm__("x0") = a0;
    register long x1 __asm__("x1") = a1;
    register long x2 __asm__("x2") = a2;
    register long x3 __asm__("x3") = a3;
    register long x4 __asm__("x4") = a4;
    register long x5 __asm__("x5") = a5;
    register long x16 __asm__("x16") = num;
    long carry;
    __asm__ volatile(
        "svc #0x80\n\t"
        "cset %[c], cs\n\t"
        : "+r"(x0), "+r"(x1), "+r"(x16), [c] "=&r"(carry)
        : "r"(x2), "r"(x3), "r"(x4), "r"(x5)
        : "memory", "cc");
    if (carry) {
        if (err) *err = (int)x0;
        return -1;
    }
    if (err) *err = 0;
    return x0;
}
#else
#include <unistd.h>
#pragma clang diagnostic push
#pragma clang diagnostic ignored "-Wdeprecated-declarations"
RASP_INLINE long rasp_svc(long num, long a0, long a1, long a2, long a3, long a4, long a5, int *err) {
    errno = 0;
    long r = syscall((int)num, a0, a1, a2, a3, a4, a5);
    if (err) *err = (r == -1) ? errno : 0;
    return r;
}
#pragma clang diagnostic pop
#endif

#define rasp_sys0(n, e)                 rasp_svc((n), 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, (e))
#define rasp_sys1(n, a, e)              rasp_svc((n), (long)(a), 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, (e))
#define rasp_sys2(n, a, b, e)           rasp_svc((n), (long)(a), (long)(b), 0, 0, 0, 0, (e))
#define rasp_sys3(n, a, b, c, e)        rasp_svc((n), (long)(a), (long)(b), (long)(c), 0, 0, 0, (e))
#define rasp_sys4(n, a, b, c, d, e)     rasp_svc((n), (long)(a), (long)(b), (long)(c), (long)(d), 0, 0, (e))
#define rasp_sys6(n, a, b, c, d, f, g, e) rasp_svc((n), (long)(a), (long)(b), (long)(c), (long)(d), (long)(f), (long)(g), (e))

RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_stat(const char *p, struct stat *st, int *err)  { return (int)rasp_sys2(RASP_SYS_stat64, p, st, err); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_lstat(const char *p, struct stat *st, int *err) { return (int)rasp_sys2(RASP_SYS_lstat64, p, st, err); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_access(const char *p, int mode, int *err)       { return (int)rasp_sys2(RASP_SYS_access, p, mode, err); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_open(const char *p, int flags, int mode, int *err) { return (int)rasp_sys3(RASP_SYS_open, p, flags, mode, err); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_close(int fd)                                   { int e; return (int)rasp_sys1(RASP_SYS_close, fd, &e); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_unlink(const char *p, int *err)                 { return (int)rasp_sys1(RASP_SYS_unlink, p, err); }
RASP_INLINE long rasp_raw_readlink(const char *p, char *buf, size_t n, int *err) { return rasp_sys3(RASP_SYS_readlink, p, buf, n, err); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_statfs(const char *p, struct statfs *s, int *err) { return (int)rasp_sys2(RASP_SYS_statfs64, p, s, err); }
RASP_INLINE long rasp_raw_pread(int fd, void *buf, size_t n, int64_t off, int *err) {
    return rasp_sys4(RASP_SYS_pread, fd, buf, n, off, err);
}
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_getpid(void)  { int e; return (int)rasp_sys0(RASP_SYS_getpid, &e); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_getppid(void) { int e; return (int)rasp_sys0(RASP_SYS_getppid, &e); }
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_csops(int pid, unsigned ops, void *buf, size_t sz, int *err) {
    return (int)rasp_sys4(RASP_SYS_csops, pid, ops, buf, sz, err);
}
RASP_INLINE int rasp_raw_sysctl(int *mib, unsigned cnt, void *old, size_t *oldlen, void *nw, size_t nwlen, int *err) {
    return (int)rasp_sys6(RASP_SYS___sysctl, mib, cnt, old, oldlen, nw, nwlen, err);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Findings
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
void rasp_add(rasp_findings_t *out, uint16_t code, uint8_t confidence, const char *fmt, ...)
    __attribute__((format(printf, 4, 5)));

#define RASP_BEGIN(out, bit)  do { (out)->checks_started |= (bit); } while (0)
#define RASP_END(out, bit)    do { (out)->checks_completed |= (bit); } while (0)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Obfuscated strings
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
#define RASP_STR_MAX 256
#define RASP_MISC(i) ((uint16_t)(RASP_LIST_MISC_START + (i)))
enum {
    RASP_MISC_DEVMODE_SYSCTL = 0, RASP_MISC_SNAPSHOT_PREFIX, RASP_MISC_BUNDLE_ROOT_PRIVATE,

};
```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_rules.c`:

```c
// Extra indicators supplied by signed remote rules. Additive only: they are
// consulted *in addition to* the compiled-in obfuscated lists.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <os/lock.h>

#define RULES_MAX 256

static os_unfair_lock g_rules_lock = OS_UNFAIR_LOCK_INIT;
static char *g_paths[RULES_MAX];
static uint32_t g_path_count;
static char *g_markers[RULES_MAX];
static uint32_t g_marker_count;

static void replace(char **dst, uint32_t *count, const char *const *src, uint32_t n) {
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < *count; i++) { free(dst[i]); dst[i] = NULL; }
    *count = 0;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n && i < RULES_MAX; i++) {
        if (!src[i] || !src[i][0] || strlen(src[i]) >= RASP_STR_MAX) continue;
        dst[(*count)++] = strdup(src[i]);
    }
}

void rasp_rules_set(const char *const *paths, uint32_t path_count,
                    const char *const *markers, uint32_t marker_count) {
    os_unfair_lock_lock(&g_rules_lock);
    replace(g_paths, &g_path_count, paths, paths ? path_count : 0);
    replace(g_markers, &g_marker_count, markers, markers ? marker_count : 0);
    os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_rules_lock);
}

uint32_t rasp_rules_copy_path(uint32_t i, char *out, size_t cap) {
    os_unfair_lock_lock(&g_rules_lock);
    uint32_t n = g_path_count;
    if (i < n) strlcpy(out, g_paths[i], cap);
    os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_rules_lock);
    return n;
}

bool rasp_rules_marker_match(const char *path) {
    bool hit = false;
    os_unfair_lock_lock(&g_rules_lock);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < g_marker_count && !hit; i++)
        hit = rasp_strcasestr(path, g_markers[i]) != NULL;
    os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_rules_lock);
    return hit;
}

```

`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_self.c`:

```c
// Self-integrity: detect code patched in memory after start-up.
//
// At baseline time we record
//   * SHA-256 of the main image's __TEXT,__text (where this core is linked);
//   * the first 32 bytes of every hook-target function and every RASP entry
//     point.
// rasp_check_self() recomputes both. Inline hooks installed after launch
// (Frida Interceptor, late Substrate/ElleKit hooks, debugger breakpoints)
// change these bytes even when the patch uses an encoding the prologue
// decoder does not recognise.
//
// Limitation: anything patched *before* the baseline (e.g. by a dylib
// constructor that runs before ours) is baked into the baseline. The static
// prologue decoder, the server-side __TEXT hash comparison and App Attest
// cover that case.

#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <dlfcn.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <os/lock.h>
#include <CommonCrypto/CommonDigest.h>
#include <mach-o/getsect.h>
#if __has_include(<ptrauth.h>)
#include <ptrauth.h>
#endif

#define SNAP_BYTES 32
#define SNAP_MAX   48

typedef struct { const void *addr; uint8_t bytes[SNAP_BYTES]; char label[32]; } snap_t;

static os_unfair_lock g_self_lock = OS_UNFAIR_LOCK_INIT;
static int g_baselined;
static uint8_t g_text_hash[CC_SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH];
static bool g_have_text_hash;
static snap_t g_snaps[SNAP_MAX];
static uint32_t g_snap_count;

static inline const void *strip_ptr(const void *p) {
#if __has_feature(ptrauth_intrinsics)
    return ptrauth_strip(p, ptrauth_key_function_pointer);
#else
    return p;
#endif
}

static bool hash_main_text(uint8_t out[CC_SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH]) {
    intptr_t slide = 0;
    const struct mach_header_64 *mh = rasp_main_header(&slide);
    unsigned long size = 0;
    const uint8_t *text = mh ? getsectiondata(mh, SEG_TEXT, SECT_TEXT, &size) : NULL;
    if (!text || !size) return false;
    CC_SHA256(text, (CC_LONG)size, out);
    return true;
}

static void add_snap(const void *fn, const char *label) {
    if (!fn || g_snap_count >= SNAP_MAX) return;
    snap_t *s = &g_snaps[g_snap_count++];
    s->addr = strip_ptr(fn);
    memcpy(s->bytes, s->addr, SNAP_BYTES);
    strlcpy(s->label, label, sizeof s->label);
}

void rasp_integrity_baseline(void) {
    os_unfair_lock_lock(&g_self_lock);
    if (g_baselined) { os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_self_lock); return; }
    g_have_text_hash = hash_main_text(g_text_hash);

    char name[64];
    for (uint16_t i = 0; i < RASP_LIST_HOOK_TARGETS_COUNT; i++) {
        size_t n = rasp_obf_decode(RASP_LIST_HOOK_TARGETS_START + i, name, sizeof name);
        if (!n) continue;
        const char *sym = (n > 2 && name[0] == 'S' && name[1] == ':') ? name + 2 : name;
        add_snap(dlsym(RTLD_DEFAULT, sym), sym);
        rasp_secure_zero(name, sizeof name);
    }
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_filesystem, "core:fs");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_sandbox, "core:sandbox");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_images, "core:images");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_hooks, "core:hooks");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_debugger, "core:debugger");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_frida, "core:frida");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_codesign, "core:codesign");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_environment, "core:env");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_check_canary, "core:canary");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_classify_image, "core:classify");
    add_snap((const void *)&rasp_obf_decode, "core:decode");
    g_baselined = 1;
    os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_self_lock);
}

void rasp_check_self(const rasp_context_t *ctx, rasp_findings_t *out) {
    (void)ctx;
    RASP_BEGIN(out, RASP_CHECK_SELF);
    rasp_integrity_baseline();   // no-op after the first call
    os_unfair_lock_lock(&g_self_lock);
    if (g_have_text_hash) {
        uint8_t now[CC_SHA256_DIGEST_LENGTH];
        if (hash_main_text(now) && memcmp(now, g_text_hash, sizeof now) != 0)
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_TEXT_MODIFIED, 97, "main __text changed since start-up");
    }
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < g_snap_count; i++) {
        if (memcmp(g_snaps[i].addr, g_snaps[i].bytes, SNAP_BYTES) != 0) {
            bool core = strncmp(g_snaps[i].label, "core:", 5) == 0;
            rasp_add(out, RASP_F_PROLOGUE_CHANGED, core ? 99 : 95, "%s patched after start-up", g_snaps[i].label);
        }
    }
    os_unfair_lock_unlock(&g_self_lock);
    RASP_END(out, RASP_CHECK_SELF);
}

```
