`Sources/CIntegrityCore/rasp_util.c`:

```c
#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <ctype.h>

void rasp_add(rasp_findings_t *out, uint16_t code, uint8_t confidence, const char *fmt, ...) {
    if (!out) return;
    if (out->count >= RASP_MAX_FINDINGS) { out->dropped++; return; }
    rasp_finding_t *f = &out->items[out->count++];
    f->code = code;
    f->confidence = confidence > 100 ? 100 : confidence;
    f->_pad = 0;
    va_list ap;
    va_start(ap, fmt);
    vsnprintf(f->detail, sizeof f->detail, fmt, ap);
    va_end(ap);
}

size_t rasp_obf_decode(uint16_t index, char *out, size_t cap) {
    if (!out || cap == 0 || index >= RASP_OBF_ENTRY_COUNT) return 0;
    const rasp_obf_entry_t *e = &rasp_obf_table[index];
    size_t n = e->length;
    if (n + 1 > cap) return 0;
    uint8_t k = e->key;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        out[i] = (char)(rasp_obf_blob[e->offset + i] ^ k);
        k = (uint8_t)(k + e->step);
    }
    out[n] = '\0';
    return n;
}

void rasp_secure_zero(void *p, size_t n) {
    volatile uint8_t *v = (volatile uint8_t *)p;
    while (n--) *v++ = 0;
}

const char *rasp_strcasestr(const char *hay, const char *needle) {
    if (!hay || !needle) return NULL;
    size_t nl = strlen(needle);
    if (nl == 0) return hay;
    for (; *hay; hay++) {
        size_t i = 0;
        while (i < nl && hay[i] && tolower((unsigned char)hay[i]) == tolower((unsigned char)needle[i])) i++;
        if (i == nl) return hay;
    }
    return NULL;
}

void rasp_random_token(char out[17]) {
    static const char hex[] = "0123456789abcdef";
    uint8_t b[8];
    arc4random_buf(b, sizeof b);
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        out[i * 2] = hex[b[i] >> 4];
        out[i * 2 + 1] = hex[b[i] & 0xF];
    }
    out[16] = '\0';
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/Attestation.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import CryptoKit
import DeviceCheck
import Security

/// One-time App Attest enrolment payload for the server.
public struct AppAttestEnrolment: Codable, Sendable {
    public let keyId: String
    public let attestationObject: Data     // CBOR, verify against Apple App Attest root CA
}

/// A report bound to a server nonce and signed by hardware-backed keys.
///
/// Server verification:
///  1. `clientDataHash == SHA256(payload || nonce)` and the nonce is the one it issued (single use, short TTL).
///  2. If `appAttestAssertion` is present: verify it with the public key from enrolment, check the
///     counter is strictly increasing and the RP ID hash matches your App ID. This proves the payload
///     came from a genuine, unmodified instance of your app on a real Apple device.
///  3. If `secureEnclaveSignature` is present: verify ECDSA-P256-SHA256 over `clientDataHash` with the
///     enrolled SE public key. Proves continuity of the device-bound key, not app genuineness.
///  4. Re-score `payload.signals` under server policy; never trust `payload.verdict` alone.
public struct SealedReport: Codable, Sendable {
    public let payload: Data                       // canonical JSON of IntegrityReport
    public let nonce: Data
    public let clientDataHash: Data                // SHA256(payload || nonce)
    public let appAttestKeyId: String?
    public let appAttestAssertion: Data?
    public let secureEnclaveSignature: Data?       // DER ECDSA
    public let secureEnclavePublicKey: Data?       // X9.63 uncompressed
    public let attestationError: String?
}

final class AttestationClient: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let keychainService = "integritykit.keys"
    private let appAttestAccount = "appattest.keyid"
    private let seAccount = "secureenclave.p256"

    // MARK: App Attest

    /// `.enrolled` only means a key ID is stored locally; the server decides
    /// whether that key was ever accepted.
    var appAttestStatus: AppAttestStatus {
        guard DCAppAttestService.shared.isSupported else { return .unsupported }
        return Keychain.read(service: keychainService, account: appAttestAccount) == nil ? .notEnrolled : .enrolled
    }

    func attestKey(challenge: Data) async throws -> AppAttestEnrolment {
        let service = DCAppAttestService.shared
        guard service.isSupported else { throw IntegrityError.appAttestUnsupported }
        do {
            let keyId = try await existingOrNewKeyId(service)
            let hash = Data(SHA256.hash(data: challenge))
            let obj = try await service.attestKey(keyId, clientDataHash: hash)
            return AppAttestEnrolment(keyId: keyId, attestationObject: obj)
        } catch let e as DCError {
            // A build without the App Attest entitlement (for example a free
            // Personal Team) fails here. Drop any stale key so a later, entitled
            // build starts clean, and report "unavailable" instead of crashing.
            Keychain.delete(service: keychainService, account: appAttestAccount)
            throw IntegrityError.appAttestUnavailable("DCError \(e.code.rawValue): \(e.localizedDescription)")
        }
    }

    private func existingOrNewKeyId(_ service: DCAppAttestService) async throws -> String {
        if let data = Keychain.read(service: keychainService, account: appAttestAccount),
           let id = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) { return id }
        let id = try await service.generateKey()
        Keychain.write(Data(id.utf8), service: keychainService, account: appAttestAccount)
        return id
    }

    func seal(_ report: IntegrityReport, nonce: Data) async throws -> SealedReport {
        let payload = try report.canonicalJSON()
        let clientDataHash = Data(SHA256.hash(data: payload + nonce))

        var keyId: String?
        var assertion: Data?
        var errors: [String] = []
        let service = DCAppAttestService.shared
        if service.isSupported,
           let data = Keychain.read(service: keychainService, account: appAttestAccount),
           let id = String(data: data, encoding: .utf8) {
            do {
                assertion = try await service.generateAssertion(id, clientDataHash: clientDataHash)
                keyId = id
            } catch {
                // DCError.invalidKey: key lost (restore/reinstall) -> re-enrol.
                errors.append("appattest: \(error.localizedDescription)")
            }
        } else {
            errors.append(service.isSupported ? "appattest: not enrolled" : "appattest: unsupported")
        }
        // Without an assertion the server must treat App Attest as absent; the
        // Secure Enclave seal below still works on any team, including a Personal Team.

        var seSig: Data?
        var sePub: Data?
        if SecureEnclave.isAvailable {
            do {
                let key = try secureEnclaveKey()
                seSig = try key.signature(for: clientDataHash).derRepresentation
                sePub = key.publicKey.x963Representation
            } catch {
                errors.append("secureenclave: \(error.localizedDescription)")
            }
        } else {
            errors.append("secureenclave: unavailable")
        }

        return SealedReport(payload: payload, nonce: nonce, clientDataHash: clientDataHash,
                            appAttestKeyId: keyId, appAttestAssertion: assertion,
                            secureEnclaveSignature: seSig, secureEnclavePublicKey: sePub,
                            attestationError: errors.isEmpty ? nil : errors.joined(separator: "; "))
    }

    // MARK: Secure Enclave

    /// The private key never leaves the Secure Enclave; `dataRepresentation` is an
    /// encrypted blob only that device's SE can use.
    private func secureEnclaveKey() throws -> SecureEnclave.P256.Signing.PrivateKey {
        if let blob = Keychain.read(service: keychainService, account: seAccount) {
            return try SecureEnclave.P256.Signing.PrivateKey(dataRepresentation: blob)
        }
        var error: Unmanaged<CFError>?
        guard let access = SecAccessControlCreateWithFlags(nil, kSecAttrAccessibleAfterFirstUnlockThisDeviceOnly,
                                                           .privateKeyUsage, &error) else {
            throw error!.takeRetainedValue() as Error
        }
        let key = try SecureEnclave.P256.Signing.PrivateKey(accessControl: access)
        Keychain.write(key.dataRepresentation, service: keychainService, account: seAccount)
        return key
    }

    // MARK: DeviceCheck

    func deviceCheckToken() async throws -> Data {
        guard DCDevice.current.isSupported else { throw IntegrityError.deviceCheckUnsupported }
        return try await DCDevice.current.generateToken()
    }
}

public enum AppAttestStatus: String, Codable, Sendable {
    /// Device or OS cannot do App Attest (simulator, Mac, virtual device).
    case unsupported
    /// Supported, but no key enrolled yet, or the build lacks the entitlement.
    case notEnrolled
    case enrolled
}

public enum IntegrityError: Error, Sendable {
    case appAttestUnsupported
    /// App Attest failed, typically because the build lacks the
    /// `com.apple.developer.devicecheck.appattest-environment` entitlement.
    case appAttestUnavailable(String)
    case deviceCheckUnsupported
}

enum Keychain {
    static func read(service: String, account: String) -> Data? {
        let q: [String: Any] = [kSecClass as String: kSecClassGenericPassword,
                                kSecAttrService as String: service,
                                kSecAttrAccount as String: account,
                                kSecReturnData as String: true,
                                kSecMatchLimit as String: kSecMatchLimitOne]
        var out: CFTypeRef?
        return SecItemCopyMatching(q as CFDictionary, &out) == errSecSuccess ? out as? Data : nil
    }

    static func delete(service: String, account: String) {
        let q: [String: Any] = [kSecClass as String: kSecClassGenericPassword,
                                kSecAttrService as String: service,
                                kSecAttrAccount as String: account]
        SecItemDelete(q as CFDictionary)
    }

    @discardableResult
    static func write(_ data: Data, service: String, account: String) -> Bool {
        let base: [String: Any] = [kSecClass as String: kSecClassGenericPassword,
                                   kSecAttrService as String: service,
                                   kSecAttrAccount as String: account]
        SecItemDelete(base as CFDictionary)
        var add = base
        add[kSecValueData as String] = data
        add[kSecAttrAccessible as String] = kSecAttrAccessibleAfterFirstUnlockThisDeviceOnly
        return SecItemAdd(add as CFDictionary, nil) == errSecSuccess
    }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/Checks/AppIntegrityChecks.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit
import LocalAuthentication
import CryptoKit
import DeviceCheck

/// Swift-side checks that need Foundation/UIKit or the configuration.
/// They always report what they see; `IntegrityConfiguration.treatment(for:)`
/// decides whether a signal is scored or informational.
struct AppIntegrityChecks {
    let configuration: IntegrityConfiguration
    let build: AppBuildFacts
    let osMajor: Int
    let developerModeSysctl: Int32

    func run() -> [RawSignal] {
        var s: [RawSignal] = []
        s += provisioning()
        s += bundleIdentity()
        s += encryption()
        s += developerModeInference()
        s += textHash()
        if configuration.probeURLSchemes { s += Self.probeJailbreakURLSchemes() }
        return s
    }

    // MARK: Provisioning profile

    struct Profile {
        let teamIdentifiers: [String]
        let provisionsAllDevices: Bool
        let hasProvisionedDevices: Bool
        let getTaskAllow: Bool
    }

    /// embedded.mobileprovision is a CMS envelope with a plain XML plist inside.
    static func loadProfile() -> Profile? {
        guard let url = Bundle.main.url(forResource: "embedded", withExtension: "mobileprovision"),
              let data = try? Data(contentsOf: url),
              let start = data.range(of: Data("<?xml".utf8)),
              let end = data.range(of: Data("</plist>".utf8), in: start.lowerBound..<data.endIndex)
        else { return nil }
        let plistData = data.subdata(in: start.lowerBound..<end.upperBound)
        guard let plist = try? PropertyListSerialization.propertyList(from: plistData, format: nil) as? [String: Any]
        else { return nil }
        let ent = plist["Entitlements"] as? [String: Any] ?? [:]
        return Profile(teamIdentifiers: plist["TeamIdentifier"] as? [String] ?? [],
                       provisionsAllDevices: plist["ProvisionsAllDevices"] as? Bool ?? false,
                       hasProvisionedDevices: plist["ProvisionedDevices"] != nil,
                       getTaskAllow: ent["get-task-allow"] as? Bool ?? false)
    }

    /// Best-effort classification of how this binary was signed.
    static func detectDistribution(profile: Profile?, cryptID: Int32, csFlags: UInt32) -> String {
        #if targetEnvironment(simulator)
        return "simulator"
        #else
        guard let p = profile else { return cryptID == 1 ? "appStore" : "unknown" }
        if p.provisionsAllDevices { return "enterprise" }
        if p.getTaskAllow || csFlags & 0x4 != 0 { return "development" }
        if p.hasProvisionedDevices { return "adHoc" }
        return "unknown"
        #endif
    }

    private func provisioning() -> [RawSignal] {
        guard let profile = Self.loadProfile() else { return [] }
        var s: [RawSignal] = [.init(.provisioningProfilePresent, 90, "signed as \(build.detectedDistribution)")]
        if profile.provisionsAllDevices {
            s.append(.init(.enterpriseProfile, 90, "ProvisionsAllDevices=true"))
        }
        if let expected = configuration.expectedTeamIdentifier,
           !profile.teamIdentifiers.isEmpty, !profile.teamIdentifiers.contains(expected) {
            s.append(.init(.teamIdentifierMismatch, 95, "profile team \(profile.teamIdentifiers.joined(separator: ","))"))
        }
        return s
    }

    // MARK: Bundle identity and location

    private func bundleIdentity() -> [RawSignal] {
        var s: [RawSignal] = []
        if let expected = configuration.expectedBundleIdentifier, build.bundleIdentifier != expected {
            s.append(.init(.bundleIdentifierMismatch, 95, "bundle id \(build.bundleIdentifier)"))
        }
        #if !targetEnvironment(simulator)
        if !ProcessInfo.processInfo.isiOSAppOnMac {
            let path = (Bundle.main.bundlePath as NSString).resolvingSymlinksInPath
            let base: UInt16 = ObfuscatedList.misc.lowerBound
            var roots: [String] = []
            if let r = CoreBridge.decode(base + 2) { roots.append(r) }
            if let r = CoreBridge.decode(base + 3) { roots.append(r) }
            if !roots.contains(where: { path.hasPrefix($0) }) {
                s.append(.init(.bundlePathUnexpected, 85, path))
            }
        }
        #endif
        return s
    }

    // MARK: FairPlay

    private func encryption() -> [RawSignal] {
        #if targetEnvironment(simulator)
        return []
        #else
        // App Store/TestFlight binaries are FairPlay-encrypted (cryptid=1).
        // Xcode, ad hoc and enterprise builds are not, and neither is a
        // dumped-and-resigned IPA. Scored only when expectedDistribution == .appStore.
        if build.cryptID == 0 { return [.init(.binaryDecrypted, 90, "cryptid=0")] }
        if build.cryptID == -1 { return [.init(.binaryDecrypted, 60, "no LC_ENCRYPTION_INFO")] }
        return []
        #endif
    }

    // MARK: Developer Mode inference (posture)

    private func developerModeInference() -> [RawSignal] {
        let getTaskAllow = build.codeSigningFlags & 0x4 != 0
        guard osMajor >= 16, getTaskAllow, developerModeSysctl != 1 else { return [] }
        return [.init(.developerModeInferred, 85, "get-task-allow on iOS \(osMajor)")]
    }

    // MARK: __TEXT hash

    private func textHash() -> [RawSignal] {
        guard let expected = configuration.expectedTextSHA256?.lowercased(), !build.textSHA256.isEmpty,
              expected != build.textSHA256 else { return [] }
        return [.init(.textHashMismatch, 90, "text sha256 \(build.textSHA256.prefix(16))...")]
    }

    // MARK: URL schemes

    /// Only schemes that no stock Apple app registers on iOS 15-27. Each
    /// candidate must be audited against stock apps before being added (see
    /// Scripts/gen_strings.py). TrollStore's `apple-magnifier` is excluded:
    /// the stock Magnifier app owns it.
    static func probeJailbreakURLSchemes() -> [RawSignal] {
        var schemes: [String] = ObfuscatedList.urlSchemes.compactMap(CoreBridge.decode)
        for extra in RulesRuntime.extraURLSchemes where !schemes.contains(extra) { schemes.append(extra) }
        let probe: () -> [RawSignal] = {
            schemes.compactMap { scheme in
                guard let url = URL(string: "\(scheme)://"), UIApplication.shared.canOpenURL(url) else { return nil }
                return RawSignal(.jailbreakURLScheme, 80, "\(scheme)://")
            }
        }
        if Thread.isMainThread { return probe() }
        return DispatchQueue.main.sync(execute: probe)
    }
}

/// Swift-side equivalent of a C finding.
struct RawSignal {
    let code: FindingCode
    let confidence: UInt8
    let detail: String
    init(_ code: FindingCode, _ confidence: UInt8, _ detail: String) {
        self.code = code; self.confidence = confidence; self.detail = detail
    }
}

enum PostureProbe {
    static func collect(osVersion: String, osMajor: Int, model: String, devModeSysctl: Int32,
                        csFlags: UInt32, ddiMounted: Bool) -> DevicePosture {
        #if targetEnvironment(simulator)
        let isSim = true
        #else
        let isSim = false
        #endif
        let devMode: DevicePosture.TriState
        if osMajor < 16 { devMode = .off }                       // does not exist before 16
        else if devModeSysctl == 1 { devMode = .on }
        else if devModeSysctl == 0 { devMode = .off }
        else if csFlags & 0x4 != 0 { devMode = .on }             // get-task-allow app running => on
        else { devMode = .unknown }

        let ctx = LAContext()
        var err: NSError?
        let canOwnerAuth = ctx.canEvaluatePolicy(.deviceOwnerAuthentication, error: &err)
        let passcode: DevicePosture.TriState
        if canOwnerAuth { passcode = .on }
        else if let e = err as? LAError, e.code == .passcodeNotSet { passcode = .off }
        else { passcode = .unknown }

        let bioCtx = LAContext()
        _ = bioCtx.canEvaluatePolicy(.deviceOwnerAuthenticationWithBiometrics, error: nil)
        let biometry: String
        switch bioCtx.biometryType {
        case .faceID: biometry = "Face ID"
        case .touchID: biometry = "Touch ID"
        case .none: biometry = "none"
        default: biometry = "other"
        }

        return DevicePosture(model: model, osVersion: osVersion, osMajor: osMajor, isSimulator: isSim,
                             isiOSAppOnMac: ProcessInfo.processInfo.isiOSAppOnMac,
                             developerMode: devMode, developerDiskImageMounted: ddiMounted,
                             passcodeSet: passcode, biometry: biometry,
                             secureEnclaveAvailable: SecureEnclave.isAvailable,
                             appAttestSupported: DCAppAttestService.shared.isSupported)
    }
}
```

`Sources/KaiselKit/Checks/CoreBridge.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import CIntegrityCore

/// Thin bridge over the C core. Owns the findings buffer and context lifetime.
struct RawFinding {
    let code: UInt16
    let confidence: UInt8
    let detail: String
}

enum CoreCheck: CaseIterable {
    case filesystem, sandbox, images, hooks, debugger, frida, codesign, environment, devmode, canary, selfIntegrity

    var bit: UInt32 {
        switch self {
        case .filesystem:  return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_FILESYSTEM)
        case .sandbox:     return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_SANDBOX)
        case .images:      return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_IMAGES)
        case .hooks:       return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_HOOKS)
        case .debugger:    return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_DEBUGGER)
        case .frida:       return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_FRIDA)
        case .codesign:    return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_CODESIGN)
        case .environment: return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_ENVIRONMENT)
        case .devmode:     return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_DEVMODE)
        case .canary:      return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_CANARY)
        case .selfIntegrity: return UInt32(RASP_CHECK_SELF)
        }
    }

    var name: String { String(describing: self) }

    func run(_ ctx: UnsafePointer<rasp_context_t>, _ out: UnsafeMutablePointer<rasp_findings_t>) {
        switch self {
        case .filesystem:  rasp_check_filesystem(ctx, out)
        case .sandbox:     rasp_check_sandbox(ctx, out)
        case .images:      rasp_check_images(ctx, out)
        case .hooks:       rasp_check_hooks(ctx, out)
        case .debugger:    rasp_check_debugger(ctx, out)
        case .frida:       rasp_check_frida(ctx, out)
        case .codesign:    rasp_check_codesign(ctx, out)
        case .environment: rasp_check_environment(ctx, out)
        case .devmode:     rasp_check_devmode(ctx, out)
        case .canary:      rasp_check_canary(ctx, out)
        case .selfIntegrity: rasp_check_self(ctx, out)
        }
    }
}

struct CoreRunResult {
    var findings: [RawFinding]
    var started: UInt32
    var completed: UInt32
    var dropped: UInt32
}

enum CoreBridge {
    static func run(checks: [CoreCheck], configuration: IntegrityConfiguration) -> CoreRunResult {
        let bundlePath = strdup((Bundle.main.bundlePath as NSString).resolvingSymlinksInPath)
        let execPath = strdup(Bundle.main.executablePath ?? "")
        let tmp = strdup((NSTemporaryDirectory() as NSString).standardizingPath)
        defer { free(bundlePath); free(execPath); free(tmp) }

        let out = UnsafeMutablePointer<rasp_findings_t>.allocate(capacity: 1)
        memset(out, 0, MemoryLayout<rasp_findings_t>.size)
        defer {
            memset(out, 0, MemoryLayout<rasp_findings_t>.size)
            out.deallocate()
        }

        configuration.fridaPorts.withUnsafeBufferPointer { ports in
            var ctx = rasp_context_t(
                bundle_path: bundlePath,
                executable_path: execPath,
                container_tmp: tmp,
                frida_ports: ports.baseAddress,
                frida_port_count: UInt32(ports.count),
                probe_timeout_ms: configuration.portProbeTimeoutMs,
                allow_fork_probe: configuration.allowForkProbe,
                scan_memory: configuration.scanMemory)
            withUnsafePointer(to: &ctx) { ctxPtr in
                // Randomised order: an attacker cannot rely on a fixed call
                // sequence to time a hook or to patch "the third check".
                for check in checks.shuffled() { check.run(ctxPtr, out) }
            }
        }
        return CoreRunResult(findings: readFindings(out),
                             started: out.pointee.checks_started,
                             completed: out.pointee.checks_completed,
                             dropped: out.pointee.dropped)
    }

    private static func readFindings(_ out: UnsafeMutablePointer<rasp_findings_t>) -> [RawFinding] {
        let count = min(Int(out.pointee.count), Int(RASP_MAX_FINDINGS))
        return withUnsafeMutablePointer(to: &out.pointee.items) { tuplePtr in
            tuplePtr.withMemoryRebound(to: rasp_finding_t.self, capacity: Int(RASP_MAX_FINDINGS)) { items in
                (0..<count).map { i in
                    var item = items[i]
                    let detail = withUnsafePointer(to: &item.detail) {
                        $0.withMemoryRebound(to: CChar.self, capacity: Int(RASP_DETAIL_LEN)) { String(cString: $0) }
                    }
                    return RawFinding(code: item.code, confidence: item.confidence, detail: detail)
                }
            }
        }
    }

    static func facts() -> rasp_facts_t {
        var f = rasp_facts_t()
        rasp_collect_facts(&f)
        return f
    }

    static func textHash(_ f: inout rasp_facts_t) -> String {
        withUnsafePointer(to: &f.text_sha256) {
            $0.withMemoryRebound(to: CChar.self, capacity: 65) { String(cString: $0) }
        }
    }

    /// Decodes one obfuscated string. Callers should not retain the result longer than needed.
    static func decode(_ index: UInt16) -> String? {
        var buf = [CChar](repeating: 0, count: 256)
        let n = rasp_obf_decode(index, &buf, buf.count)
        defer { rasp_secure_zero(&buf, buf.count) }
        return n > 0 ? String(cString: buf) : nil
    }

    static func classifyImage(_ path: String) -> FindingCode? {
        classifyImageEvidence(path)?.code
    }

    /// Finding code and confidence for an image path, nil when trusted or unknown.
    static func classifyImageEvidence(_ path: String) -> (code: FindingCode, confidence: UInt8)? {
        let bundle = (Bundle.main.bundlePath as NSString).resolvingSymlinksInPath
        let cls = rasp_image_class(path, bundle)
        let raw = rasp_image_class_code(cls)
        guard raw != 0, let code = FindingCode(rawValue: raw) else { return nil }
        return (code, rasp_image_class_confidence(cls))
    }

    static func drainNewImages() -> [String] {
        let slots = 32
        let stride = Int(RASP_DETAIL_LEN)
        var buf = [CChar](repeating: 0, count: slots * stride)
        let n = Int(rasp_drain_new_images(&buf, UInt32(slots)))
        return (0..<n).map { i in
            buf.withUnsafeBufferPointer { String(cString: $0.baseAddress! + i * stride) }
        }
    }
}
```

`Sources/KaiselKit/Configuration.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

public struct IntegrityConfiguration: Sendable {
    public enum Distribution: String, Codable, Sendable {
        /// App Store or TestFlight: FairPlay-encrypted, no embedded profile, no get-task-allow.
        case appStore
        /// Apple Developer Enterprise Program in-house distribution.
        case enterprise
        /// Ad hoc builds.
        case adHoc
        /// Xcode development builds.
        case development
    }

    /// The distribution you *expect* this binary to have in production.
    ///
    /// `nil` (default): app-build signals (provisioning profile, get-task-allow,
    /// FairPlay, team ID) are reported as **informational** and never affect
    /// any verdict. Use this for development, ad hoc and demo builds.
    ///
    /// `.appStore` etc.: app-build signals that contradict the expectation are
    /// scored into `appVerdict`, which then also feeds the overall verdict.
    public var expectedDistribution: Distribution?
    /// 10-character Apple team ID the app must be signed with (checked against
    /// the embedded profile when present).
    public var expectedTeamIdentifier: String?
    public var expectedBundleIdentifier: String?
    /// SHA-256 of the main executable's __TEXT,__text recorded at build time.
    public var expectedTextSHA256: String?

    /// Loopback ports probed for frida-server (27042 is Frida's default).
    public var fridaPorts: [UInt16]
    public var portProbeTimeoutMs: UInt32
    /// Scan the VM map for anonymous executable memory.
    public var scanMemory: Bool
    /// fork() probe (child exits immediately).
    public var allowForkProbe: Bool
    /// Task exception ports your own crash reporter registers.
    public var allowedExceptionPorts: Int32
    /// Probe jailbreak package-manager URL schemes (needs LSApplicationQueriesSchemes).
    public var probeURLSchemes: Bool
    /// Debugger evidence is expected while developing; discount it.
    public var debuggerExpected: Bool
    /// Per-code weight overrides (0...1).
    public var weightOverrides: [FindingCode: Double]
    /// Codes to drop entirely.
    public var suppressedCodes: Set<FindingCode>

    public init(expectedDistribution: Distribution? = nil,
                expectedTeamIdentifier: String? = nil,
                expectedBundleIdentifier: String? = nil,
                expectedTextSHA256: String? = nil,
                fridaPorts: [UInt16] = [27042, 27043],
                portProbeTimeoutMs: UInt32 = 120,
                scanMemory: Bool = true,
                allowForkProbe: Bool = true,
                allowedExceptionPorts: Int32 = 0,
                probeURLSchemes: Bool = true,
                debuggerExpected: Bool = false,
                weightOverrides: [FindingCode: Double] = [:],
                suppressedCodes: Set<FindingCode> = []) {
        self.expectedDistribution = expectedDistribution
        self.expectedTeamIdentifier = expectedTeamIdentifier
        self.expectedBundleIdentifier = expectedBundleIdentifier
        self.expectedTextSHA256 = expectedTextSHA256
        self.fridaPorts = fridaPorts
        self.portProbeTimeoutMs = portProbeTimeoutMs
        self.scanMemory = scanMemory
        self.allowForkProbe = allowForkProbe
        self.allowedExceptionPorts = allowedExceptionPorts
        self.probeURLSchemes = probeURLSchemes
        self.debuggerExpected = debuggerExpected
        self.weightOverrides = weightOverrides
        self.suppressedCodes = suppressedCodes
    }

    /// How a code is treated under this configuration.
    struct Treatment { let multiplier: Double; let informational: Bool }

    func treatment(for code: FindingCode) -> Treatment {
        switch code.domain {
        case .posture:
            // Developer Mode etc. describe the device, they are not compromise.
            return .init(multiplier: 0, informational: true)
        case .app:
            guard let expected = expectedDistribution else { return .init(multiplier: 0, informational: true) }
            switch (expected, code) {
            case (.appStore, _):
                return .init(multiplier: 1, informational: false)
            case (.enterprise, .provisioningProfilePresent), (.enterprise, .binaryDecrypted), (.enterprise, .enterpriseProfile),
                 (.adHoc, .provisioningProfilePresent), (.adHoc, .binaryDecrypted),
                 (.development, .provisioningProfilePresent), (.development, .binaryDecrypted), (.development, .getTaskAllow):
                return .init(multiplier: 0, informational: true)
            default:
                return .init(multiplier: 1, informational: false)
            }
        case .runtime:
            let debuggerCodes: Set<FindingCode> = [.ptraced, .codeSigningDebugged, .exceptionPorts,
                                                   .parentProcessUnexpected, .breakpointInstruction]
            if (debuggerExpected || expectedDistribution == .development) && debuggerCodes.contains(code) {
                return .init(multiplier: 0, informational: true)
            }
            return .init(multiplier: 1, informational: false)
        case .device:
            return .init(multiplier: 1, informational: false)
        }
    }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/DetectionRules.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import CryptoKit
import CIntegrityCore

/// Remotely updatable detection rules, signed with your ECDSA P-256 key.
///
/// Files served by your backend (see Server/):
///   rules.json  UTF-8 JSON, exactly the bytes that were signed
///   rules.sig   base64 DER ECDSA-P256-SHA256 signature over rules.json
///
/// Safety model:
/// - **Additive only.** Rules can add paths, image markers, URL schemes and
///   Frida ports on top of the compiled-in lists. They cannot remove built-in
///   indicators, so a compromised or blocked rules channel cannot disable detection.
/// - **Bounded tuning.** Weight overrides are clamped to [0.5x, 1.5x] of the
///   built-in weight (and to 0...1). Thresholds may move by at most +/-15 points.
/// - **Anti-rollback.** A rules file whose `version` is lower than the highest
///   version already accepted on this device is rejected.
/// - **Expiry.** Expired rules are ignored; the compiled-in lists still apply.
/// - **Fail safe.** Any parse, signature or policy error leaves the previous
///   valid rules (or none) in place.
public struct DetectionRules: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    public struct Thresholds: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
        public var low: Int
        public var elevated: Int
        public var high: Int
        public var compromised: Int
    }

    public var version: Int
    public var issuedAt: Date
    public var expiresAt: Date
    public var minSdkVersion: String?
    public var extraJailbreakPaths: [String]
    /// Substrings matched against the *names* of images outside the system and
    /// the app bundle. Since 1.4.0 a match is `imageMarkerMatch` (206), an
    /// indicator: signed rules can never create a conclusive jailbreak verdict.
    /// Promoting an identity to conclusive needs a reviewed change to
    /// Server/classification.json and a new SDK release.
    public var extraImageMarkers: [String]
    /// Must also be listed under LSApplicationQueriesSchemes, or canOpenURL
    /// always returns false. Pre-declare a reserve list in Info.plist.
    public var extraURLSchemes: [String]
    public var extraFridaPorts: [UInt16]
    /// Keyed by finding code raw value as a string, e.g. "503": 0.4.
    public var weightOverrides: [String: Double]
    public var thresholds: Thresholds?
}

public enum RulesError: Error, Sendable {
    case badSignatureEncoding, signatureInvalid, decodeFailed(String), rollback(current: Int, offered: Int),
         expired, sdkTooOld(String), network(String)
}

/// Verifies, stores and serves the current rules.
public final class RulesStore: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let publicKey: P256.Signing.PublicKey
    private let directory: URL
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private var cached: DetectionRules?
    private let versionAccount = "rules.highest-version"
    private let keychainService = "integritykit.rules"

    /// - Parameter publicKeyPEM: your rules-signing public key ("-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----...").
    ///   Pin it in the binary; rotate by shipping a new app version.
    public init(publicKeyPEM: String, directory: URL? = nil) throws {
        self.publicKey = try P256.Signing.PublicKey(pemRepresentation: publicKeyPEM)
        let base = directory ?? FileManager.default.urls(for: .applicationSupportDirectory, in: .userDomainMask)[0]
        self.directory = base.appendingPathComponent("IntegrityRules", isDirectory: true)
        try? FileManager.default.createDirectory(at: self.directory, withIntermediateDirectories: true)
        self.cached = try? loadFromDisk()
    }

    /// Current valid, unexpired rules, or nil (compiled-in lists only).
    public var current: DetectionRules? {
        lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
        guard let r = cached, r.expiresAt > Date() else { return nil }
        return r
    }

    /// Verifies and installs a rules file. On success it is persisted.
    @discardableResult
    public func install(rulesData: Data, signatureBase64: String) throws -> DetectionRules {
        let rules = try verify(rulesData: rulesData, signatureBase64: signatureBase64)
        lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
        let highest = highestAcceptedVersion()
        if rules.version < highest { throw RulesError.rollback(current: highest, offered: rules.version) }
        try rulesData.write(to: directory.appendingPathComponent("rules.json"), options: .atomic)
        try Data(signatureBase64.utf8).write(to: directory.appendingPathComponent("rules.sig"), options: .atomic)
        Keychain.write(Data(String(rules.version).utf8), service: keychainService, account: versionAccount)
        cached = rules
        return rules
    }

    /// Downloads rules.json and rules.sig and installs them. Errors leave the
    /// previous rules in place.
    @discardableResult
    public func update(rulesURL: URL, signatureURL: URL, session: URLSession = .shared) async throws -> DetectionRules {
        do {
            let (rulesData, _) = try await session.data(from: rulesURL)
            let (sigData, _) = try await session.data(from: signatureURL)
            let sig = String(decoding: sigData, as: UTF8.self).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
            return try install(rulesData: rulesData, signatureBase64: sig)
        } catch let e as RulesError {
            throw e
        } catch {
            throw RulesError.network(error.localizedDescription)
        }
    }

    func verify(rulesData: Data, signatureBase64: String) throws -> DetectionRules {
        guard let der = Data(base64Encoded: signatureBase64),
              let sig = try? P256.Signing.ECDSASignature(derRepresentation: der) else {
            throw RulesError.badSignatureEncoding
        }
        guard publicKey.isValidSignature(sig, for: rulesData) else { throw RulesError.signatureInvalid }
        let dec = JSONDecoder()
        dec.dateDecodingStrategy = .iso8601
        let rules: DetectionRules
        do { rules = try dec.decode(DetectionRules.self, from: rulesData) }
        catch { throw RulesError.decodeFailed(String(describing: error)) }
        if rules.expiresAt <= Date() { throw RulesError.expired }
        if let min = rules.minSdkVersion,
           IntegrityKit.sdkVersion.compare(min, options: .numeric) == .orderedAscending {
            throw RulesError.sdkTooOld(min)
        }
        return rules
    }

    private func loadFromDisk() throws -> DetectionRules? {
        let r = directory.appendingPathComponent("rules.json")
        let s = directory.appendingPathComponent("rules.sig")
        guard let rd = try? Data(contentsOf: r), let sd = try? Data(contentsOf: s) else { return nil }
        let sig = String(decoding: sd, as: UTF8.self).trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        // Re-verified on every load: a file swapped on disk is rejected.
        return try verify(rulesData: rd, signatureBase64: sig)
    }

    private func highestAcceptedVersion() -> Int {
        guard let d = Keychain.read(service: keychainService, account: versionAccount),
              let v = Int(String(decoding: d, as: UTF8.self)) else { return 0 }
        return v
    }
}

/// Applies rules to the C core and Swift-side lists.
enum RulesRuntime {
    private static let lock = NSLock()
    private static var schemes: [String] = []

    static var extraURLSchemes: [String] {
        lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
        return schemes
    }

    static func apply(_ rules: DetectionRules?) {
        let paths: [String] = rules?.extraJailbreakPaths ?? []
        let markers: [String] = rules?.extraImageMarkers ?? []
        withCStringArray(paths) { p, np in
            withCStringArray(markers) { m, nm in
                rasp_rules_set(p, np, m, nm)
            }
        }
        lock.lock()
        schemes = rules?.extraURLSchemes ?? []
        lock.unlock()
    }

    /// Bounded weight: 0.5x to 1.5x of the built-in weight, within 0...1.
    static func boundedWeight(_ code: FindingCode, _ proposed: Double) -> Double {
        let base: Double = code.metadata.weight
        let lo: Double = Swift.max(0.0, base * 0.5)
        let hi: Double = Swift.min(1.0, base * 1.5)
        return Swift.min(Swift.max(proposed, lo), hi)
    }

    static func boundedThreshold(_ proposed: Int, _ base: Int) -> Int {
        Swift.min(Swift.max(proposed, base - 15), base + 15)
    }

    private static func withCStringArray(_ strings: [String],
                                         _ body: (UnsafePointer<UnsafePointer<CChar>?>?, UInt32) -> Void) {
        let owned: [UnsafeMutablePointer<CChar>?] = strings.map { strdup($0) }
        defer { for p in owned { free(p) } }
        let ptrs: [UnsafePointer<CChar>?] = owned.map { UnsafePointer($0) }
        ptrs.withUnsafeBufferPointer { buf in
            body(buf.baseAddress, UInt32(buf.count))
        }
    }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/Finding.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Evidence category. Signals are combined with damping *within* a category
/// (correlated evidence) and without damping *across* categories (independent
/// corroboration).
public enum SignalCategory: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Sendable {
    case jailbreakArtefact
    case filesystemIntegrity
    case sandbox
    case injection
    case hooking
    case debugger
    case instrumentation
    case codeSigning
    case environment
    case developerMode
    case tamper
    case runtimeEnvironment
}

/// What a signal is evidence about. Mirrors the Android split between
/// device integrity, app integrity and the runtime of this process.
public enum IntegrityDomain: String, Codable, CaseIterable, Sendable {
    /// The phone itself: jailbreak, tampered OS, broken sandbox.
    case device
    /// This process right now: hooks, injection, Frida, debugger.
    case runtime
    /// How this app binary was built, signed and distributed.
    case app
    /// Facts about the device's configuration that are not compromise
    /// (Developer Mode, Developer Disk Image). Never scored.
    case posture
}

/// Finding codes. Raw values 1xx-8xx mirror `RASP_F_*` in rasp_core.h; 9xx+
/// are produced by the Swift layer.
public enum FindingCode: UInt16, Codable, CaseIterable, Sendable {
    // C core
    case jailbreakPathPresent       = 100
    case jailbreakPathErrnoOracle   = 101
    case symlinkedSystemDirectory   = 102
    case rootVolumeNotSealed        = 103
    case rootVolumeWritable         = 104
    case rootlessJailbreakRoot      = 105
    case libcSyscallMismatch        = 108
    case canaryFailed               = 109
    case dyldModified               = 110
    case userspaceReboot            = 111
    case sandboxWrite               = 120
    case sandboxFork                = 121
    case sandboxRead                = 122
    /// Since 1.4.0: a *known jailbreak injector* (exact identity outside the
    /// bundle, or a jailbreak-root path). Broad name matches moved to 206.
    case suspiciousImage            = 200
    case unexpectedImagePath        = 201
    case imageListMismatch          = 202
    case imageAddedAtRuntime        = 203
    case interposeSection           = 204
    /// 1.4.0: Frida/Cycript image, or a jailbreak injector found inside the
    /// app bundle (a re-signed app). Runtime risk, never proof of a jailbreak.
    case instrumentationImage       = 205
    /// 1.4.0: broad marker (built-in or signed remote rules) in the name of an
    /// image outside the system and the bundle. Indicator only.
    case imageMarkerMatch           = 206
    case inlineHook                 = 300
    case symbolRebound              = 301
    case breakpointInstruction      = 302
    case objcSwizzled               = 303
    case syscallStubAltered         = 304
    case symbolForeignImage         = 305
    case prologueChanged            = 306
    case ptraced                    = 400
    case codeSigningDebugged        = 401
    case exceptionPorts             = 402
    case parentProcessUnexpected    = 403
    case fridaThread                = 500
    case fridaPortOpen              = 501
    case fridaDBus                  = 502
    case anonymousExecutableMemory  = 503
    case fridaMemoryMarker          = 504
    case codeSignatureInvalid       = 600
    case getTaskAllow               = 601
    case suspiciousEntitlement      = 602
    case binaryDecrypted            = 603
    case textModified               = 610
    case envInjection               = 700
    case envJailbreak               = 701
    case envGetenvMismatch          = 702
    case developerModeEnabled       = 800
    case developerDiskImageMounted  = 802
    // Swift layer
    case developerModeInferred      = 801
    case provisioningProfilePresent = 900
    case teamIdentifierMismatch     = 901
    case bundleIdentifierMismatch   = 902
    case enterpriseProfile          = 903
    case bundlePathUnexpected       = 904
    case jailbreakURLScheme         = 905
    case textHashMismatch           = 907
    case previouslyJailbroken       = 908
    case checkSuppressed            = 1000
    case findingsOverflow           = 1001
    case simulator                  = 1002
    case monitorIntervalStarved     = 1003

    public struct Metadata: Sendable {
        public let category: SignalCategory
        /// Prior probability-like weight (0...1) that this signal alone means
        /// the runtime cannot be trusted. Multiplied by the check's confidence.
        public let weight: Double
        /// Hard indicators floor the verdict at `.high` regardless of score.
        public let hard: Bool
        public let title: String
    }

    public var metadata: Metadata {
        switch self {
        case .jailbreakPathPresent:       return .init(category: .jailbreakArtefact, weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "Jailbreak artefact present")
        case .jailbreakPathErrnoOracle:   return .init(category: .jailbreakArtefact, weight: 0.45, hard: false, title: "Jailbreak artefact inferred from errno")
        case .symlinkedSystemDirectory:   return .init(category: .filesystemIntegrity, weight: 0.70, hard: false, title: "System directory replaced by symlink")
        case .rootVolumeNotSealed:        return .init(category: .filesystemIntegrity, weight: 0.55, hard: false, title: "Root volume is not the sealed snapshot")
        case .rootVolumeWritable:         return .init(category: .filesystemIntegrity, weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "Root volume mounted read-write")
        case .rootlessJailbreakRoot:      return .init(category: .jailbreakArtefact, weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "Rootless jailbreak root (/var/jb) present")
        case .libcSyscallMismatch:        return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "libc and kernel disagree (hiding hook)")
        case .dyldModified:               return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.50, hard: false, title: "dyld code differs from disk (possible loader hook)")
        case .userspaceReboot:            return .init(category: .jailbreakArtefact, weight: 0.40, hard: false, title: "Userspace reboot since kernel boot")
        case .previouslyJailbroken:       return .init(category: .jailbreakArtefact, weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "Jailbreak seen on this device before")
        case .canaryFailed:               return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.80, hard: false, title: "Control probe failed")
        case .sandboxWrite:               return .init(category: .sandbox,           weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "Wrote outside the app container")
        case .sandboxFork:                return .init(category: .sandbox,           weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "fork() permitted")
        case .sandboxRead:                return .init(category: .sandbox,           weight: 0.55, hard: false, title: "Read another domain's directory")
        case .suspiciousImage:            return .init(category: .injection,         weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "Known jailbreak injection library loaded")
        case .unexpectedImagePath:        return .init(category: .injection,         weight: 0.50, hard: false, title: "Image loaded from unexpected location")
        case .imageListMismatch:          return .init(category: .injection,         weight: 0.85, hard: true,  title: "dyld image list is being filtered")
        case .imageAddedAtRuntime:        return .init(category: .injection,         weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "Suspicious image loaded at runtime")
        case .instrumentationImage:       return .init(category: .instrumentation,   weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "Instrumentation library loaded (Frida, Cycript or injected tweak)")
        case .imageMarkerMatch:           return .init(category: .injection,         weight: 0.50, hard: false, title: "Image name matches an indicator marker")
        case .interposeSection:           return .init(category: .injection,         weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "DYLD interposing section in non-system image")
        case .inlineHook:                 return .init(category: .hooking,           weight: 0.85, hard: true,  title: "Inline hook trampoline")
        case .symbolRebound:              return .init(category: .hooking,           weight: 0.85, hard: true,  title: "Symbol pointer rebound (fishhook/interposing)")
        case .breakpointInstruction:      return .init(category: .debugger,          weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "Software breakpoint at function entry")
        case .objcSwizzled:               return .init(category: .hooking,           weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "Objective-C method implementation moved")
        case .syscallStubAltered:         return .init(category: .hooking,           weight: 0.70, hard: false, title: "Syscall stub does not trap")
        case .symbolForeignImage:         return .init(category: .hooking,           weight: 0.80, hard: false, title: "System symbol resolves outside system images")
        case .prologueChanged:            return .init(category: .hooking,           weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "Function code changed after start-up")
        case .textModified:               return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "App code modified in memory")
        case .ptraced:                    return .init(category: .debugger,          weight: 0.80, hard: false, title: "Process is being traced")
        case .codeSigningDebugged:        return .init(category: .debugger,          weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "Kernel marked process CS_DEBUGGED")
        case .exceptionPorts:             return .init(category: .debugger,          weight: 0.35, hard: false, title: "Task exception ports registered")
        case .parentProcessUnexpected:    return .init(category: .debugger,          weight: 0.50, hard: false, title: "Parent is not launchd")
        case .fridaThread:                return .init(category: .instrumentation,   weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "Frida runtime thread")
        case .fridaPortOpen:              return .init(category: .instrumentation,   weight: 0.35, hard: false, title: "Instrumentation port listening")
        case .fridaDBus:                  return .init(category: .instrumentation,   weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "frida-server D-Bus endpoint")
        case .anonymousExecutableMemory:  return .init(category: .instrumentation,   weight: 0.35, hard: false, title: "Anonymous executable memory")
        case .fridaMemoryMarker:          return .init(category: .instrumentation,   weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "Frida agent marker in memory")
        case .codeSignatureInvalid:       return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "Code signature not valid")
        case .getTaskAllow:               return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.50, hard: false, title: "Debuggable build (get-task-allow)")
        case .suspiciousEntitlement:      return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.85, hard: true,  title: "Entitlement not grantable to App Store apps")
        case .binaryDecrypted:            return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.60, hard: false, title: "Binary not FairPlay-encrypted (not an App Store install)")
        case .envInjection:               return .init(category: .environment,       weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "DYLD_* injection variable set")
        case .envJailbreak:               return .init(category: .environment,       weight: 0.95, hard: true,  title: "Jailbreak environment variable set")
        case .envGetenvMismatch:          return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "getenv() hides variables")
        case .developerModeEnabled:       return .init(category: .developerMode,     weight: 0.20, hard: false, title: "Developer Mode enabled")
        case .developerModeInferred:      return .init(category: .developerMode,     weight: 0.15, hard: false, title: "Developer Mode inferred")
        case .developerDiskImageMounted:  return .init(category: .developerMode,     weight: 0.25, hard: false, title: "Developer Disk Image mounted")
        case .provisioningProfilePresent: return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.35, hard: false, title: "Embedded provisioning profile (non-App Store signing)")
        case .teamIdentifierMismatch:     return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "Signed by an unexpected team (re-signed)")
        case .bundleIdentifierMismatch:   return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "Unexpected bundle identifier")
        case .enterpriseProfile:          return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.40, hard: false, title: "Enterprise (in-house) profile")
        case .bundlePathUnexpected:       return .init(category: .filesystemIntegrity, weight: 0.70, hard: false, title: "App installed outside the user app container")
        case .jailbreakURLScheme:         return .init(category: .jailbreakArtefact, weight: 0.55, hard: false, title: "Package manager URL scheme registered")
        case .textHashMismatch:           return .init(category: .codeSigning,       weight: 0.90, hard: true,  title: "__TEXT hash differs from build")
        case .checkSuppressed:            return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.85, hard: true,  title: "A check did not run to completion")
        case .findingsOverflow:           return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.30, hard: false, title: "Findings buffer overflowed")
        case .simulator:                  return .init(category: .runtimeEnvironment, weight: 0.50, hard: false, title: "Running in the iOS Simulator")
        case .monitorIntervalStarved:     return .init(category: .tamper,            weight: 0.40, hard: false, title: "Monitor did not run on schedule")
        }
    }

    public var domain: IntegrityDomain {
        switch self {
        case .jailbreakPathPresent, .jailbreakPathErrnoOracle, .symlinkedSystemDirectory, .rootVolumeNotSealed,
             .rootVolumeWritable, .rootlessJailbreakRoot, .libcSyscallMismatch, .sandboxWrite, .sandboxFork,
             .sandboxRead, .suspiciousEntitlement, .envJailbreak, .bundlePathUnexpected, .jailbreakURLScheme,
             .simulator, .dyldModified, .userspaceReboot, .previouslyJailbroken:
            return .device
        case .canaryFailed, .suspiciousImage, .unexpectedImagePath, .imageListMismatch, .imageAddedAtRuntime,
             .interposeSection, .inlineHook, .symbolRebound, .breakpointInstruction, .objcSwizzled,
             .syscallStubAltered, .symbolForeignImage, .ptraced, .codeSigningDebugged, .exceptionPorts,
             .parentProcessUnexpected, .fridaThread, .fridaPortOpen, .fridaDBus, .anonymousExecutableMemory,
             .fridaMemoryMarker, .envInjection, .envGetenvMismatch, .checkSuppressed, .findingsOverflow,
             .monitorIntervalStarved, .prologueChanged, .textModified, .instrumentationImage, .imageMarkerMatch:
            return .runtime
        case .codeSignatureInvalid, .getTaskAllow, .binaryDecrypted, .provisioningProfilePresent,
             .teamIdentifierMismatch, .bundleIdentifierMismatch, .enterpriseProfile, .textHashMismatch:
            return .app
        case .developerModeEnabled, .developerModeInferred, .developerDiskImageMounted:
            return .posture
        }
    }
}
```
