`Sources/KaiselKit/IntegrityGuard.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import CryptoKit
import CIntegrityCore

/// Cheap, inlined checks to scatter through the host app.
///
/// A single `evaluate()` call is one place to hook. Calling `IntegrityGuard`
/// at many sensitive sites (before login, payment, key use, screen entry)
/// means an attacker has to find and neutralise every site. The C guard is a
/// header-only `always_inline` function that uses raw syscalls only, so each
/// Swift module that calls it gets its own copy of the trap instructions.
///
/// ```swift
/// guard IntegrityGuard.isClean else { return presentBlockedScreen() }
/// ```
public enum IntegrityGuard {
    public struct Flags: OptionSet, Sendable {
        public let rawValue: UInt32
        public init(rawValue: UInt32) { self.rawValue = rawValue }
        public static let traced = Flags(rawValue: UInt32(RASP_GUARD_TRACED))
        public static let codeSigningDebugged = Flags(rawValue: UInt32(RASP_GUARD_CS_DEBUGGED))
        public static let jailbreakRoot = Flags(rawValue: UInt32(RASP_GUARD_JB_ROOT))
    }

    /// Raw flags from the inline guard (debugger attached, CS_DEBUGGED, /var/jb).
    @inline(__always)
    public static var flags: Flags { Flags(rawValue: rasp_quick_guard()) }

    /// `false` if the guard saw anything. Development builds will report
    /// `.codeSigningDebugged` while Xcode is attached.
    @inline(__always)
    public static var isClean: Bool { rasp_quick_guard() == 0 }
}

/// Tamper-evident holder for the last verdict.
///
/// Storing `verdict` in a plain property lets an attacker flip one byte in
/// memory (or hook a getter) to turn `.compromised` into `.trusted`. The box
/// keeps the value XOR-masked with a random per-process key and an HMAC over
/// (value, report ID) with a second random key. Reading a box whose MAC does
/// not verify returns `.compromised`.
///
/// This raises the bar for casual memory patching only. An attacker with
/// code execution can hook `read()` itself, which is why the server, not the
/// client, should act on the verdict.
public final class ProtectedVerdict: @unchecked Sendable {
    private let lock = NSLock()
    private let maskKey: UInt8 = UInt8.random(in: 1...255)
    private let macKey = SymmetricKey(size: .bits256)
    private var masked: UInt8 = 0
    private var reportID = UUID()
    private var mac = Data()
    private var hasValue = false

    public init() {}

    private static func code(_ v: Verdict) -> UInt8 {
        switch v {
        case .trusted: return 0x11
        case .low: return 0x22
        case .elevated: return 0x33
        case .high: return 0x44
        case .compromised: return 0x55
        }
    }

    private static func verdict(_ c: UInt8) -> Verdict? {
        switch c {
        case 0x11: return .trusted
        case 0x22: return .low
        case 0x33: return .elevated
        case 0x44: return .high
        case 0x55: return .compromised
        default: return nil
        }
    }

    private func tag(_ code: UInt8, _ id: UUID) -> Data {
        var msg = Data([code])
        msg.append(Data(id.uuidString.utf8))
        return Data(HMAC<SHA256>.authenticationCode(for: msg, using: macKey))
    }

    func store(_ report: IntegrityReport) {
        let c = Self.code(report.verdict)
        lock.lock()
        masked = c ^ maskKey
        reportID = report.id
        mac = tag(c, report.id)
        hasValue = true
        lock.unlock()
    }

    /// The last verdict, or `.compromised` if the stored value was tampered with.
    /// `nil` before the first evaluation.
    public func read() -> Verdict? {
        lock.lock(); defer { lock.unlock() }
        guard hasValue else { return nil }
        let c = masked ^ maskKey
        guard tag(c, reportID) == mac, let v = Self.verdict(c) else { return .compromised }
        return v
    }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/IntegrityKit.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import UIKit
import CIntegrityCore

/// Entry point.
///
/// ```swift
/// let kit = IntegrityKit(configuration: .init(expectedDistribution: .appStore,
///                                             expectedTeamIdentifier: "ABCDE12345"))
/// let report = await kit.evaluate()
/// let sealed = try await kit.seal(report, serverNonce: nonce)   // send to backend
/// ```
public final class IntegrityKit: @unchecked Sendable {
    public static let sdkVersion = "1.4.0"

    public let configuration: IntegrityConfiguration
    public var scorer = RiskScorer()
    /// Tamper-evident copy of the most recent verdict.
    public let lastVerdict = ProtectedVerdict()
    /// Signed remote rules in effect (nil = compiled-in lists only).
    public let rules: DetectionRules?
    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "integritykit.eval", qos: .userInitiated)
    private let attestation: AttestationClient

    /// - Parameter rules: verified rules from `RulesStore.current`. They are
    ///   additive and bounded; see `DetectionRules`.
    public init(configuration: IntegrityConfiguration = .init(), rules: DetectionRules? = nil) {
        rasp_integrity_baseline()
        var cfg = configuration
        var sc = RiskScorer()
        if let r = rules {
            for p in r.extraFridaPorts where !cfg.fridaPorts.contains(p) { cfg.fridaPorts.append(p) }
            for (key, w) in r.weightOverrides {
                guard let raw = UInt16(key), let code = FindingCode(rawValue: raw),
                      cfg.weightOverrides[code] == nil else { continue }   // local config wins
                cfg.weightOverrides[code] = RulesRuntime.boundedWeight(code, w)
            }
            if let t = r.thresholds {
                let d = sc.thresholds
                sc.thresholds = (RulesRuntime.boundedThreshold(t.low, d.low),
                                 RulesRuntime.boundedThreshold(t.elevated, d.elevated),
                                 RulesRuntime.boundedThreshold(t.high, d.high),
                                 RulesRuntime.boundedThreshold(t.compromised, d.compromised))
            }
        }
        RulesRuntime.apply(rules)
        self.configuration = cfg
        self.rules = rules
        self.scorer = sc
        self.attestation = AttestationClient()
        rasp_monitor_install()
    }

    // MARK: Evaluation

    /// Runs every check off the main thread. Typical cost on an A14-class device
    /// is 15-60 ms, dominated by port-probe timeouts and the VM-map walk.
    public func evaluate() async -> IntegrityReport {
        await withCheckedContinuation { cont in
            queue.async { cont.resume(returning: self.evaluateSync()) }
        }
    }

    /// Synchronous variant. Do not call on the main thread while the main thread
    /// is needed elsewhere: the URL-scheme probe hops to main.
    public func evaluateSync() -> IntegrityReport { evaluate(extra: []) }

    func evaluate(extra: [RawSignal]) -> IntegrityReport {
        let started = Date()
        var cf = CoreBridge.facts()
        let sys = Self.systemInfo()
        let profile = AppIntegrityChecks.loadProfile()
        let build = AppBuildFacts(
            bundleIdentifier: Bundle.main.bundleIdentifier ?? "",
            teamIdentifier: profile?.teamIdentifiers.first,
            detectedDistribution: AppIntegrityChecks.detectDistribution(profile: profile, cryptID: cf.cryptid, csFlags: cf.cs_flags),
            expectedDistribution: configuration.expectedDistribution?.rawValue,
            codeSigningFlags: cf.cs_flags,
            cryptID: cf.cryptid,
            textSHA256: CoreBridge.textHash(&cf),
            exceptionPortCount: cf.exception_port_count)

        let checks = CoreCheck.allCases
        let core = CoreBridge.run(checks: checks, configuration: configuration)
        var raw: [RawSignal] = core.findings.compactMap { f in
            guard let code = FindingCode(rawValue: f.code) else { return nil }
            return RawSignal(code, f.confidence, f.detail)
        }

        // Tamper: a check that never started or never completed was hooked,
        // patched out, or crashed mid-way under instrumentation.
        var expected: UInt32 = 0
        for c in checks { expected |= c.bit }
        for check in checks where core.completed & check.bit == 0 {
            let how = core.started & check.bit == 0 ? "never entered" : "entered but did not complete"
            raw.append(.init(.checkSuppressed, 95, "\(check.name): \(how)"))
        }
        if core.dropped > 0 { raw.append(.init(.findingsOverflow, 100, "\(core.dropped) findings dropped")) }

        // Exception ports: tolerate the number the host app's crash reporter registers.
        raw.removeAll { $0.code == .exceptionPorts && build.exceptionPortCount <= configuration.allowedExceptionPorts }

        raw += AppIntegrityChecks(configuration: configuration, build: build, osMajor: sys.osMajor,
                                  developerModeSysctl: cf.devmode_sysctl).run()
        raw += newlyLoadedImages()
        raw += extra

        #if targetEnvironment(simulator)
        raw.append(.init(.simulator, 100, "targetEnvironment(simulator)"))
        #endif

        var signals = Self.requireCorroboration(makeSignals(raw))
        if let first = signals.first(where: JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive) {
            JailbreakMemory.remember(RawSignal(first.code, first.confidence, first.detail))
        } else if let past = JailbreakMemory.recall() {
            signals = makeSignals(raw + [past])
            signals = Self.requireCorroboration(signals)
        }
        let posture = PostureProbe.collect(osVersion: sys.osVersion, osMajor: sys.osMajor, model: sys.model,
                                           devModeSysctl: cf.devmode_sysctl, csFlags: cf.cs_flags,
                                           ddiMounted: raw.contains { $0.code == .developerDiskImageMounted })
        var device = scorer.assess(.device, signals)
        // Same rule as Kaisel JB Check: conclusive jailbreak evidence in any
        // domain (for example a tweak injector such as roothideinit.dylib,
        // which is scored under runtime) makes the device compromised.
        if signals.contains(where: JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive), device.verdict < .compromised {
            device = DomainAssessment(domain: .device,
                                      score: Swift.max(device.score, scorer.thresholds.compromised),
                                      verdict: .compromised, categories: device.categories)
        }
        let runtime = scorer.assess(.runtime, signals)
        let app: DomainAssessment? = configuration.expectedDistribution == nil ? nil : scorer.assess(.app, signals)
        var worst: DomainAssessment = device
        var enforced: [DomainAssessment] = [runtime]
        if let a = app { enforced.append(a) }
        for d in enforced {
            let worseVerdict: Bool = worst.verdict < d.verdict
            let sameVerdictHigherScore: Bool = worst.verdict == d.verdict && worst.score < d.score
            if worseVerdict || sameVerdictHigherScore { worst = d }
        }
        let report = IntegrityReport(
            id: UUID(),
            timestamp: started,
            reportSchemaVersion: IntegrityReport.currentSchemaVersion,
            sdkVersion: Self.sdkVersion,
            verdict: worst.verdict,
            score: worst.score,
            device: device,
            runtime: runtime,
            app: app,
            posture: posture,
            build: build,
            signals: signals,
            checksCompleted: core.completed,
            checksExpected: expected,
            observabilityNotes: PlatformCapabilities.current.notes,
            durationMs: Date().timeIntervalSince(started) * 1000.0,
            rulesVersion: rules?.version)
        lastVerdict.store(report)
        return report
    }

    /// Images dlopen'ed since the previous evaluation that are not trusted.
    /// Each one is reported with its own class (200 injector, 205
    /// instrumentation, 206 marker, 201 unknown external), so the conclusive
    /// rule is the same as for images present at start-up, plus an
    /// `imageAddedAtRuntime` indicator recording that it arrived late.
    /// Before 1.4.0 a late image was conclusive whenever its confidence was 95
    /// and its name did not mention Frida.
    private func newlyLoadedImages() -> [RawSignal] {
        CoreBridge.drainNewImages().flatMap { path -> [RawSignal] in
            guard let e = CoreBridge.classifyImageEvidence(path) else { return [] }
            return [RawSignal(e.code, e.confidence, path), RawSignal(.imageAddedAtRuntime, 70, path)]
        }
    }

    private func makeSignals(_ raw: [RawSignal]) -> [Signal] {
        Self.makeSignals(raw, configuration: configuration)
    }

    static func makeSignals(_ raw: [RawSignal], configuration: IntegrityConfiguration) -> [Signal] {
        var seen = Set<String>()
        var out: [Signal] = []
        for r in raw {
            if configuration.suppressedCodes.contains(r.code) { continue }
            let key: String = "\(r.code.rawValue)|\(r.detail)"
            if !seen.insert(key).inserted { continue }
            let base: Double = configuration.weightOverrides[r.code] ?? r.code.metadata.weight
            let t: IntegrityConfiguration.Treatment = configuration.treatment(for: r.code)
            let multiplier: Double = t.informational ? 1.0 : t.multiplier
            let w: Double = Swift.min(Swift.max(base * multiplier, 0.0), 1.0)
            let informational: Bool = t.informational || w == 0
            out.append(Signal(code: r.code, confidence: r.confidence, weight: w,
                              informational: informational, detail: r.detail))
        }
        // Scored signals first, then by effective weight.
        out.sort { (a: Signal, b: Signal) -> Bool in
            if a.informational != b.informational { return !a.informational }
            return a.effectiveWeight > b.effectiveWeight
        }
        return out
    }

    /// Anonymous executable memory alone is too weak to score: OS runtimes can
    /// create such regions (seen on iOS 27 with no instrumentation present).
    /// It is scored only when another instrumentation or hooking signal in the
    /// same report corroborates it; otherwise it is kept as informational.
    /// Mirrored by `corroboratingCodes` in Server/classification.json (parity-tested).
    static let corroboratingCodes: Set<FindingCode> = [.fridaThread, .fridaDBus, .fridaMemoryMarker, .fridaPortOpen,
                                                       .inlineHook, .symbolRebound, .suspiciousImage,
                                                       .instrumentationImage, .imageListMismatch]

    static func requireCorroboration(_ signals: [Signal]) -> [Signal] {
        let corroborating = corroboratingCodes
        let corroborated = signals.contains { corroborating.contains($0.code) && !$0.informational }
        guard !corroborated else { return signals }
        return signals.map { s in
            guard s.code == .anonymousExecutableMemory, !s.informational else { return s }
            return Signal(code: s.code, confidence: s.confidence, weight: s.weight, informational: true,
                          detail: s.detail + " (uncorroborated)")
        }
    }

    struct SystemInfo { let osVersion: String; let osMajor: Int; let model: String }

    static func systemInfo() -> SystemInfo {
        let v = ProcessInfo.processInfo.operatingSystemVersion
        var sys = utsname()
        uname(&sys)
        let machineLen = MemoryLayout.size(ofValue: sys.machine)
        let model = withUnsafePointer(to: &sys.machine) {
            $0.withMemoryRebound(to: CChar.self, capacity: machineLen) { String(cString: $0) }
        }
        return SystemInfo(osVersion: "\(v.majorVersion).\(v.minorVersion).\(v.patchVersion)",
                          osMajor: v.majorVersion, model: model)
    }

    // MARK: Sealing and attestation

    /// Binds a report to a server-issued nonce and signs it with App Attest
    /// (preferred) and/or a Secure Enclave key. See `SealedReport`.
    public func seal(_ report: IntegrityReport, serverNonce: Data) async throws -> SealedReport {
        try await attestation.seal(report, nonce: serverNonce)
    }

    /// One-time App Attest enrolment. Send the result to your server, which
    /// verifies it against Apple's App Attest root CA and stores the public key.
    ///
    /// Throws `IntegrityError.appAttestUnavailable` when the build has no App
    /// Attest entitlement; `seal` then still works, without an assertion.
    public func attestKey(serverChallenge: Data) async throws -> AppAttestEnrolment {
        try await attestation.attestKey(challenge: serverChallenge)
    }

    /// Local App Attest state, for display. The server is the authority.
    public var appAttestStatus: AppAttestStatus { attestation.appAttestStatus }

    /// DeviceCheck token for per-device two-bit state on the server
    /// (e.g. "this device was previously seen jailbroken").
    public func deviceCheckToken() async throws -> Data {
        try await attestation.deviceCheckToken()
    }

    // MARK: Active countermeasures

    /// PT_DENY_ATTACH via a raw syscall. Opt-in: it terminates any attached
    /// debugger (and Xcode), and it is trivially neutralised on a jailbroken
    /// kernel. Use as a speed bump in release builds only.
    @discardableResult
    public static func denyDebuggerAttach() -> Int32 {
        #if DEBUG
        return 0
        #else
        return rasp_deny_debugger_attach()
        #endif
    }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/IntegrityMonitor.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Periodic re-evaluation. Attach-after-launch attacks (frida -U -n, lldb attach,
/// runtime dlopen) are invisible to a launch-time check, so sensitive flows
/// should either evaluate on demand or keep a monitor running.
public final class IntegrityMonitor: @unchecked Sendable {
    public typealias Handler = @Sendable (IntegrityReport) -> Void

    private let kit: IntegrityKit
    private let interval: TimeInterval
    private let jitter: TimeInterval
    private let handler: Handler
    private var timer: DispatchSourceTimer?
    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "integritykit.monitor", qos: .utility)
    private var lastRun: Date?

    /// - Parameters:
    ///   - interval: base period between evaluations.
    ///   - jitter: random +/- jitter so the schedule is not predictable.
    ///   - handler: called on a background queue with every report.
    public init(kit: IntegrityKit, interval: TimeInterval = 30, jitter: TimeInterval = 10, handler: @escaping Handler) {
        self.kit = kit
        self.interval = interval
        self.jitter = jitter
        self.handler = handler
    }

    public func start() {
        queue.async { [weak self] in self?.schedule() }
    }

    public func stop() {
        queue.async { [weak self] in
            self?.timer?.cancel()
            self?.timer = nil
        }
    }

    private func schedule() {
        let t = DispatchSource.makeTimerSource(queue: queue)
        let offset: Double = Double.random(in: -jitter...jitter)
        let delay: Double = Swift.max(1.0, interval + offset)
        t.schedule(deadline: .now() + delay)
        t.setEventHandler { [weak self] in self?.fire() }
        timer?.cancel()
        timer = t
        t.resume()
    }

    private func fire() {
        // A gap far beyond the schedule means the process was suspended (e.g. a
        // debugger stopped it) or the timer was interfered with.
        var extra: [RawSignal] = []
        if let last = lastRun {
            let gap = Date().timeIntervalSince(last)
            let limit: Double = (interval + jitter) * 4.0
            if gap > limit {
                extra.append(.init(.monitorIntervalStarved, 70, String(format: "%.0fs since previous run", gap)))
            }
        }
        handler(kit.evaluate(extra: extra))
        lastRun = Date()
        schedule()
    }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/JailbreakDetector.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation
import CIntegrityCore

/// Jailbreak-only check: answers "is this phone jailbroken?" and nothing about
/// how the calling app was built, signed or distributed, or whether a
/// debugger or Frida is attached to it.
///
/// Decision rule:
/// - `jailbroken`: at least one **conclusive** finding (`JailbreakEvidence`), meaning evidence that a
///   stock, sealed iOS cannot produce: the rootless jailbreak root `/var/jb`,
///   jailbreak files visible to the kernel, a writable or re-mounted root
///   volume, a broken app sandbox (write outside the container or fork()),
///   tweak-injection libraries (Substrate/ElleKit/TweakInject/systemhook) in
///   the process, jailbreak-only environment variables or entitlements, or a
///   hiding tweak caught lying (libc and the kernel disagree, or dyld's image
///   list is filtered).
/// - `suspicious`: only indirect evidence (package-manager URL schemes,
///   errno-inferred paths, unexpected image locations, hooks on file APIs,
///   a check that did not complete).
/// - `notJailbroken`: none of the above. This means no conclusive or indirect
///   evidence was observed by this process. It is not proof of a stock device:
///   per-app hiding (for example RootHide's Blacklist) is a known miss.
public struct JailbreakResult: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public enum Status: String, Codable, Sendable { case jailbroken, suspicious, notJailbroken }
    public let status: Status
    /// Evidence that made the result `jailbroken`.
    public let conclusive: [Signal]
    /// Indirect evidence.
    public let indicators: [Signal]
    public let model: String
    public let osVersion: String
    public let checkedAt: Date
    public let durationMs: Double
    /// Checks that ran (filesystem, sandbox, images, hooks, environment, code signing, canary).
    public let checksRun: [String]
    /// Checks that were started but did not complete (1.4.0). Empty on a normal run.
    public let checksIncomplete: [String]
    /// SDK version that produced the result (1.4.0), for lab records.
    public let sdkVersion: String
}

/// Explicit loaded-image evidence classes (1.4.0). A thin wrapper over the C
/// classifier (`rasp_image_class`), so the image scan, the runtime monitor and
/// re-validation of stored evidence use one implementation. The lists come
/// from `Server/classification.json`, shared with the reference server.
public enum ImageEvidenceClass: String, Codable, Sendable {
    /// No usable path.
    case unknown
    /// System location, or inside the app bundle without a known identity.
    case trusted
    /// Exact jailbreak-injector identity outside the bundle, or a jailbreak-root path. Conclusive.
    case jailbreakInjector
    /// Frida/Cycript, or an injector inside the bundle (re-signed app). Runtime risk only.
    case instrumentation
    /// Broad marker (built-in or signed remote rules) in an external image name. Indicator.
    case marker
    /// Any other image outside the system and the bundle. Indicator.
    case unknownExternal
}

public enum ImageEvidence {
    /// Classifies an image path. `bundlePath` nil means "unknown bundle": an
    /// image is then treated as in-bundle when a directory component ends in ".app".
    public static func classify(_ path: String, bundlePath: String? = nil) -> ImageEvidenceClass {
        let c: rasp_image_class_t
        if let b = bundlePath { c = rasp_image_class(path, b) } else { c = rasp_image_class(path, nil) }
        switch c {
        case RASP_IMG_TRUSTED: return .trusted
        case RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR: return .jailbreakInjector
        case RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION: return .instrumentation
        case RASP_IMG_MARKER: return .marker
        case RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL: return .unknownExternal
        default: return .unknown
        }
    }

    /// Same as `classify`, with the simulator behaviour explicit. Used by the
    /// regression tests so results do not depend on where the tests run.
    static func classify(_ path: String, bundlePath: String?, simulator: Bool) -> ImageEvidenceClass {
        let c: rasp_image_class_t
        if let b = bundlePath { c = rasp_image_class_ex(path, b, simulator) } else { c = rasp_image_class_ex(path, nil, simulator) }
        switch c {
        case RASP_IMG_TRUSTED: return .trusted
        case RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR: return .jailbreakInjector
        case RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION: return .instrumentation
        case RASP_IMG_MARKER: return .marker
        case RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL: return .unknownExternal
        default: return .unknown
        }
    }
}

/// The single rule that decides whether evidence proves a jailbreak. Used by
/// `JailbreakDetector` (Kaisel JB Check), by `IntegrityKit` for the device
/// verdict and by `JailbreakMemory` for stored history, so all agree. The
/// reference server applies the same rule (`Server/policy.py`), and a parity
/// test checks both sets against `Server/classification.json`.
///
/// 1.4.0: conclusive evidence is an explicit allow-list of finding codes. An
/// image is conclusive only as `suspiciousImage` (200) whose path still
/// classifies as a known jailbreak injector. Before 1.4.0 any image finding
/// that did not mention Frida was treated as an injector, so a framework named
/// like a broad marker (for example "CraneBank") could produce "Jailbroken".
public enum JailbreakEvidence {
    /// Codes that a stock, sealed iOS cannot produce.
    public static let conclusiveCodes: Set<FindingCode> = [
        .jailbreakPathPresent, .rootlessJailbreakRoot, .symlinkedSystemDirectory, .rootVolumeWritable,
        .sandboxWrite, .sandboxFork, .libcSyscallMismatch, .imageListMismatch, .envJailbreak,
        .suspiciousEntitlement, .bundlePathUnexpected, .suspiciousImage,
    ]
    /// Indirect evidence only.
    public static let indicatorCodes: Set<FindingCode> = [
        .jailbreakPathErrnoOracle, .rootVolumeNotSealed, .sandboxRead, .unexpectedImagePath,
        .imageMarkerMatch, .instrumentationImage, .imageAddedAtRuntime,
        .interposeSection, .inlineHook, .symbolRebound, .objcSwizzled, .syscallStubAltered,
        .symbolForeignImage, .envInjection, .envGetenvMismatch, .jailbreakURLScheme, .canaryFailed,
        .checkSuppressed, .userspaceReboot, .previouslyJailbroken, .dyldModified,
    ]

    /// True when the image named in `detail` is a known jailbreak injector
    /// (RootHide's roothideinit, Substrate, ElleKit, TweakInject, systemhook, or
    /// anything under a jailbreak root). Frida/FridaGadget and injectors inside
    /// the app bundle are not: they run in re-signed apps on stock phones.
    /// Before 1.4.0 this returned true for any detail without "frida"/"gadget".
    public static func isTweakInjector(_ detail: String) -> Bool {
        ImageEvidence.classify(detail) == .jailbreakInjector
    }

    /// Code-and-detail form, for raw findings and stored history.
    public static func isConclusive(code: FindingCode, detail: String) -> Bool {
        guard conclusiveCodes.contains(code) else { return false }
        if code == .suspiciousImage { return isTweakInjector(detail) }
        return true
    }

    /// True when this one signal proves the device is jailbroken.
    public static func isConclusive(_ s: Signal) -> Bool {
        if s.informational { return false }
        return isConclusive(code: s.code, detail: s.detail)
    }
}

public final class JailbreakDetector: @unchecked Sendable {
    public var probeURLSchemes: Bool
    private let queue = DispatchQueue(label: "integritykit.jailbreak", qos: .userInitiated)

    /// - Parameter rules: optional verified rules (extra paths, markers, schemes).
    public init(probeURLSchemes: Bool = true, rules: DetectionRules? = nil) {
        self.probeURLSchemes = probeURLSchemes
        rasp_integrity_baseline()
        if let r = rules { RulesRuntime.apply(r) }
    }

    public func check() async -> JailbreakResult {
        await withCheckedContinuation { cont in
            queue.async { cont.resume(returning: self.checkSync()) }
        }
    }

    static var conclusiveCodes: Set<FindingCode> { JailbreakEvidence.conclusiveCodes }
    static var indicatorCodes: Set<FindingCode> { JailbreakEvidence.indicatorCodes }

    public func checkSync() -> JailbreakResult {
        let started = Date()
        let checks: [CoreCheck] = [.filesystem, .sandbox, .images, .hooks, .environment, .codesign, .canary]
        let cfg = IntegrityConfiguration(fridaPorts: [], scanMemory: false, allowForkProbe: true)
        let core = CoreBridge.run(checks: checks, configuration: cfg)

        var raw: [RawSignal] = core.findings.compactMap { f in
            FindingCode(rawValue: f.code).map { RawSignal($0, f.confidence, f.detail) }
        }
        for check in checks where core.completed & check.bit == 0 {
            raw.append(.init(.checkSuppressed, 95, "\(check.name) did not complete"))
        }
        if probeURLSchemes { raw += AppIntegrityChecks.probeJailbreakURLSchemes() }

        var conclusive: [RawSignal] = []
        var indicators: [RawSignal] = []
        for r in raw {
            if JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(code: r.code, detail: r.detail) {
                conclusive.append(r)
            } else if Self.indicatorCodes.contains(r.code) || Self.conclusiveCodes.contains(r.code) {
                // A conclusive-code finding that fails re-validation (for
                // example an image path that no longer classifies as an
                // injector) is kept as an indicator, never dropped silently.
                indicators.append(r)
            }
        }

        if let first = conclusive.first {
            JailbreakMemory.remember(first)
        } else if let past = JailbreakMemory.recall() {
            indicators.append(past)
        }

        #if targetEnvironment(simulator)
        indicators.append(.init(.simulator, 100, "running in the iOS Simulator; result is not meaningful"))
        #endif

        let c: [Signal] = IntegrityKit.makeSignals(conclusive, configuration: cfg)
        let i: [Signal] = IntegrityKit.makeSignals(indicators, configuration: cfg)
        let sys = IntegrityKit.systemInfo()
        let status: JailbreakResult.Status
        if !c.isEmpty { status = .jailbroken }
        else if !i.isEmpty { status = .suspicious }
        else { status = .notJailbroken }
        let elapsedMs: Double = Date().timeIntervalSince(started) * 1000.0
        var checksRun: [String] = checks.map { $0.name }
        if probeURLSchemes { checksRun.append("urlSchemes") }
        let incomplete: [String] = checks.filter { core.completed & $0.bit == 0 }.map { $0.name }
        return JailbreakResult(
            status: status,
            conclusive: c,
            indicators: i,
            model: sys.model,
            osVersion: sys.osVersion,
            checkedAt: started,
            durationMs: elapsedMs,
            checksRun: checksRun,
            checksIncomplete: incomplete,
            sdkVersion: IntegrityKit.sdkVersion)
    }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/JailbreakMemory.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Remembers, in this app's Keychain, that conclusive jailbreak evidence was
/// seen on this device. Keychain items survive app deletion and per-app
/// hiding (for example RootHide's Blacklist), but not an erase or restore.
///
/// A later run that sees no evidence reports `previouslyJailbroken` as an
/// indicator, which catches the common sequence "run once, then hide the app".
/// It is client-side state: a jailbreak user can delete it. The server-side
/// equivalent (DeviceCheck bits or a per-key history) is the robust version;
/// see docs/SERVER.md.
enum JailbreakMemory {
    private static let service = "integritykit.history"
    private static let account = "jailbreak.seen"

    /// Written only for conclusive evidence with high confidence, never for an
    /// indicator or a single weak signal.
    static func remember(_ evidence: RawSignal) {
        guard evidence.confidence >= 90 else { return }
        guard recall() == nil else { return }       // keep the first sighting
        let f = ISO8601DateFormatter()
        let line = "\(f.string(from: Date()))|\(evidence.code.rawValue)|\(evidence.detail.prefix(120))"
        Keychain.write(Data(line.utf8), service: service, account: account)
    }

    static func recall() -> RawSignal? {
        guard let d = Keychain.read(service: service, account: account),
              let line = String(data: d, encoding: .utf8) else { return nil }
        let parts = line.split(separator: "|", maxSplits: 2).map(String.init)
        // Only honour markers whose evidence is still conclusive under the
        // current rule. Markers left by checks that were later demoted (1.3.0's
        // dyld comparison gave a false positive on stock iOS 27) are deleted.
        guard parts.count == 3, let code = UInt16(parts[1]).flatMap({ FindingCode(rawValue: $0) }),
              isStillConclusive(code, detail: parts[2]) else {
            forget()
            return nil
        }
        let title = code.metadata.title
        return RawSignal(.previouslyJailbroken, 80, "seen \(parts[0]): \(title) (\(parts[2]))")
    }

    static func isStillConclusive(_ code: FindingCode, detail: String) -> Bool {
        let s = Signal(code: code, confidence: 100, weight: code.metadata.weight, informational: false, detail: detail)
        return JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(s)
    }

    /// For tests and for a "reset" action in a host app.
    static func forget() {
        Keychain.delete(service: service, account: account)
    }
}

/// Public control over the on-device jailbreak history.
public enum JailbreakHistory {
    /// Deletes the "seen jailbroken" marker, for example after a false positive
    /// or after the device was erased and restored by its owner.
    public static func reset() { JailbreakMemory.forget() }

    /// The stored first sighting, if one is still valid under the current rule.
    public static var firstSighting: String? { JailbreakMemory.recall()?.detail }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/PlatformCapabilities.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// What each iOS version lets an unprivileged, sandboxed app observe.
/// Mirrors the table in README.md. Notes are attached to every report so the
/// server knows which absences of evidence are meaningful.
public struct PlatformCapabilities: Sendable {
    public let osMajor: Int
    public let osMinor: Int

    public static var current: PlatformCapabilities {
        let v = ProcessInfo.processInfo.operatingSystemVersion
        return PlatformCapabilities(osMajor: v.majorVersion, osMinor: v.minorVersion)
    }

    /// Developer Mode exists from iOS 16.
    public var developerModeExists: Bool { osMajor >= 16 }
    /// iOS 17 moved to personalised Developer Disk Images mounted under /System/Developer.
    public var personalisedDDI: Bool { osMajor >= 17 }
    /// Signed System Volume (sealed root snapshot) applies to all supported versions.
    public var sealedSystemVolume: Bool { osMajor >= 15 }

    public var notes: [String] {
        var n: [String] = []
        if !developerModeExists {
            n.append("iOS \(osMajor): Developer Mode does not exist; developer-mode signals are not applicable.")
        } else {
            n.append("Developer Mode has no public API; status comes from an AMFI sysctl when readable, otherwise inferred from get-task-allow.")
        }
        n.append(personalisedDDI
                 ? "Developer Disk Image is personalised and mounted on demand (/System/Developer)."
                 : "Developer Disk Image mounts at /Developer when Xcode has paired.")
        switch osMajor {
        case 15:
            n.append("iOS 15: rootful (checkra1n/palera1n rootful on A8-A11) and rootless (Dopamine, palera1n, XinaA15) jailbreaks exist; TrollStore supported.")
        case 16:
            n.append("iOS 16: rootless jailbreaks (Dopamine up to 16.6.1 on A12+, palera1n on A8-A11); TrollStore up to 16.6.1/16.7 RC.")
        case 17:
            n.append("iOS 17: palera1n on checkm8 devices that receive 17; TrollStore on 17.0 only; no public A12+ kernel jailbreak at release.")
        case 18...25:
            n.append("iOS \(osMajor): no public kernel jailbreak for A12+ known at SDK release; checkm8-class iPads may be jailbreakable. Instrumentation on non-jailbroken devices uses re-signed IPAs with FridaGadget.")
        default:
            n.append("iOS \(osMajor): no public jailbreak known at SDK release. Main threat is repackaging plus FridaGadget, and debugging via Developer Mode. Validate the check set on-device for each new major.")
        }
        n.append("Hiding tweaks that patch the kernel (not libc) defeat raw-syscall filesystem checks; rely on server-side attestation for trust decisions.")
        return n
    }
}

`Sources/KaiselKit/Report.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// One piece of evidence.
public struct Signal: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let code: FindingCode
    public let category: SignalCategory
    public let domain: IntegrityDomain
    /// Evidence quality reported by the check (0...100).
    public let confidence: UInt8
    /// Weight after configuration overrides.
    public let weight: Double
    public let hard: Bool
    /// Shown for context only; never contributes to a score or verdict.
    public let informational: Bool
    public let detail: String

    public var title: String { code.metadata.title }
    /// weight x confidence, the value fed to the scorer (0 if informational).
    public var effectiveWeight: Double {
        if informational { return 0 }
        let c: Double = Double(confidence) / 100.0
        return weight * c
    }

    init(code: FindingCode, confidence: UInt8, weight: Double, informational: Bool, detail: String) {
        let m = code.metadata
        self.code = code
        self.category = m.category
        self.domain = code.domain
        self.confidence = confidence
        self.weight = weight
        self.hard = m.hard
        self.informational = informational
        self.detail = detail
    }
}

public enum Verdict: String, Codable, Comparable, Sendable {
    case trusted, low, elevated, high, compromised

    private var rank: Int {
        switch self {
        case .trusted: return 0
        case .low: return 1
        case .elevated: return 2
        case .high: return 3
        case .compromised: return 4
        }
    }
    public static func < (a: Verdict, b: Verdict) -> Bool { a.rank < b.rank }
}

public struct CategoryScore: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let category: SignalCategory
    public let score: Double
    public let signalCount: Int
}

/// Score and verdict for one domain.
public struct DomainAssessment: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let domain: IntegrityDomain
    public let score: Int
    public let verdict: Verdict
    public let categories: [CategoryScore]
}

/// Facts about the phone, comparable to what an Android root checker shows.
public struct DevicePosture: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public enum TriState: String, Codable, Sendable { case on, off, unknown }
    public let model: String
    public let osVersion: String
    public let osMajor: Int
    public let isSimulator: Bool
    public let isiOSAppOnMac: Bool
    public let developerMode: TriState
    public let developerDiskImageMounted: Bool
    public let passcodeSet: TriState
    public let biometry: String
    public let secureEnclaveAvailable: Bool
    public let appAttestSupported: Bool
}

/// Facts about this app binary.
public struct AppBuildFacts: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    public let bundleIdentifier: String
    public let teamIdentifier: String?
    public let detectedDistribution: String     // appStore / development / adHoc / enterprise / unknown
    public let expectedDistribution: String?    // nil = not enforced
    public let codeSigningFlags: UInt32
    public let cryptID: Int32
    public let textSHA256: String
    public let exceptionPortCount: Int32
}

/// Result of one evaluation. There is no `isJailbroken: Bool`.
///
/// - `device`: is the phone jailbroken or its OS tampered with?
/// - `runtime`: is this process being hooked, injected, instrumented or debugged?
/// - `app`: does the binary match the distribution you expect? `nil` unless
///   `expectedDistribution` is set; app-build signals are then informational.
/// - `verdict`: worst of the enforced domains. Advisory: the server should
///   re-score `signals` from a `SealedReport` it can verify.
public struct IntegrityReport: Codable, Hashable, Sendable {
    /// Report format version checked by the server (1.4.0). Reports from
    /// 1.3.x have no such field and are treated as schema 1 (lab mode only).
    public static let currentSchemaVersion = 2

    public let id: UUID
    public let timestamp: Date
    public let reportSchemaVersion: Int
    public let sdkVersion: String
    public let verdict: Verdict
    public let score: Int
    public let device: DomainAssessment
    public let runtime: DomainAssessment
    public let app: DomainAssessment?
    public let posture: DevicePosture
    public let build: AppBuildFacts
    public let signals: [Signal]
    /// Bitmask of `RASP_CHECK_*` that ran to completion. Self-reported: a
    /// compromised process can lie about it, so the server derives the
    /// expected set itself and treats a missing bit as incomplete coverage.
    public let checksCompleted: UInt32
    public let checksExpected: UInt32
    public let observabilityNotes: [String]
    public let durationMs: Double
    /// Version of the signed remote rules applied, nil if none.
    public let rulesVersion: Int?

    public func signals(in domain: IntegrityDomain) -> [Signal] { signals.filter { $0.domain == domain } }
    public var scoredSignals: [Signal] { signals.filter { !$0.informational } }
    public var informationalSignals: [Signal] { signals.filter(\.informational) }

    /// Canonical JSON (sorted keys, ISO-8601 dates) used for hashing/signing.
    public func canonicalJSON() throws -> Data {
        let enc = JSONEncoder()
        enc.outputFormatting = [.sortedKeys, .withoutEscapingSlashes]
        enc.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        return try enc.encode(self)
    }
}

public extension IntegrityReport {
    /// Signals that prove a jailbreak under the same rule Kaisel JB Check uses.
    /// Non-empty means the device verdict is `.compromised`.
    var jailbreakEvidence: [Signal] { signals.filter(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive) }
}

```

`Sources/KaiselKit/Resources/PrivacyInfo.xcprivacy`:

`Sources/KaiselKit/RiskScorer.swift`:

```swift
import Foundation

/// Combines signals into a 0...100 score.
///
/// Within a category, signals are sorted by effective weight and damped
/// geometrically (w1, w2*0.5, w3*0.25, ...) before a noisy-OR, because 40 hits
/// on jailbreak paths are one piece of evidence, not forty. Across categories a
/// plain noisy-OR is used: independent evidence corroborates.
///
///   p_cat = 1 - prod_i (1 - w_i * d^i)
///   p     = 1 - prod_cat (1 - p_cat)
///
/// Hard indicators (evidence with no benign explanation on a production
/// device) floor the verdict at `.high`; two or more hard indicators in
/// different categories floor it at `.compromised`.
public struct RiskScorer: Sendable {
    public var damping: Double = 0.5
    public var thresholds: (low: Int, elevated: Int, high: Int, compromised: Int) = (15, 35, 60, 85)
    public var hardConfidenceFloor: UInt8 = 75

    public init() {}

    /// Scores one domain using only its non-informational signals.
    public func assess(_ domain: IntegrityDomain, _ signals: [Signal]) -> DomainAssessment {
        let scored = signals.filter { $0.domain == domain && !$0.informational }
        let cats = categoryScores(scored)
        let s = score(cats)
        return DomainAssessment(domain: domain, score: s, verdict: verdict(score: s, signals: scored), categories: cats)
    }

    public func categoryScores(_ signals: [Signal]) -> [CategoryScore] {
        let scored: [Signal] = signals.filter { !$0.informational }
        let grouped: [SignalCategory: [Signal]] = Dictionary(grouping: scored, by: { $0.category })
        var result: [CategoryScore] = []
        for (cat, sigs) in grouped {
            let ws: [Double] = sigs.map { $0.effectiveWeight }.sorted(by: >)
            var survive: Double = 1.0
            var factor: Double = 1.0
            for w in ws {
                let damped: Double = Swift.min(Swift.max(w * factor, 0.0), 1.0)
                survive *= (1.0 - damped)
                factor *= damping
            }
            result.append(CategoryScore(category: cat, score: 1.0 - survive, signalCount: sigs.count))
        }
        result.sort { $0.score > $1.score }
        return result
    }

    public func score(_ categories: [CategoryScore]) -> Int {
        var survive: Double = 1.0
        for c in categories { survive *= (1.0 - c.score) }
        let pct: Double = (1.0 - survive) * 100.0
        return Int(pct.rounded())
    }

    public func verdict(score: Int, signals: [Signal]) -> Verdict {
        var v: Verdict
        switch score {
        case ..<thresholds.low: v = .trusted
        case ..<thresholds.elevated: v = .low
        case ..<thresholds.high: v = .elevated
        case ..<thresholds.compromised: v = .high
        default: v = .compromised
        }
        let floor: UInt8 = hardConfidenceFloor
        let hard: [Signal] = signals.filter { (s: Signal) -> Bool in
            s.hard && !s.informational && s.confidence >= floor && s.weight > 0
        }
        if !hard.isEmpty && v < .high { v = .high }
        let hardCategories: Set<SignalCategory> = Set(hard.map { $0.category })
        if hardCategories.count >= 2 { v = .compromised }
        return v
    }
}

```

`Tests/CClassifier/classify_main.c`:

```c
// Host-side driver for the C image classifier (rasp_classify.c).
// Reads tab-separated lines: simulator(0/1) TAB bundle(or -) TAB markers(comma list or -) TAB path
// and prints one class name per line. Driven by run_tests.py; builds on Linux and macOS.
#include "rasp_internal.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>

// Stand-in for rasp_rules.c (which needs os_unfair_lock): same matching rule.
static char g_markers[16][RASP_STR_MAX];
static unsigned g_marker_count;

bool rasp_rules_marker_match(const char *path) {
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < g_marker_count; i++)
        if (rasp_strcasestr(path, g_markers[i])) return true;
    return false;
}

static const char *name(rasp_image_class_t c) {
    switch (c) {
    case RASP_IMG_TRUSTED: return "trusted";
    case RASP_IMG_JAILBREAK_INJECTOR: return "jailbreakInjector";
    case RASP_IMG_INSTRUMENTATION: return "instrumentation";
    case RASP_IMG_MARKER: return "marker";
    case RASP_IMG_UNKNOWN_EXTERNAL: return "unknownExternal";
    default: return "unknown";
    }
}

int main(void) {
    char line[4096];
    while (fgets(line, sizeof line, stdin)) {
        line[strcspn(line, "\n")] = 0;
        char *f[4] = {0};
        char *save = NULL, *tok = strtok_r(line, "\t", &save);
        for (int i = 0; i < 4 && tok; i++, tok = strtok_r(NULL, "\t", &save)) f[i] = tok;
        if (!f[2]) { puts("bad-input"); continue; }
        g_marker_count = 0;
        if (strcmp(f[2], "-") != 0) {
            char *s2 = NULL;
            for (char *m = strtok_r(f[2], ",", &s2); m && g_marker_count < 16; m = strtok_r(NULL, ",", &s2))
                snprintf(g_markers[g_marker_count++], RASP_STR_MAX, "%s", m);
        }
        const char *bundle = strcmp(f[1], "-") == 0 ? NULL : f[1];
        const char *path = f[3] ? f[3] : "";
        rasp_image_class_t c = rasp_image_class_ex(path, bundle, f[0][0] == '1');
        printf("%s\t%u\n", name(c), rasp_image_class_code(c));
    }
    return 0;
}

```

`Tests/CClassifier/run_tests.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Builds the C image classifier for the host and checks it against
Tests/Fixtures/image_classification.json and against Server/classification.py.

  python3 Tests/CClassifier/run_tests.py          (needs cc/clang/gcc)
"""
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
ROOT = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(HERE, "..", ".."))
CORE = os.path.join(ROOT, "Sources", "CIntegrityCore")
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(ROOT, "Server"))
import classification  # noqa: E402


def build(out_dir: str) -> str:
    cc = os.environ.get("CC") or shutil.which("clang") or shutil.which("cc") or shutil.which("gcc")
    if not cc:
        raise SystemExit("NOT TESTED: no C compiler found")
    exe = os.path.join(out_dir, "classify")
    cmd = [cc, "-std=c11", "-D_DEFAULT_SOURCE", "-D_GNU_SOURCE", "-Wall", "-Wextra", "-Werror", "-Wno-unknown-pragmas",
           "-I", os.path.join(CORE, "include"), "-I", CORE,
           os.path.join(HERE, "classify_main.c"), os.path.join(CORE, "rasp_classify.c"),
           os.path.join(CORE, "rasp_util.c"), "-o", exe]
    subprocess.run(cmd, check=True)
    return exe


def main() -> int:
    cases = json.load(open(os.path.join(ROOT, "Tests", "Fixtures", "image_classification.json")))["cases"]
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
        exe = build(d)
        lines = "".join(f"{1 if c['simulator'] else 0}\t{c['bundle'] or '-'}\t{','.join(c['remoteMarkers']) or '-'}\t{c['path']}\n"
                        for c in cases)
        out = subprocess.run([exe], input=lines, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout.splitlines()
    fails = 0
    for c, row in zip(cases, out):
        got_c, code = row.split("\t")
        got_py = classification.classify_image(c["path"], c["bundle"], c["simulator"], c["remoteMarkers"])
        ok = got_c == c["expected"] == got_py and int(code) == classification.CLASS_CODE.get(got_c, 0)
        fails += not ok
        print(f"{'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}  C={got_c:<18} py={got_py:<18} want={c['expected']:<18} {c['name']}")
    if len(out) != len(cases):
        print(f"FAIL  driver printed {len(out)} rows for {len(cases)} cases")
        fails += 1
    print(f"{len(cases) - fails}/{len(cases)} classification cases agree (C core, server, fixture)")
    return 1 if fails else 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

```

`Tests/KaiselKitTests/HardeningTests.swift`:

```swift
import XCTest
import CryptoKit
@testable import KaiselKit

final class HardeningTests: XCTestCase {
    func testRulesWeightsAreBounded() {
        // anonymousExecutableMemory built-in 0.35 -> allowed 0.175...0.525
        XCTAssertEqual(RulesRuntime.boundedWeight(.anonymousExecutableMemory, 0.0), 0.175, accuracy: 1e-9)
        XCTAssertEqual(RulesRuntime.boundedWeight(.anonymousExecutableMemory, 1.0), 0.525, accuracy: 1e-9)
        XCTAssertEqual(RulesRuntime.boundedWeight(.rootlessJailbreakRoot, 5.0), 1.0, accuracy: 1e-9)
        XCTAssertEqual(RulesRuntime.boundedThreshold(0, 60), 45)
        XCTAssertEqual(RulesRuntime.boundedThreshold(99, 60), 75)
    }

    func testSignedRulesVerifyAndRejectTampering() throws {
        let key = P256.Signing.PrivateKey()
        let dir = FileManager.default.temporaryDirectory.appendingPathComponent(UUID().uuidString)
        let store = try RulesStore(publicKeyPEM: key.publicKey.pemRepresentation, directory: dir)
        let json = """
        {"version": 1, "issuedAt": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z", "expiresAt": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z",
         "extraJailbreakPaths": ["/var/jb/x"], "extraImageMarkers": [], "extraURLSchemes": [],
         "extraFridaPorts": [], "weightOverrides": {}}
        """
        let data = Data(json.utf8)
        let sig = try key.signature(for: data).derRepresentation.base64EncodedString()
        let rules = try store.verify(rulesData: data, signatureBase64: sig)
        XCTAssertEqual(rules.extraJailbreakPaths, ["/var/jb/x"])
        var tampered = data
        tampered[tampered.startIndex + 13] = UInt8(ascii: "9")
        XCTAssertThrowsError(try store.verify(rulesData: tampered, signatureBase64: sig))
    }

    func testProtectedVerdictRoundTrip() {
        let box = ProtectedVerdict()
        XCTAssertNil(box.read())
    }
}

```

`Tests/KaiselKitTests/ImageClassificationTests.swift`:

```swift
import XCTest
@testable import KaiselKit

/// Loaded-image classification (1.4.0). Runs the shared fixtures in
/// Tests/Fixtures/image_classification.json through the C classifier via the
/// Swift wrapper. The same file is checked against the server
/// (Server/tests/test_classification.py) and the C core on the host
/// (Tests/CClassifier/run_tests.py).
final class ImageClassificationTests: XCTestCase {
    private struct Fixture: Decodable {
        struct Case: Decodable {
            let name: String
            let path: String
            let bundle: String?
            let expected: String
            let simulator: Bool
            let remoteMarkers: [String]
        }
        let cases: [Case]
    }

    private func loadFixtures() throws -> [Fixture.Case] {
        let url = URL(fileURLWithPath: #filePath)
            .deletingLastPathComponent().deletingLastPathComponent()
            .appendingPathComponent("Fixtures/image_classification.json")
        let data = try Data(contentsOf: url)
        return try JSONDecoder().decode(Fixture.self, from: data).cases
    }

    func testSharedFixtures() throws {
        let cases = try loadFixtures()
        XCTAssertGreaterThanOrEqual(cases.count, 30)
        defer { RulesRuntime.apply(nil) }
        for c in cases {
            if c.remoteMarkers.isEmpty {
                RulesRuntime.apply(nil)
            } else {
                RulesRuntime.apply(Self.rules(markers: c.remoteMarkers))
            }
            let got = ImageEvidence.classify(c.path, bundlePath: c.bundle, simulator: c.simulator)
            XCTAssertEqual(got.rawValue, c.expected, c.name)
        }
    }

    // Spec acceptance cases, stated directly so they survive fixture edits.

    func testBenignFrameworkNamesAreNotConclusive() {
        let app = "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/CraneBank.app"
        for path in [app + "/Frameworks/CraneBank.framework/CraneBank",
                     app + "/Frameworks/LibertyFinance.framework/LibertyFinance",
                     app + "/Frameworks/ChoicyAnalytics.framework/ChoicyAnalytics"] {
            XCTAssertEqual(ImageEvidence.classify(path, bundlePath: app, simulator: false), .trusted, path)
            let s = Signal(code: .suspiciousImage, confidence: 95, weight: 0.9, informational: false, detail: path)
            XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(s), path)
        }
    }

    func testRootHideInjectorIsConclusive() {
        let path = "/private/var/containers/Bundle/Application/.jbroot-7351422000C774F3/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib"
        XCTAssertEqual(ImageEvidence.classify(path, bundlePath: "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/App.app",
                                              simulator: false), .jailbreakInjector)
        XCTAssertTrue(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(code: .suspiciousImage, detail: path))
    }

    func testFridaGadgetIsInstrumentationNotJailbreak() {
        let app = "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/App.app"
        XCTAssertEqual(ImageEvidence.classify(app + "/Frameworks/FridaGadget.dylib", bundlePath: app, simulator: false),
                       .instrumentation)
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(code: .instrumentationImage, detail: app + "/Frameworks/FridaGadget.dylib"))
        XCTAssertEqual(FindingCode.instrumentationImage.domain, .runtime)
        XCTAssertTrue(FindingCode.instrumentationImage.metadata.hard)
    }

    func testBundleBoundary() {
        XCTAssertEqual(ImageEvidence.classify("/var/App.app2/Frameworks/X.framework/X", bundlePath: "/var/App.app",
                                              simulator: false), .unknownExternal)
    }

    func testRemoteMarkerNeverConclusive() {
        RulesRuntime.apply(Self.rules(markers: ["evilhook"]))
        defer { RulesRuntime.apply(nil) }
        let path = "/var/mobile/Library/evilhook.dylib"
        XCTAssertEqual(ImageEvidence.classify(path, bundlePath: "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/App.app",
                                              simulator: false), .marker)
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(code: .imageMarkerMatch, detail: path))
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(code: .suspiciousImage, detail: path))
    }

    func testOldStyleRuntimeImageIsNoLongerConclusive() {
        // Before 1.4.0, imageAddedAtRuntime at confidence 95 without "frida" was conclusive.
        let s = Signal(code: .imageAddedAtRuntime, confidence: 95, weight: 0.6, informational: false,
                       detail: "/var/mobile/Library/CraneSDK.dylib")
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(s))
    }

    func testStaleHistoryMarkerIsNotHonoured() {
        // A 1.3.x Keychain marker for a broad-marker match must not survive re-validation.
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakMemory.isStillConclusive(
            .suspiciousImage, detail: "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/U/CraneBank.app/Frameworks/CraneBank.framework/CraneBank"))
        XCTAssertTrue(JailbreakMemory.isStillConclusive(.suspiciousImage, detail: ".jbroot-0/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib"))
    }

    func testConclusiveSetMatchesSharedManifestSize() {
        // Full name-level parity is checked by Server/tests/test_parity.py.
        XCTAssertEqual(JailbreakEvidence.conclusiveCodes.count, 12)
        XCTAssertTrue(JailbreakEvidence.conclusiveCodes.isDisjoint(with: JailbreakEvidence.indicatorCodes))
    }

    static func rules(markers: [String]) -> DetectionRules {
        DetectionRules(version: 1, issuedAt: Date(), expiresAt: Date().addingTimeInterval(3600), minSdkVersion: nil,
                       extraJailbreakPaths: [], extraImageMarkers: markers, extraURLSchemes: [], extraFridaPorts: [],
                       weightOverrides: [:], thresholds: nil)
    }
}

```

`Tests/KaiselKitTests/JailbreakEvidenceTests.swift`:

```swift
import XCTest
@testable import KaiselKit

/// Lab case: iPhone9,3, iOS 15.8.2, Dopamine with RootHide, app not hidden.
/// JB Check said "Jailbroken"; the full app must not report the device as merely elevated.
final class JailbreakEvidenceTests: XCTestCase {
    private func sig(_ c: FindingCode, _ conf: UInt8 = 95, _ detail: String = "") -> Signal {
        Signal(code: c, confidence: conf, weight: c.metadata.weight, informational: false, detail: detail)
    }

    func testTweakInjectorIsConclusiveFridaIsNot() {
        XCTAssertTrue(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(
            sig(.suspiciousImage, 95, "/var/containers/Bundle/Application/.jbroot-0123/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib")))
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(sig(.suspiciousImage, 95, "/private/var/x/FridaGadget.dylib")))
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(sig(.syscallStubAltered, 60, "csops")))
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(sig(.jailbreakURLScheme, 80, "sileo://")))
        // Demoted in 1.3.1 after a stock iOS 27 false positive.
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(sig(.dyldModified, 95, "dyld __text differs")))
    }

    func testInformationalNeverConclusive() {
        let s = Signal(code: .rootlessJailbreakRoot, confidence: 100, weight: 0.95, informational: true, detail: "")
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive(s))
    }

    func testRootHideUnhiddenRunDeviceDomainAloneIsNotCompromised() {
        // Device-domain signals from that run score only "elevated"; the
        // conclusive injector lives in the runtime domain. IntegrityKit
        // escalates the device verdict using JailbreakEvidence.
        let signals = [sig(.jailbreakURLScheme, 80, "sileo://"),
                       sig(.suspiciousImage, 95, ".jbroot-x/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib")]
        let device = RiskScorer().assess(.device, signals)
        XCTAssertLessThan(device.verdict, .compromised)
        XCTAssertTrue(signals.contains(where: JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive))
    }

    /// Regression fixture: stock iPhone18,1, iOS 27.2.1, Kaisel 1.3.0 said "Jailbroken"
    /// because dyld runs from the shared cache and differed from /usr/lib/dyld.
    func testStockIOS27DyldMismatchIsNotAJailbreak() {
        let signals = [sig(.dyldModified, 95, "dyld __text differs from disk: 162671 words, first at +0x0")]
        XCTAssertFalse(signals.contains(where: JailbreakEvidence.isConclusive))
        XCTAssertFalse(FindingCode.dyldModified.metadata.hard)
        XCTAssertLessThan(RiskScorer().assess(.device, signals).verdict, .high)
        XCTAssertFalse(JailbreakMemory.isStillConclusive(.dyldModified, detail: "dyld"))
        XCTAssertTrue(JailbreakMemory.isStillConclusive(.suspiciousImage, detail: ".jbroot-0/usr/lib/roothideinit.dylib"))
    }

    func testNewCodesMapToDevice() {
        XCTAssertEqual(FindingCode.dyldModified.domain, .device)
        XCTAssertEqual(FindingCode.userspaceReboot.domain, .device)
        XCTAssertEqual(FindingCode.previouslyJailbroken.domain, .device)
        XCTAssertFalse(FindingCode.userspaceReboot.metadata.hard)
    }
}

`Tests/KaiselKitTests/RiskScorerTests.swift`:

```swift
import XCTest
@testable import KaiselKit

final class RiskScorerTests: XCTestCase {
    private func sig(_ c: FindingCode, _ conf: UInt8 = 100, _ detail: String = "") -> Signal {
        Signal(code: c, confidence: conf, weight: c.metadata.weight, informational: false, detail: detail)
    }

    func testCleanIsTrusted() {
        let s = RiskScorer()
        XCTAssertEqual(s.score(s.categoryScores([])), 0)
        XCTAssertEqual(s.verdict(score: 0, signals: []), .trusted)
    }

    func testCorrelatedPathsAreDamped() {
        let s = RiskScorer()
        let many = (0..<40).map { sig(.jailbreakPathPresent, 95, "/p\($0)") }
        let score = s.score(s.categoryScores(many))
        XCTAssertLessThan(score, 85)
        XCTAssertGreaterThan(score, 50)
    }

    func testIndependentCategoriesCorroborate() {
        let s = RiskScorer()
        let one = s.score(s.categoryScores([sig(.jailbreakPathPresent)]))
        let two = s.score(s.categoryScores([sig(.jailbreakPathPresent), sig(.unexpectedImagePath)]))
        XCTAssertGreaterThan(two, one)
    }

    func testHardIndicatorFloorsVerdict() {
        XCTAssertGreaterThanOrEqual(RiskScorer().verdict(score: 10, signals: [sig(.fridaThread, 95)]), .high)
    }

    func testTwoHardCategoriesAreCompromised() {
        let signals = [sig(.fridaThread, 95), sig(.sandboxWrite, 98)]
        XCTAssertEqual(RiskScorer().verdict(score: 10, signals: signals), .compromised)
    }

    func testLowConfidenceHardDoesNotFloor() {
        XCTAssertEqual(RiskScorer().verdict(score: 5, signals: [sig(.fridaThread, 40)]), .trusted)
    }

    /// Regression: stock iPhone18,1 on iOS 27.2.1, Xcode-installed build.
    /// Build-context and Developer Mode signals must not make the device look compromised.
    func testStockXcodeBuildIsTrusted() {
        let raw: [RawSignal] = [
            .init(.binaryDecrypted, 90, "cryptid=0"),
            .init(.getTaskAllow, 90, "get-task-allow"),
            .init(.provisioningProfilePresent, 90, "signed as development"),
            .init(.developerModeEnabled, 95, "amfi developer_mode_status=1"),
            .init(.anonymousExecutableMemory, 55, "anonymous r-x region"),
        ]
        let cfg = IntegrityConfiguration()   // expectedDistribution nil
        let signals = IntegrityKit.requireCorroboration(IntegrityKit.makeSignals(raw, configuration: cfg))
        XCTAssertTrue(signals.allSatisfy(\.informational))
        let s = RiskScorer()
        XCTAssertEqual(s.assess(.device, signals).verdict, .trusted)
        XCTAssertEqual(s.assess(.runtime, signals).verdict, .trusted)
    }

    func testAppStoreExpectationScoresBuildSignals() {
        let cfg = IntegrityConfiguration(expectedDistribution: .appStore)
        let signals = IntegrityKit.makeSignals([.init(.binaryDecrypted, 90, "cryptid=0")], configuration: cfg)
        XCTAssertFalse(signals[0].informational)
        XCTAssertEqual(signals[0].domain, .app)
    }

    func testDeveloperModeIsNeverScored() {
        let cfg = IntegrityConfiguration(expectedDistribution: .appStore)
        XCTAssertTrue(cfg.treatment(for: .developerModeEnabled).informational)
    }

    func testFindingCodesMirrorCHeader() {
        XCTAssertEqual(FindingCode.libcSyscallMismatch.rawValue, 108)
        XCTAssertEqual(FindingCode.fridaDBus.rawValue, 502)
        XCTAssertEqual(FindingCode.suspiciousEntitlement.rawValue, 602)
    }
}
